[\[1b\]]
[\[1b.1\]{D4123}]@##/_/rgya gar skad du/_bi na ya kA ri kA/_bod skad du/_'dul ba tshig le'ur byas pa/_bam po dang po/_thams cad mkhyen pa la phyag 'tshal lo/_/'phags pa nyan thos 
[\[1b.2\]]dang rang sangs rgyas thams cad la phyag 'tshal lo/_/gang gis nyid kyi dbu yang rin chen 'od 'phro lta bu dang /_/rab rgyas pad+ma gser mdog dang 'dra'i btsun mo rnams kyang byin/_/ma lus 
[\[1b.3\]]don gnyer rab dgar mdzad nas byang chub mchog brnyes pa/_/chos 'dul zhi ba'i don du ston mdzad de la phyag 'tshal lo/_/gang thob ngan 'gro'i 'jigs pa rnams las nges par sgrol 'gyur 
[\[1b.4\]]zhing /_/srid pa'i 'ching ba mtha' dag bcad nas mya ngan 'das gyur pa/_/'khor ba'i rgya mtsho gting mtha' med pa kun las sgrol ba yi/_/gzings gyur gcig pu bde gshegs kyis 
[\[1b.5\]]bstan chos de la yang 'dud/_/gang yang yon tan kun ldan yon gnas dam par gang gyur pas/_/cung zad phul yang 'bras bu rgya chen shin tu 'phel 'gyur ba/_/bdag nyid kun gyis phyag 
[\[2a\]]
[\[2a.1\]]'os bde bar gshegs pas rab tu bsngags/_/gang zag brgyad mchog legs spyod kyis brgyan 'phags tshogs rnams la'ang 'dud/_/gang gis 'dul ba bstan pa dang /_/gang gi gang zhig 
[\[2a.2\]]'dul ba thob/_/ma dul spangs nas 'dul dbang gyur/_/'dul ba la ni yang dag gnas/_/chos dang 'phags pa'i tshogs bcas kyi/_/ston pa de la phyag 'tshal nas/_/gdul bya rnams ni gdul ba'i phyir/_/
[\[2a.3\]]'dul ba las brtsams cung zad brjod/_/le lo can dang blo dman dag_/gzhung [{]mangs [,]mang [}]rnams kyis 'jigs pa ni/_/legs gsungs 'dul ba'i rgya mtsho la/_/yon tan ldan yang 'dod mi byed/_/de phyir de dag gzhug don 
[\[2a.4\]]du/_/skyo med 'gro ba'i che ba yi/_/tshig ler byas pa'i them skas kyis/_/rtag tu mu stegs ston byed bzhin/_/rab bsngags nor gyis phun tshogs shing /_/lha yi khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dam par skye/_/ting nge 'dzin dang nges 
[\[2a.5\]]'byung 'thob/_/tshul khrims 'di ni 'bras bu che/_/de la 'dir ni rnam kun du/_/'dul ba min pas dag pa med/_/ji ltar chu med 'dag chal gyis/_/mnyes kyang dri ma mi 'dag bzhin/_/ji ltar zla ba med pa yi/_/
[\[2b\]]
[\[2b.1\]]mtshan mo dag ni mdzes ma yin/_/de bzhin tshul khrims mi gnas pa'i/_/rab byung rgyal ba'i bstan mi mdzes/_/de phyir snyom las spangs nas ni/_/bya dang bya min shes nus phyir/_/tshul khrims kyis ni brgyan 'dod pas/_/'dul la 'bad pas gnas par bya/_/gang phyir 'dul 
[\[2b.2\]]ba shes gyur cing /_/gus par byed pa'i dge slong ni/_/rang nyid yid ches gnas gyur pas/_/gdams ngag rjes bstan ston pa yin/_/de yang 'khor rnam bzhi po yis/_/shin tu gus bcas mdun bdar zhing /_/'das dang ma byon da ltar gyi/_/sangs rgyas rnams kyi gces mdzod 
[\[2b.3\]]'dzin/_/skyes chen rnams la phan phyir yang /_/dam pa'i chos la legs gnas brtson/_/'di yi rang gi tshul khrims kyi/_/phung po legs bsrungs nyams pa med/_/gzhan gang 'gyod pa dang bcas pa/_/thams cad de yi rjes su 'brang /_/don rnams rnam par nges 
[\[2b.4\]]pa yang /_/phul phyin mkhas pa thob pa yin/_/chos rig chos dang bcas pa yis/_/pha rol rgol ba pham par byed/_/gtsug lag khang du'ang gzhan mi 'gran/_/dge 'dun dbus na 'jigs pa med/_/de phyir phyogs kyi sgron ma ste/_/'od gsal rab tu snang bar byed/_/
[\[2b.5\]]gang du 'dul ba mkhas gnas pa/_/de ru nga ni snying las chung /_/de ltar dpag med rnam pa yis/_/thub pas 'dul ba'i phan yon gsungs/_/de bas gus pa dang bcas pas/_/chos 'dul gzung phyir brtson par bya/_/lo bcu yang dag rdzogs pa yi/_/dge slong kun sdom bdag 
[\[2b.6\]]nyid can/_/gsal zhing kun spyod rig pa yis/_/rab byung dang ni bsnyen rdzogs bya/_/so sor thar dang rnam 'byed don/_/mkhas shing dge bas sbyin bya ste/_/gzhan la rjes su ston pa yi/_/mthu ldan zhu gnas bca' ba'i 'os/_/ston pa'i gsung ni ma 'khrugs pa'i/_/byis pas 
[\[2b.7\]]mdo tsam bklags pa yis/_/gsal bar shes pa ma yin pas/_/rnam par phye ste rtogs par bya/_/so sor thar dang rnam 'byed ni/_/gang 'dir shin tu mi mkhas lo/_/drug cu lon yang byis pa ste/_/de ni gzhan la brten pas gnas/_/slob dpon de las rgan yang ni/_/gzhon 
[\[3a\]]
[\[3a.1\]]nus ji bzhin nges brten te/_/gsar bu yin phyir phyag mi bya/_/gzhan ni thams cad byis pas bzhin/_/pham pa byung dang bu lon chags/_/nad pa rab tu dbyung mi bya/_/bran dang rgyal pos bkrabs par grags/_/chom rkun rgyal mtshan 'chang ba min/_/smad 
[\[3a.2\]]pa'i rigs dang yan lag med/_/sgul gyol lag sor sbrul 'dra dang /_/smad 'khyal lag rdum 'theng po dang /_/sna leb dang ni sna [{]rings [,]ring [}]min/_/gzhon ches pa dang rgan ches dang /_/ha cang bong ring ha cang thung /_/yan lag mi min rnam pa can/_/ha cang mgul [{]rings [,]ring [}]mjing nub min/_/
[\[3a.3\]]bud med lam dang khur gyis dub/_/mgo ring mgo thung mgo chung dang /_/so ni shin tu phyir gyed dang /_/mig yon dang ni lta stangs 'jigs/_/dmar dang ser dang sngo ches dang /_/chu bur rnam pa lta bu'i mig_/rab tu byung ba bkag pa ste/_/sprul pa la sogs de bzhin no/_/spang 
[\[3a.4\]]bar bya ba mdor bsdu na/_/mdog dang dbyibs la skyon chags dang /_/ma rungs rigs te rnam pa gsum/_/'khor gyur kun nas smod pa'o/_/mdog skyon dmar skya la sogs te/_/dbyibs skyon bzhin ngan la sogs yin/_/bong bu la sogs rna dang mgo/_/skra dang rna ba med nyid spang /_/
[\[3a.5\]]glang po rta dang spri sna 'dra/_/sna med pa dang mig gcig dang /_/mig med ba lang la sogs kyi/_/so 'dra gang yang so med pa'o/_/rigs ni gshed ma smyig ma mkhan/_/g.yung po la sogs rnam par spang /_/shing rta mkhan la sogs pa dang /_/bstan bcos las ni gang bkag pa/_/de 
[\[3a.6\]]bzhin gsal shing mkhan sogs dang /_/chang 'tshong la sogs gang yin pa/_/rang bzhin dman gyur de dag la/_/rnam pa kun du bslab mi sbyin/_/de dag dge rgyun med min zhing /_/thar pa'ang mi 'dod ma yin mod/_/gcig tu bstan la skye bo yis/_/thos na skyon du lta phyir bkag_/bsam 
[\[3a.7\]]dang rjes kyi bsam shes shing /_/mngon shes ting 'dzin thob pa ni/_/mthu yis 'jig rten tshim byed phyir/_/rab tu 'byin na nyes pa med/_/ji skad bstan skyon rnams bral zhing /_/dge ba don du gnyer rnams la/_/bar chad mtha' dag dris nas ni/_/mgo nas rkang pa'i bar du brtag_/zhag 
[\[3b\]]
[\[3b.1\]]brgyad la sogs bzung nas kyang /_/ji srid bar du legs par brtag_/gang zhig sngon du yongs brtags nas/_/nyi ma gcig kyang gzhag mi bya/_/thog mar skyabs gsum byin nas ni/_/bslab gzhi lnga po dag kyang ngo /_/dkar po'i gos ni 'dzin bzhin la/_/tshogs kyi nang du mkhyen 
[\[3b.2\]]par gsol/_/dge 'dun rtse gcig rjes gnang tshe/_/rab byung cho ga sngon 'gro bas/_/slob dpon rnam par mkhas pa yis/_/yan lag bcu yi sdom 'dzin gzhug_/de ltar dge tshul byas ma thag_/'di ni khyim pa thams cad kyis/_/phyag byed smon par 'gyur ba ste/_/gang phyir khyim gyi 
[\[3b.3\]]'ching grol phyir/_/tshul ngan rab rib mun bsal te/_/ngur smrig gos ni 'dzin pa yis/_/nyi ma shar ltar lhang nger ni/_/phul phyin de ni shin tu mdzes/_/sum cu rtsa gsum yongs 'du'i shing /_/sa brtol lo 'dab rnying pa bzhin/_/sdig pa kun las ldog don du/_/'di yi bsnyen rdzogs 
[\[3b.4\]]de bzhin no/_/yul dbus su ni dge slong rnams/_/bcu las nyung du mi rung ste/_/mtha' 'khob tu ni bsnyen rdzogs phyir/_/lnga la sogs pas 'dod pa yin/_/li kha ra na tshang tshing nags/_/li khar shing 'phel shar phyogs nyid/_/u shi ra zhes brjod pa yi/_/ri bo mun pa can ni byang /_/
[\[3b.5\]]ka ba nye ba'i ka ba yi/_/grong khyer nub na gnas pa yin/_/grong khyer 'dam bu can gyi grong /_/chu klung 'dam bu can ni lho/_/'di dag gi ni bar rnams la/_/yul dbus su ni shes bya ste/_/phyi rol mtha' 'khob ces byar ni/_/mtha' yas mkhyen pas gsungs pa yin/_/tshul khrims phun sum 
[\[3b.6\]]tshogs pa yi/_/dge slong gis bsnyen rdzogs gsungs kyi/_/dung gi sgra dang khong rul dang /_/'bras kyi nang gi srin bus min/_/gal te bzhi ni las brjod mkhas/_/tshul khrims ldan par gyur na ni/_/bsnyen rdzogs ma smad gyur pa ste/_/lha yis kyang ni phyag byar 'os/_/dge 'dun tshang dang 
[\[3b.7\]]mtshams phyir min/_/las mkhan tshul khrims ldan pa dang /_/las dag pa dang bar chad med/_/bsnyen par rdzogs pa'i rgyu yin no/_/gang phyir bsnyen par rdzogs pa ni/_/gzhan yang bye brag smra bas brjod/_/ji ltar gnas pa mkhyen pas na/_/'dren pa rang rgyal rang byung gi'o/_/
[\[4a\]]
[\[4a.1\]]'phags pa kauN+Di n+ya sogs/_/lnga sde rnams ni nges pa las/_/gang zhig slob ma bzang sde rnams/_/thams cad skyabs su 'gro bas thob/_/pho nya yis ni mchod sbyin ma/_/tshur shog dge slong zhes byas kyang /_/ston par khas blangs pa las ni/_/dge slong 
[\[4a.2\]]'od srungs bdag nyid che/_/dri bas mnyes pa nyid kyis ni/_/slob ma legs sbyin de bzhin no/_/dbus dang mtha' 'khob pa dag la/_/bsnyen rdzogs yang dag bstan pa ni/_/bcu dang lnga yi tshogs kyis sam/_/de bas lhag pas kyang ni rung /_/de bzhin 'khor tshogs bcas pa yi/_/gau 
[\[4a.3\]]ta ma lci chos kyis/_/de las gzhan ni ci rigs par/_/las ni sngon 'gro bya ba ste/_/bsnyen par rdzogs la sngar gyi ni/_/nyes par grags pa med pa yin/_/dgun dus dang ni so ga nyid/_/dbyar dang thung ngu de bzhin du/_/ring po'i ming can du yang shes/_/'di dag dus ni rnam 
[\[4a.4\]]pa lnga/_/lhung bzed snam sbyar grib tshod dang /_/de bzhin dbyar ni tshul 'jug dag_/rtag tu bdag gi dran byas te/_/gzhan du de la nyes byas 'gyur/_/dge slong rnams las 'di bsnyen rdzogs/_/de la zla phyed gdams ngag gsol/_/dge slong bcas gnas dbyar gnas nas/_/tshogs 
[\[4a.5\]]gnyis dang ni dgag dbye bya/_/tshul 'chal dge slong min par ni/_/mi gleng khro ba nyid kyang ngo /_/lci ba'i chos las 'das pa ni/_/zla phyed tshogs gnyis mgu byas te/_/bsnyen par rdzogs pa gsar bu las/_/dge slong ma yi phyag bya'o/_/lci ba'i chos brgyad 'di dag ni/_/dge slong ma la 
[\[4a.6\]]gsungs pa yin/_/bud med skyes pa'i rnam pa dang /_/skyes pa bud med rnam pa dang /_/mkhan po gtan med nyid dang ni/_/mkhan po khyim pa'i rtags sogs nyid/_/gang yang skal ldan rkun po dang /_/yan lag bcad sogs khong chud dang /_/de yang bsnyen par rdzogs 'gyur mod/_/dge 
[\[4a.7\]]slong 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/mi 'dod bsnyen par rdzogs mi bya/_/gang gi lo ni mi shes na/_/de yi mtshan ni brtag bya ste/_/des ni na tshod mngon gsal 'gyur/_/gang 'di nyi shu ma rdzogs pa'i/_/lo yis dge slong byas na ni/_/zla shol mngal gyi zla rnams kyis/_/mkhas pa 
[\[4b\]]
[\[4b.1\]]yi ni dgang bar bya/_/gal te de lta na yang 'di/_/lo ni nyi shu ma tshang na/_/dge tshul nyid du gnas 'gyur te/_/de ni bsnyen par rdzogs min nyid/_/lo ni gcig gam gnyis dag na/_/gal te dran pa rnyed gyur na/_/'di yi lo ni tshang ba yis/_/bsnyen par rdzogs pa nyid yin no/_/'on pas kyang ni go gyur na/_/las 
[\[4b.2\]]brjod pa ni ma thos kyang /_/'di la bsnyen rdzogs yod pa ste/_/tshul khrims ldan la nyes pa med/_/bsnyen par rdzogs par byas nas de/_/gal te mtshan ni gyur pa na/_/'di ni bsnyen rdzogs kho na ste/_/dge slong ma yi 'khor nang bzhag_/mkhan po mtshan ni gyur pa na/_/gal te gsol ba thos na ni/_/bsnyen par rdzogs pa kho na ste/_/las byed pa 
[\[4b.3\]]la'ang nyes pa med/_/las byed pa ni mtshan gyur na/_/gal te las brjod thos na 'dir/_/mkhan po la ni nyes byas te/_/las byed rnams la skyon yod min/_/las byed nam mkha' la gnas te/_/sa la 'dug la byed gyur na/_/bsnyen par rdzogs pa rgyang ring ste/_/tshul khrims 'chal pas mtho ris bzhin/_/ji ltar mi yi bdag 
[\[4b.4\]]po yi/_/gzhon nu rigs rgyud 'phel phyir bsrung /_/de bzhin ston pa'i bstan pa dag_/'phel phyir dge tshul rnams kyang bsrung /_/shing sdong mtho la dge tshul ni/_/bla ma'i bka' yis 'dzegs pa las/_/shing chag nas ni lhung ba'i tshe/_/de la 'di dag gsungs pa yin/_/gang phyir 'di ni 
[\[4b.5\]]rab byung ba/_/bstan la thugs rje'i bdag nyid kyis/_/bya rog skrod nus gyur lta na/_/lo bdun pa yang rjes su gnang /_/rab byung dang ni bsnyen rdzogs la/_/lhung bzed can min mi drang ste/_/bsod snyoms rgyu ba rnams la ni/_/de tshe snod don gnyer bas 'thob/_/gnas brtan nye dgas 
[\[4b.6\]]gang gi tshe/_/dge tshul bru bas nyen pa na/_/lhung bzed med par lhung bzed don/_/gzhan song ston mo'i bar du thob/_/blangs nas kyang ni lhung bzed sogs/_/blo [{]bzangs kyis [,]bzang gis [}]ni sbyin par bya/_/rab byung bsnyen rdzogs dag kyang rung /_/bram ze bzhin du slar mi 'gro/_/bsnyen par ma rdzogs 
[\[4b.7\]]gong du ni/_/nges par gnas rnams mi brjod de/_/thos nas 'di ni bya dka' zhes/_/bram ze slar yang khyim du song /_/dge slong rnams kyi las gcig gis/_/bzhi ni bsnyen par rdzogs mi bya/_/tshogs dag la ni tshogs gnyis kyis/_/las ni gang du'ang cung zad med/_/gnyis sam 
[\[5a\]]
[\[5a.1\]]gsum po dag kyang rung /_/dus gcig la ni bsnyen rdzogs na/_/na tshod bye brag yod gyur kyang /_/rgan po med cing gsar bu med/_/phan tshun phyag kyang yod min te/_/rnyed pa gral rims bzhin du'o/_/skul byed dbang gis de dag la/_/las bcol ba ni rim pas 
[\[5a.2\]]so/_/mtshams med lnga ni byas pa dang /_/rku thabs gnas dang sprul pa dang /_/mu stegs can zhugs 'on dang lkugs/_/'khrugs dang de bzhin phas pham byung /_/dge slong ma ni sun phyung dang /_/ma ning la sogs gnas mi bya/_/tshwa sgo'i zhing ltar de dag la/_/tshul khrims sa bon 
[\[5a.3\]]skye ba med/_/de bzhin gzhan yang bag med long /_/bsdigs pa la sogs las rnams kyis/_/smad do blang bar bya ba ni/_/sems ni dul bar gyur na yin/_/mdo sde 'dul ba mngon pa rnams/_/mkhas shing grags dang ldan pa dang /_/bsod nams chen po la sogs ni/_/bskrad 
[\[5a.4\]]cing bkar bar 'os pa min/_/gang phyir 'di dag mu stegs kyi/_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]skrag byed seng ge'i bu/_/'di dag khyim par gyur na yang /_/ston pa'i blo yis rab dad bskyed/_/'di dag rnams kyis gtsug lag khang /_/mdzes byed khyu mchog sgra sgrogs shing /_/de bas 'di dag smad na ni/_/
[\[5a.5\]]rang gi bstan pa smad pa yin/_/kun nas nyon mongs skyes na de/_/ma rungs gdon gyis rnam rmongs pas/_/bya min byed pa kho na ste/_/'jig rten pas ni smod par 'gyur/_/'thab krol can dang khyim sun 'byin/_/gsum pa tshul khrims 'chal pa ste/_/las ni byed pa na yang 'di/_/
[\[5a.6\]]gal te 'gyur ba rten byed na/_/'bad pas gaN+DI brdung byas nas/_/dge 'dun gyis ni bskrad par bya/_/ka ba la ni 'jus kyang bcad/_/sgo skyes la 'jus de bzhin no/_/yang na dge 'dun thams cad kyis/_/slar yang gso ba'i 'du byed bya/_/gang yang dge 'dun de dag las/_/dpung byed 
[\[5a.7\]]yan lag 'byin bya ste/_/min na de 'dra'i dge slong gi_/rtags sun 'byin pa bzhag mi rung /_/nan tan gyis ni bskrad bya ste/_/'thab dang rtsod pa med par bya/_/de yi gnas pa cir 'gyur na/_/ro dang 'dra ba kho nar gsungs/_/dge slong shes skyon dri ma can/_/dge slong tshogs kyis 
[\[5b\]]
[\[5b.1\]]chad byin la/_/dge slong mas ni gus bstan phyir/_/sngon du ldang gi phyag mi bya/_/dge bsnyen gsong por smra mi bya/_/khyim pas bsod snyoms sbyin bya ste/_/'phags pa'i tshogs ni mnyes pa na/_/bya ba thams cad sngon bzhin no/_/'khyud chags phrag dog nad dang skyes/_/
[\[5b.2\]]zla phyed ces bya ma ning lnga/_/'di dag re re'i mtshan nyid ni/_/rtogs pa'i don du brjod par bya/_/gang la gzhan gyis mnan pa na/_/'khrig phyir sems la 'dod chags ni/_/rnam rgyas de ni blo ldan gyis/_/'khyud dang ma ning shes par bya/_/gzhan gyi mtshan ni mthong ba las/_/
[\[5b.3\]]rang la 'dod skye phrag dog grags/_/mtshon nam nad kyis rtags nyams pa/_/de ni nad kyi ma ning brjod/_/gang skyes nyid nas mtshan ma ni/_/gnyis ka med pa skyes nas yin/_/zla phyed bud med zla phyed ni/_/skyes gyur zla phyed ma ning brjod/_/'dir ni sogs kyi sgra med kyang /_/zla sogs ma ning 
[\[5b.4\]]grub pa yin/_/gang zhig grong gi chos dag gi_/nus med de ni za ma ste/_/mtshan ma gnyis ka dod pa ni/_/mtshan gnyis par ni 'dod pa yin/_/mu stegs log ltas bsgos pa yi/_/yid can de ni mu stegs 'dod/_/mu stegs chos kyi longs spyod gang /_/de ni mu stegs can 
[\[5b.5\]]zhugs pa'o/_/dge tshul gang yang [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]pa yis/_/rang gi rtags ni bzung nas kyang /_/dge slong nyid du rab shes byed/_/de ni rku thabs gnas par 'dod/_/phyogs las gzhan gyi phyogs dag ni/_/tha dad tshogs la yang dag brten/_/gang zhig tshogs kyis chad pa byin/_/de ni tha 
[\[5b.6\]]dad gnas par 'dod/_/phas pham dag la log par lta/_/dge slong ma sogs sun 'byin dang /_/bden dang dkon mchog gsum gyi ni/_/sngags min sgrogs pa'i dge tshul nyid/_/skyon can bcu po 'di dag ni/_/chad pa'i las kyis bkar bar bya/_/phyung nas slar yang mi gzhug ste/_/
[\[5b.7\]]ji bzhin bshags na skal ba bzhin/_/dge slong pham pa ma byung na/_/cho ga bzhin du bslab phul nas/_/song na'ang skal ldan slar yang ni/_/bsnyen rdzogs dga' ston chen po bya/_/sdom gtong rang bzhin gnas pa yi/_/brda phrad mi yi drung dag tu/_/bdag gis gang bzung bslab pa ni/_/
[\[6a\]]
[\[6a.1\]]gtong ngo zhes sogs tshig rnams 'bul/_/bsnyen par rdzogs pa'i rjes thogs la/_/phas pham rnams ni brjod par bya/_/rtogs ldan blo ni de dag las/_/'da' bar byed pa ma yin no/_/mi 'chab pa yi sems kyis ni/_/gal te byung na gcig la'ang mthol/_/
[\[6a.2\]]chung ngu la'ang 'jig pa'i phyir/_/de la phas pham mi 'byung ngo /_/brtul zhugs can gyis bla ma pas/_/sngar nyid mal nas langs nas ni/_/so shing bcas nas de 'og tu/_/ston pa la ni phyag bya'o/_/de nas rab gus bskyed pa yis/_/bla ma la ni phyag bya ste/_/de ni de la gdams 
[\[6a.3\]]ngag sogs/_/cho ga bzhin du phan 'dogs yin/_/de la khams bde sogs dris nas/_/rab tu 'bad pas de yi ni/_/gnas khang chag chag yang dag gdab/_/nyer spyod sa yang sbyang bar bya/_/ston pa la ni maN+Dal dang /_/mchod rten la yang bskor ba bya/_/brtul zhugs rgan 
[\[6a.4\]]la phyag byas nas/_/de yi nus pa ji bzhin du/_/bsam gtan klog pa'i las dag gis/_/lus la snying po blang bar bya/_/ngur smrig gyon tsam gyis chog pa'i/_/bsam pa nam yang mi bya'o/_/so shing dus dang mthun pa'i chu/_/'dug stan la sogs bstabs par bya/_/sman 
[\[6a.5\]]dang rnyed dka' zhim pa'i zas/_/'bras bu sogs kyang de bzhin no/_/lhung bzed la sogs bkru ba yis/_/gus pas bla ma'i nye gnas bya/_/bla ma rnam par mdzangs pas kyang /_/thams cad du ni tshod rig bya/_/tshul khrims 'chal la zhe sdang dang /_/phyag kyang nam yang mi 
[\[6a.6\]]bya ste/_/de dang longs spyod kyang ma yin/_/'di ni ro bsregs mgal dum bzhin/_/khyim pa sdom la gnas kyang ni/_/dge tshul gyi ni phyag mi bya/_/'dod can khyim gyi chos rnams la/_/dge slong gis lta smos ci dgos/_/de bzhin khyim pa sdom ldan yang /_/sdom brtson kun 
[\[6a.7\]]gyis phyag mi rung /_/kun nas ma bsdams de dag la/_/phyag len dbang ni yod ma yin/_/srid pa tha ma pa la ni/_/rigs pas rnam dpyad mi 'thad de/_/mi brtan 'khor bar 'khor bzhin pa'i/_/sems can rnams la snga ma bzhin/_/gzhan yang phyag bya min mang ste/_/
[\[6b\]]
[\[6b.1\]]spo dang bskrad pa la sogs pa'o/_/dge slong gis rtags phyag bya ba/_/sangs rgyas dang ni sdom pas rgan/_/mi slob pa ni bdag por spyod/_/slob pa byin pa spyod pa ste/_/bsam gtan klog dang ldan pa ni/_/rjes gnang spyod pas nyes pa med/_/lhag ma le los bcom pa 
[\[6b.2\]]yi/_/bdag nyid rnams ni bu lon spyod/_/khrims 'chal rnams ni nang du yang /_/gtsug lag khang sogs nyer spyod bkag_/bshang khang dang ni nye ba ru/_/sna tshogs gtam ni mi bya ste/_/klog dang kha ton la sogs dang /_/tshon gyi las kyang khyad par du'o/_/bshang khang dang ni gci ba'i 
[\[6b.3\]]gnas/_/tha dad du ni bya bar gsungs/_/chu 'dor tshe na gci khang du/_/sgra brda byas nas 'jug par bya/_/de yi khang par gros sogs min/_/dal bu dal bus sgra med par/_/rlung dang bshang ba gci ba rnams/_/dor byas nan gyis mi bsdam mo/_/sngon du 'jam po gang rung gis/_/'og 
[\[6b.4\]]gi sgo ni phyis nas kyang /_/de nas lan gnyis lan gsum du/_/sa yis dag par bkru bar bya/_/lag pa g.yon par 'jam pa yi/_/sa yi phung po bdun bya ste/_/gnyis kar yang ni bdun po nyid/_/so sor tha dad du ni bzhag_/thog mar shA ri'i bu la 'di/_/bslab bcas 
[\[6b.5\]]gzhan du nyes byas 'gyur/_/de nas lag pa'ang 'bad pa yis/_/chu mang pos ni rab tu bkru/_/lag ngar byin pa phyis nas ni/_/slar yang lag pa dag ni bkru/_/lag gdong rkang pa dag dang ni/_/phyi rol dag byar gsungs pa yin/_/dri yi byug pa spangs pa yi/_/gtsang sbra sems 
[\[6b.6\]]ni sbyang ba yin/_/'di 'dra'i byug mchog yod pa la/_/stong phrag sa yis ci zhig bya/_/rdzogs sangs rgyas kyis nang gi ni/_/gtsang sbra don dam dag pa nyid/_/lus dang ngag dang yid dag gis/_/sdig pa spong bar gsungs pa yin/_/so shing byed dang bshang ba dang /_/gci ba 'dor dang 
[\[6b.7\]]chu 'thung dang /_/mtshams nang rten la phyag 'tshal ba/_/bla ma dag la zhu mi dgos/_/gang du skabs dang mthun pa yi/_/ra ba rtsig pa rta babs nas/_/'dom ni bzhi bcu rtsa dgu'i bar/_/nang gi mtshams su shes par bya/_/sdom pa lhag ma med gcod dang /_/dge 'dun lhag 
[\[7a\]]
[\[7a.1\]]mas de ldang dang /_/de bzhin gang zag lhag ma can/_/ltung ba rnam pa gsum du grags/_/dang por phas pham gsungs pa ni/_/bzhi yin shin tu lci ba'i phyir/_/'di dag gang rung byung na ni/_/dge slong rnam par nyams pa yin/_/ston ka'i chu bzhin dri med par/_/
[\[7a.2\]]ston pa'i bstan pa bcu gnyis lor/_/ltung ba med par legs gnas te/_/nyon mongs spong bar byed pa yin/_/de nas bzang sbyin bu yi phyir/_/chung ma la ni 'khrig pa spyad/_/dge slong dgon pa pa yis kyang /_/spra mo la zhugs kun mkhyen gyis/_/'di skad smad pa mdzad de khyed/_/
[\[7a.3\]]'dod chags la sogs zhi bya'i phyir/_/nga yis chos rnams bstan pa la/_/'di ltar sdig pa'i las la chags/_/de nas phan yon bcu gzigs shing /_/slob ma rnams la phan bzhed pas/_/bslab pa'i gzhi ni bca' ba dag_/thugs rje'i bdag nyid can gyis mdzad/_/mchil 
[\[7a.4\]]ma gci dang bshang ba yi/_/lam du chags can zhugs na ni/_/gso byar mi rung bdug pa yi/_/phas pham sbrul gyis dge slong zin/_/kha shun so dang pags pa las/_/nor bu 'das na phas pham ste/_/phyi rol char ni ci rigs par/_/sbom po'am dge 'dun lhag ma'o/_/gang phyir stobs 
[\[7a.5\]]kyis mnan nas kyang /_/brtul zhugs can gyis grong chos kyi/_/bde bsten de yang phas pham gsungs/_/nyams pa dang ni rma yi sgor/_/rab zhugs sbom por shes par bya/_/mi shes byis pa chung ngu gang /_/bsten mi bzod la spyad pa yang /_/cung zad zin dang shas cher zin/_/skrangs 
[\[7a.6\]]dang rul te nyams pa'o/_/gang zhig gnyi ga rnal du gnas/_/skyon med de ni ma nyams pa'o/_/thams cad du ni rma yi sgo/_/gsum po nyams pa la yang ni/_/gang zhig de la zhugs pa na/_/sbom po yin no mthar thug min/_/nang du ma chud pa dang ni/_/las su mi rung bas kyang ni/_/
[\[7a.7\]]'khrig pa spyod par byed pa la/_/sbom po nyid du grags pa yin/_/mo mtshan mtshams dang ma 'byor dang /_/'jigs pas bud med 'dor bzhin dang /_/yang na ngo tsha yis kyang 'dor/_/mi 'dor gyur kyang nyams pa dang /_/gang yang byis pa 'dod pa yi/_/longs spyod mi shes mi rung 
[\[7b\]]
[\[7b.1\]]la/_/de ltar bstan na thams cad du/_/sbom po'i kha na ma tho bcas/_/chung ngu 'bring sogs dbye ba yis/_/'dus byas thams cad tha dad de/_/de yi ltung ba nges pa yi/_/dbye ba grub par the tshom med/_/bu ga gzhan dang lus kyi ni/_/bu ga bral nas de dag tu/_/lus skyes 
[\[7b.2\]]'jug par byed na yang /_/sdom brtson la ni sbom por brjod/_/byis pa chung la zhugs pa yi/_/dge slong pham par yong mi brtag_/nu ma mchu dang sha mtshams bzhin/_/nyes spyod rgyas par mi 'gyur dman/_/mche ba rno [{]mangs [,]mang [}]gdug pa yi/_/sbrul gyi khar ni chud pa bla/_/lus bde 
[\[7b.3\]]bstan pa'i rma la ni/_/lus skyes 'jug pa ji ltar rung /_/sbrul gdug gis ni reg gyur kyang /_/skye ba gcig nyid 'chi 'gyur gyi/_/khrims 'chal skye ba brgyud na ni/_/stong phrag sdug bsngal mi bzad 'thob/_/lci dang shin tu lci ba nyid/_/yang dang shin tu yang ba ste/_/phas pham 
[\[7b.4\]]pas ni bslang ba yi/_/sbom po rnam pa bzhi yin no/_/skrag par mi byed lci ba ste/_/gzhan gyis de la 'bad pa yis/_/bzlog kyang mi nyan rab lcir shes/_/gzhan la khrel yod yang ba ste/_/rang nyid ngo tshas shin tu yang /_/bsam pa'i dbye bas 'gyur ba yin/_/de bzhin 
[\[7b.5\]]dge 'dun lhag ma yis/_/kun bslang sbom po rnam pa bzhi/_/dge slong nyid dang de ma nyams/_/ma nyams pa yi lam dag tu/_/bcug reg ro ni myong ba dang /_/gang phyir sems ni rang bzhin gnas/_/rang gi khang pa brtsig pa'i phyir/_/rgyal po ma skyes dgra yi shing /_/dge slong nor can 
[\[7b.6\]]gyis brkus te/_/de brten 'di dag kun bslang bcas/_/ma 'khrul gnas nas spo byed cing /_/gzhan nor brku sems mthu yis sam/_/'jab bu yis kyang de la mthar/_/mi las ma sha lnga tshang brkus/_/ma sha ma ga d+hA pa ste/_/de las lhag pa mkhas pas shes/_/dngos po bskyod 
[\[7b.7\]]pas sbom po ste/_/sbyor ba la ni nyes byas so/_/sa gzhi 'jam zhing mnyam pa la/_/bskyod kyang mthar ni mi 'gyur te/_/sa phyogs grum gas la sogs pa/_/mthon dman can la rab tu bskyed/_/zhe sdang gi ni rgya la sogs/_/chud son byed pa sbom po yin/_/sems can rnams ni dgrol 
[\[8a\]]
[\[8a.1\]]don du/_/bsod nams 'dod pas bshags bya'o/_/gang gi yul ni de yi dus/_/gang gi tshad kyi 'gron bu ni/_/de yi bzhi cha brkus pa gang /_/dge slong la ni pham par 'gyur/_/sa gzhi rtsod bcas rnam pa gnyis/_/rigs dang rgyal po'i rigs kyis te/_/gnyi ga 
[\[8a.2\]]la yang dge slong ni/_/rgyal na ltung ba sbom por brjod/_/khyim pas gal te sbyor btang na/_/gnyis kar yang ni phas pham nyid/_/phas pham me yis bsregs pa yi/_/shing tshig lta bur sdom brtson 'gyur/_/nor bu mu tig la sogs nor/_/sngags kyis bkug nas rku ba na/_/mthong 
[\[8a.3\]]ba tsam gyis de las 'dir/_/ltung ba'i mthar ni 'gyur ba yin/_/bdag gi lo tog phun tshogs bya/_/gzhan la min zhes sred pa yis/_/rang sar chu ni 'dren byed cing /_/tshan gyis 'jig par byed kyang rung /_/rang gi lo tog phun tshogs shing /_/gzhan gyi lo tog nyams pa na/_/
[\[8a.4\]]lo tog rin ni shes byas nas/_/sbom po'am phas pham ci rigs brtag_/sdig pa kun las ldog phyir dang /_/sems can rnams la phan gdags phyir/_/nam yang gzhan gyi 'tsho ba ni/_/dge slong gis ni dbrog mi bya/_/rkun pos slob ma phrogs pa'am/_/gser sogs stobs kyis mi bslang 
[\[8a.5\]]ste/_/gzhung lugs ji bzhin ma bsgrubs na/_/ma rungs ltung ba dag dang bcas/_/chos bshad pa 'am phyed rin nam/_/rin ni tshang ba yis kyang rung /_/blang bya rkun ma rgyal po la/_/phul na ltung ba sbom por 'gyur/_/slob ma nyid phyir mthun btsal nas/_/dge tshul dge slong gis phrogs na/_/
[\[8a.6\]]'khor dbye ba yi kun bslang phyir/_/sbom po'i blo can sbom pos reg_/dge slong gis ni sho gam sogs/_/rang ngam gzhan gyi lag gis phrogs/_/dngos po brtag par bya ba ste/_/ltung ba nges par bsten par 'gyur/_/rgyal po la sogs dbang ba yi/_/chu 'gram gru btsas las byung ba/_/
[\[8a.7\]]ma byin lam gang nas song na/_/rang ngam gzhan gyi lag nyid kyis/_/de ni de yi phrogs 'dra ste/_/ltung ba rnam par dpyad pa na/_/ma sha lnga yi rin gyis ni/_/mthar 'gyur gzhan du ma yin no/_/sangs rgyas chos dang 'phags tshogs sam/_/pha phyir gang zhig bsams pa yi/_/bsngags 
[\[8b\]]
[\[8b.1\]]pa de la brjod pas sam/_/phyi nas brtul zhugs can gyis bgyir/_/mang po ri ba'i dngos po gcig_/rung ba'i chos gos khyer ba bzhin/_/des ni sba bar mi bya ste/_/nges par ltung ba med par 'dod/_/gzhan yang sho gam la 'da' bar/_/kha tshar bcad pa la sogs sam/_/chu 
[\[8b.2\]]yis bsnyugs nas kyang ni rung /_/thugs rje'i bdag nyid can gyis gnang /_/gang gis chos gos la sogs pa/_/'dod pas dang du len pa ni/_/'gyod pas btang yang de la ni/_/ltung ba sbom por shes par bya/_/ri la 'dzeg gam gru brgal tshe/_/phan tshun so sor go byas nas/_/
[\[8b.3\]]lhung bzed la sogs snod bskur te/_/bag yod rtag tu dran pa yis/_/'di ni bzung ngam btang ngo zhes/_/smras thos gal te brlag gyur na/_/de rin nges par sbyin pa la/_/'dir ni rnam dpyad mi dgos so/_/ci te ma bos zan za na/_/dge slong la ni nyes byas te/_/'di ni sde 
[\[8b.4\]]ldan lhag ma la'ang /_/nyes byas nyid du rab tu grags/_/rgyal pos byin dang rkun pos byin/_/las byed dag gis ma dris byin/_/gzhan dang 'brel par 'du shes na/_/sbyin po de las mi blang ngo /_/gzhan dang 'brel pa pham pa yin/_/ster bar rnam par shes na ni/_/'dod 
[\[8b.5\]]pa mi mthun mi blang ste/_/mi shes na ni nges pa med/_/sbyin bdag nges par rnam dpyad nas/_/de phyir gtso bo ma yin na/_/de las blang bar mi rigs te/_/de ni sbyin por mi 'dod do/_/gal te dge 'dun sogs kyi nor/_/rang gi gnyen nam gzhan gyis kyang /_/ster na nor med nad pa 
[\[8b.6\]]yis/_/slar yang bsab par 'dod pas blang /_/gal te shi na nyes med de/_/gson na gang yang rung ba ni/_/blangs nas kyang ni ci nus kyis/_/de yi dkor ni bsab par bya/_/ba lang la sogs longs spyod mchog_/grong la sogs par longs spyod pa/_/'di dag dge 'dun la ni 
[\[8b.7\]]gnang /_/gang zag la ni bkag pa yin/_/longs spyod zhing dang grang dang rta/_/ba lang ma he ra lug rnams/_/'dul bar longs spyod mchog nyid du/_/de nyid rig pas gsungs pa yin/_/dngos po rnam pa lnga po ni/_/shes rab can gyis mi bcang zhing /_/mi gtsang mi bgo 
[\[9a\]]
[\[9a.1\]]thams cad du/_/de bzhin khas kyang mi blang ngo /_/skyed mos tshal dang gtsug lag khang /_/de yi dngos dang mal stan rnams/_/tshul ldan rnams kyis sbyin bdag gi/_/bsod nams spel phyir bsrung bar bya/_/lha khang skyed tshal kun dga' ra/_/gtsug lag 
[\[9a.2\]]khang ni gcig tu 'dod/_/de dag dngos po gnas khang yin/_/mal stan dag ni grags par zad/_/gtsug lag khang steng gnas pa yi/_/bsgo ba gang du gsal bar thos/_/bka' bsgo ba ni byed pa yis/_/de la nges par 'jug par bya/_/las kyi bya ba byed pa'i tshe/_/zas dang gos kyi 
[\[9a.3\]]rjes su bzung /_/nad pa la ni rjes su gnang /_/las ni mi nus pa la yang /_/brdeg dang 'tshog dang skyo ba dag_/'phags pa'i chos dang 'gal ba yin/_/bsad dang bcing dang nyon mongs byed/_/'phags pas de phyir rnam par spang /_/ci rigs mchog gis gsungs pa dang /_/
[\[9a.4\]]rigs kyi chos ldan gyis bstan pa'i/_/yul chos ldan pa'i chad pa ni/_/dge 'dun gyis ni dang du blang /_/rgyal po la sogs yongs btang ba'i/_/rang skal bsod nams [(]gyis [,]kyis [)]rnyed na/_/yongs su longs spyod nyes med de/_/de dag sngar bstan bzhin du gnas/_/'dren pas dngos 
[\[9a.5\]]po thams cad kyis/_/tha snyad rang gis ma gsungs te/_/dge slong ma 'am dge tshul dag_/nges pa'i zhal ta ba yis blta/_/ston pa'i chos las 'das nas ni/_/sred ldan gang zhig chad pas gcod/_/de ni bslab pa nyams pa ste/_/ma rig sgrib g.yogs [(]gyis [,]kyis [)]bskyed pa'o/_/
[\[9a.6\]]yang dag lta dang gzhan thob kyi/_/log lta can dang tshul khrims med/_/de la rab tu byin na ni/_/dad pas byin pa'i 'bras bu med/_/gang yang 'tsho ba'i tshad tsam zhig_/khengs las lhag dang brtul zhugs can/_/ston mo dag las len byed na/_/des ni rnam par nyams par byas/_/
[\[9a.7\]]ji tsam gang gis chog gyur pa/_/de tsam ston mo las ni blang /_/lhag ma kham gcig len byed kyang /_/bsams bzhin yin na nyams pa nyid/_/pha ma'am nad pa la sogs la/_/sbyin par byed pa bkag pa med/_/sbyin bdag dag gis ma spags par/_/nam yang gzhan la sbyin 
[\[9b\]]
[\[9b.1\]]mi bya/_/gang du 'chag pa la sogs tshe/_/gri khab la sogs rnyed gyur na/_/gtsug lag khang skyong dag la gtad/_/lung bzhin de yis go bar bya/_/dge 'dun dbus su zhag gsum bar/_/de yis de dag bsten par bya/_/su yang ngo len med na ni/_/ci bde bar ni tshogs la dbul/_/
[\[9b.2\]]dgos pa'i tshe na las rnams bya/_/bsod nams bsags pas las bya yi/_/rnyed pa'i don du dge slong gis/_/las ni nam yang mi bya'o/_/yid ches gyur la rang gi blos/_/ji srid du ni nges pa yis/_/'jog pa la ni nyes med de/_/dbang po gzhan ni bzhag nas so/_/gang phyir 
[\[9b.3\]]dman dang 'bring dang mchog_/grogs po gsum ni rnam dpyad nas/_/yid ches brtan po nyid la bca'i/_/nges par sems mi brtan la min/_/shin tu mchog la mchog nyid de/_/'bring la 'bring po tha ma la/_/tha ma nyid de dman la chung /_/yid ches rnam pa gsum du 'dod/_/nad 
[\[9b.4\]]pa la ni dris nas sman/_/phan pa'i ched du sbyin bya ste/_/rang gi yid ches kyis kyang rung /_/til snod dge slong ma bzhin min/_/mi dang gzhan nor rku sems kyis/_/sbyor ba gcig la tshang na ni/_/mdor na bslab pa nyams pa ste/_/phas pham pa yi rgyu yin no/_/dge slong mi gtsang yid 
[\[9b.5\]]byung gang /_/[{]ri dgas [,]ri dwags [}]dbyug thogs zhes pas bsad/_/bsod nams thob dang lhung bzed sogs/_/'dod pa de bsten bkag pa yin/_/yang dag bsam pa ma 'khrul pas/_/rang ngam gzhan gyis pha rol bsad/_/mi la 'chi ba'i bsngags pa dag_/brjod grub des kyang mthar 
[\[9b.6\]]'gyur ro/_/de 'chi'i thabs ni tshig gis sam/_/sbyor ba rnams kyis mngon bsngags shing /_/sems ni rjes su yi rang na/_/sbom po'o me yis nags bsreg dang /_/mi yi yan lag gcod pa dang /_/de yi sha ni za yang ngo /_/nad pa la ni nad g.yog dag_/phan phyir byis pa legs par 
[\[9b.7\]]bsgo/_/mkhas pa'am gnas brtan dag la yang /_/sman ni yongs su dris nas su/_/nad pa dag la phan blos sbyin/_/gzhan du 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/gang gi nad la ci 'dra ba'i/_/yo byad nad pa dag la gzhag/_/brtags pa za ba la sogs kyang /_/rjes su brtse bas nyes pa 
[\[10a\]]
[\[10a.1\]]med/_/dge 'dun kun gyi nad pa ni/_/blta phyir brtson byar bka' stsal te/_/thams cad du ni rim pa bzhin/_/'dug nas kyang ni blta bya ste/_/nad pa'i nad g.yog dag gis kyang /_/'bad pas 'dug pa'i don du ni/_/gus pas rtag tu stan rnams ni/_/nges par nye bar 
[\[10a.2\]]bstab par bya/_/gal te nad pa nus med pas/_/skyo bar gyur pa de lta na/_/de la 'dir ni re mos kyis/_/yang dag bsgo bar brtag pa nyid/_/nad pa'i mdun du gang zhig gis/_/thos sam tshor bar de yi sems/_/'chi ba'i 'dun pa skye 'gyur ba/_/de 'dra'i chos bshad mi bya'o/_/
[\[10a.3\]]kye ma khyod kyis sbyin pa byin/_/tshul khrims kyang ni ma nyams bsrungs/_/dkon mchog gsum la gus byas te/_/mya ngan 'das pa mdun na gnas/_/khyod ni lus dang bral ma thag_/lha yi khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]thag mi ring /_/srog dang bral na myur du ni/_/mya ngan 'das pa lag na 
[\[10a.4\]]gnas/_/de skad gang smra'i dge slong ni/_/mi zad ltung ba dag dang bcas/_/de bas khyod ni nang bral nas/_/yun ring chos spyod 'tsho bar shog_/chos spyod ji srid gson na ni/_/de srid bsod nams tshogs chen po/_/rdzogs par 'gyur zhes brjod par bya/_/nad kyis gdungs la sman 
[\[10a.5\]]dag dang /_/mkhas pas gdams ngag sbyin bya zhes/_/gnas dang gnas min mkhyen pas gsungs/_/skra can 'tshong na sbom po yin/_/lhung bzed sogs chags gzhan dag la/_/gdol pa bzhin du brtse med par/_/gang zhig 'dod sems skyed byed pa/_/de la nyes byas nyid 
[\[10a.6\]]du rig_/bzhad gad rgod bag ched du yang /_/ga ga tshil ni mi bya ste/_/sngon ni gzhon nu bcu bdun sdes/_/dge slong g.ya' bsgags shi bas bcas/_/mchod rten la sogs las byed pas/_/khyim pa med tshe lci ba'i khur/_/mi khyer mi gzhug shing mkhan ni/_/shing bteg shi ba 
[\[10a.7\]]la rten bkag_/so phag gas po la sogs pa/_/brda ma sprad par sbyin mi bya/_/nyi ma zad par dge slong gis/_/gla mi bzhin du las mi bya/_/las kyi zhal ta blo ldan gyis/_/de dag la ni go byas nas/_/de rnams las dub spang ba'i phyir/_/khye sus bza' btung gis tshim bya/_/
[\[10b\]]
[\[10b.1\]]gtsug lag khang ni bsrung ba'i phyir/_/brtson par yang ni rjes su gnang /_/sems can dag la ltos med par/_/ma bsams dbyug sogs mi 'phang ngo /_/khru bcu'i nang rol spyod yul du/_/rdo ba la sogs dor bar rung /_/bslab pa rjes dran bskyed pa yis/_/nang gi bdag nyid 
[\[10b.2\]]nyams mi 'gyur/_/gtsug lag khang gi las byed la/_/mtshan mo chos sgrogs gnas dag tu/_/sgo sogs bsdams par byas sam zhes/_/gnas brtan gyis ni dri bar bya/_/gtsug lag khang 'joms bzlog pa'i phyir/_/gang la bcing ba brtag bya bar/_/bcing ba sngar ni brjod pa 
[\[10b.3\]]yis/_/gtsug lag khang ni bsrung bar bya/_/'khrul gtan steng gzer 'og 'jug dang /_/sgo lcags lde mig kyog po dang /_/gtsug lag khang gnas de dag la/_/zhal ta ba yis ci rigs sbyin/_/gcig dang gnyis dang gsum sogs kyis/_/tha dad bcing ba gzhug byas te/_/
[\[10b.4\]]thams cad gzhug par 'os na yang /_/rtog pa po yis thams cad gzhug_/ston pa'i bstan la gus pa yi/_/dge slong lus dub thang chad dag_/lam du dge slong rnams kyis ni/_/pha bzhin bsgrub par mi bya'o/_/sngon byung pha rgan dal 'gro la/_/dus yol gyur pa'i 'jigs pa yis/_/
[\[10b.5\]]bu yis phul bas lam du de/_/dub nas 'chi ba'i dus byas so/_/mu ge'i dus shig dge slong rnams/_/mi srid bla ma'i yon tan rnams/_/phan tshun bsgrags te go byas rgyus/_/'tsho ba byas tshe bkag pa mdzad/_/khyad par gyur pa'i rtogs pa rnams/_/ma thob par ni thob bo zhes/_/
[\[10b.6\]]mngon pa'i nga rgyal med smra na/_/sdom brtson mtha' yi ltung ba 'thob/_/mi chos bla ma'i yon tan 'dir/_/bsam gtan rnams dang gzugs med sogs/_/mthong zad phul phyin mya ngan 'das/_/'phags pa'i lam gyi thob [(]smra' [,]smra'o[)]/_/mkhas pas khyad par rtogs pa'i don/_/gang 
[\[10b.7\]]gsungs 'bras bu bzhi po dang /_/sdug bsngal la sogs yul shes pa/_/bden pa bzhi po mthong zhes dang /_/bsam gtan zhes brjod bzhi po yi/_/de la reg pas gnas pa dang /_/sdug bsngal la sogs bdag gis shes/_/lha la sogs pa bdag gis mthong /_/lha klu 
[\[11a\]]
[\[11a.1\]]gnod sbyin dri za sogs/_/mthong ngo bdag gis gtam yang byas/_/de dag kyang ni bdag la smra/_/zhes smra dge slong mthar 'gyur ro/_/lha yi skad ni bdag gis thos/_/de ni mtshan mo bdag drung 'ongs/_/de bzhin gnod sbyin sogs kyang ste/_/rang bzhin 
[\[11a.2\]]gyis ni pham par 'dod/_/de dag kun la shes bya ba/_/sbom po sha za mthong sogs te/_/'di na gang zhig lha sogs dag/_/mthong ba yod ces smra na yang /_/rnam rnags mi rtag 'du shes sogs/_/'bras bu mngon shes rab ston na/_/mi 'dod na yang mtha' yi ni/_/mtshon 
[\[11a.3\]]chas mgo bcad thob par 'gyur/_/gang zhig 'di na dge slong gang /_/rdul khrod sha za mthong ba yod/_/bdag ces smra bar mi byed kyang /_/de skad brjod na nyes byas so/_/ltung ba 'chab dang reg pa dang /_/lus bkan bag med phyogs byed nyid/_/de bzhin dge slong phas 
[\[11a.4\]]pham bzhis/_/dge slong ma yi rtsa ba 'jig_/rgyal dang pham dang de bzhin du/_/char 'bab mtshan ni 'gyur ba sogs/_/dgongs su gsol zhes brjod bya ste/_/gzhan du 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/dge slong ser skyas 'od srungs kyi/_/bstan la ngag rtsub [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]
[\[11a.5\]]smras pas/_/chu srin bco brgyad gdong skyes bcas/_/nyon mongs dgra pham khas blangs nas/_/nyon mongs dag gis pham byas na/_/de phyir phas pham gyur pa ste/_/khrims dral zhes ni nges par brjod/_/phas pham pa yi chos dag tu/_/gang gsungs ma lus brjod nas ni/_/rim pa 
[\[11a.6\]]ji bzhin dge 'dun gyi/_/lhag ma rnams ni brjod par bya/_/rma sgo gsum ni ma gtogs par/_/shes lus rang bzhin yan lag gis/_/bsams bzhin khu ba 'byin pa ni/_/dge 'dun gyi ni lhag mas reg_/sla dang ska ba nyid dang dmar/_/ser po dang ni sngon po ste/_/khu ba rnam pa 
[\[11a.7\]]lngar bstan pa/_phyi ma 'khor los sgyur rgyal gyis/_/de yi bu ni sngon po ste/_/de yi 'og tu ser po yin/_/dmar po de yi blon po ste/_/ska ba dbang po smin pa nyid/_/bud med kyis dub dbang po dag_/ma smin pa yi sla bar shes/_/thams cad du yang dge 'dun gyis/_/dag bya lhag ma'i 
[\[11b\]]
[\[11b.1\]]ltung ba nyid/_/shes bzhin rtsig khung bum pa yi/_/sbubs sogs du ba 'byin ba ni/_/sbom po yis ni bcom pa ste/_/pha bong sbom pos snad pa bzhin/_/brtsams te khu ba ma phyin nam/_/chags pas dbang po 'jal byed dam/_/gar bsgyur mkha' la bskyed pas sam/_/mnye bshud 
[\[11b.2\]]kyis ni phyung na yang /_/rdzi ldog chu rgyun bzlog phyogs su/_/gtod pa la yang de bzhin 'dod/_/rdzi phyogs rgyun phyogs mthun gtod pas/_/nyes byas 'gyur bar gsungs pa yin/_/chu bsti ba ni mthong sogs la/_/chags pa'i sems dang ldan yang ngo /_/chags pas don nyams rtsa ba 
[\[11b.3\]]ste/_/de bas rtag tu spang bar bya/_/dze ta'i shing sogs reg pas sam/_/brla sogs dag la bshud pa la/_/der ni khu ba byung yang ste/_/rmi lam na ni nyes med cha'o/_/dge slong tshogs la chos lhag par/_/rgyas par yang dag bshad pa rnams/_/dge tshul la ni thams cad du/_/
[\[11b.4\]]nyes byas nges par shes par bya/_/mtha' las byung ba'i nyon mongs ngan/_/kun slong nges par bshags bya ste/_/sbom po lci ba tshogs pa la/_/yang ba'i ming can bzhir brjod do/_/dge 'dun lhag ma las byung ba'i/_/lci ba lnga la yang ba bzhis/_/dge 'dun lhag ma tshogs la 
[\[11b.5\]]ste/_/lhag ma gang zag gcig la'ang rung /_/sngar ni ltung ba byung ba yis/_/ltung ba mthun snang la mi 'dod/_/dri mas dri ma rnam dag pa/_/gang du yang ni rung ma yin/_/gang la dge 'dun lhag mas gos/_/ji srid nges par 'chab byed pa/_/de srid de ni dag bya'i phyir/_/spo ba dag la 
[\[11b.6\]]nges par bzhag_/dge 'dun lhag ma gang byung ba/_/ji srid de ni rjes bzung min/_/de srid de la spo ba yi/_/cho ga yongs su dag phyir bya/_/mtshan mo dang ni sems sbyor las/_/'chab pa yang ni rnam pa gnyis/_/shin tu mi rung 'dir sems pas/_/sems kyi ming du shes par 
[\[11b.7\]]bya/_/'dir ni spo ba la gnas te/_/gal te nyon mongs kyis pham pas/_/rmongs pas slar yang ltung spyod na/_/slar yang dngos gzhi'i spo ba ni/_/gzhan dag kyang ni sbyin bya ste/_/'dul ba legs shes gang dag gis/_/lan gsum bar du de la sbyin/_/snying rje'i zhing ni de 'dra la/_/
[\[12a\]]
[\[12a.1\]]nyon mongs dbang gis 'jug shes pas/_/ngo tsha'i khur ni chen po yis/_/brgya lam zhig na 'dud par 'gyur/_/de lta na yang mi dge las/_/gal te mi ldog pa nyid na/_/de ni mdo bzhin dor bya ste/_/ji srid skyo bar gyur gyi bar/_/yid ni yang dag skyo gyur cing /_/phyir 
[\[12a.2\]]bcos don du gnyer ba na/_/tshogs kyis dbyung bar 'dod pa yis/_/mgu ba spyod pa de la sbyin/_/mgu ba'i chu yis rnam bkrus nas/_/dri ma lhag ma dag bya'i phyir/_/gang du dge slong nyi shu yis/_/de ni nges par dbyung bar bya/_/de la dge 'dun gtso bo ste/_/bsam pa mkhyen pa'i 
[\[12a.3\]]dge 'dun yin/_/dge 'dun gyis ni rjes gnang na/_/las kyi bya ba byed par rung /_/dge 'dun nang du de yi las/_/bya phyir de yi rigs pa yis/_/tshogs las ldang ngo de yi phyir/_/dge 'dun lhag ma zhes byar brjod/_/gal te byin rlabs mgu bas kyang /_/gnas pa'i tshe na shi gyur na/_/
[\[12a.4\]]dbyung ba ma byas na yang ni/_/nges par mtho ris dag tu skye/_/rang gi las kyis co dris pas/_/ngan 'gro dag tu mi skye mod/_/'on kyang snying rje'i bdag can gyis/_/btang snyoms mi bya rjes su bzung /_/gang zhig ming ni grags chen dang /_/mdo sde la sogs mang du thos/_/de dag 
[\[12a.5\]]gcig gi mdun du yang /_/bshags pas dag par nges par rung /_/bsam pa yang ni che ba dang /_/de bzhin ngo tshas bcabs pa yang /_/de yi spo bar yongs brtags byed/_/de yi sgo nas cho ga bzhin/_/de ni mthol ba lan gsum yin/_/nad kyis chad pa nyid dang ni/_/gos kyis 
[\[12a.6\]]dkris dang nyams pa'i sgor/_/byed na ltung ba che spangs nas/_/sbom po'i ltung bas 'joms par 'gyur/_/rkang pa nas ni spyi bo'i bar/_/chags bcas bud med yan lag la/_/gos kyis ma chod reg na ltung /_/chod pa la reg sbom po nyid/_/shing rta gru sogs la 'jug dang /_/
[\[12a.7\]]'phul ba dang ni 'then pa dang /_/bar bcas bar med pa la ni/_/reg pa bdag byed sngar bzhin des/_/bud med kyis tshur dge slong la/_/reg cing chags sems bdag byed na/_/'dir yang sngar bzhin thams cad du/_/'dren pa la sogs byed kyang ngo /_/'khrig pa'i bsam pas 
[\[12b\]]
[\[12b.1\]]'di dag la/_/gnyen po stobs chung byed na ni/_/de la phas pham [(]kyis [,]gyis [)]bslang ba'i/_/sbom po yi ni ltung bar 'gyur/_/'di dag bud med bsten bzod de/_/gzhan du sbom po'i ming can yin/_/skyes pa'am ma ning la sogs sam/_/dud 'gro mo la nyes byas yin/_/dge slong gang 
[\[12b.2\]]zhig bud med la/_/rtse phyir yang ni smad tshig smra/_/dge slong gzugs brnyan nyid yin te/_/de yang dge 'dun lhag ma yin/_/'ga' zhig rma yi sgo gsum po/_/legs par 'dod la shin tu 'jam/_/yang na de dag legs par ni/_/ma dod dga' ba mi skyed gnas/_/'di dang 'di ni 
[\[12b.3\]]khyod la sbyin/_/gang khyod khyo ni nor ldan te/_/gang 'dir khyod dang lhan cig tu/_/bde myong de ni rnyed pa che/_/de la sogs pa'i 'khrig tshig dag_/gang brjod dag 'dun lhag ma'o/_/'khrig dngos sgra ni mi brjod na/_/sbom po yi ni ltung bar gsungs/_/bud med ngo tsha las 'das 
[\[12b.4\]]tshig_/tshul smra'ang de la ci rigs nyid/_/gang zhig 'khrig pa'i tshig gyur pa/_/de ni smra bar mi bya'o/_/smyos pa dang ni sems 'khrugs dang /_/smra mi shes dang las dang po/_/tshor bas gzir la brjod ngan gyi/_/ltung ba dag ni mi 'byung ngo //_//bam po gnyis pa/_
[\[12b.5\]]rang nyid bsngags pa'i bye brag dag_/'khrig pa don gnyer bud med kyi/_/mdun du rang nyid kyis bsngags na/_/lhag ma'o dge 'dun gyi zad byed/_/longs spyod 'di yis nyer gnas na/_/mchog dang phul sogs nges bstan pa/_/sring mo bsnyen bkur mchog tshig smra/_/gsol 
[\[12b.6\]]ba la sogs las kyis ni/_/tshul khrims ldan par gang bshad pa/_/tshul khrims phung por yang dag ldan/_/dge ba'i chos can nyid kyis na/_/ting 'dzin phung por yang dag ldan/_/tshangs par spyod pa nges brjod pas/_/shes rab phung por yang dag ldan/_/'khrig pa'i 
[\[12b.7\]]tshig ces bya ba 'dir/_/gnyis kyis gnyis sprad sbyor ba yin/_/'di dag nang nas tshig gcig kyang /_/sdom brtson chags pa'i sems kyis ni/_/brda sprad bud med la smra na/_/ltung ba yin la dpyad mi dgos/_/'khrig pa gsal bar byed pa'i tshig_/gal te bud med kyang ni smra/_/
[\[13a\]]
[\[13a.1\]]gang 'dir tshangs par spyod rnams la/_/'khrig pa longs spyod mchog ces dang /_/khyed lta'i tshul khrims ldan pa ni/_/rtag tu dge ba'i chos can de/_/yon gnas khyad par 'phags pa dag_/rtsod pa'i dus na rnyed par dka'/_/de la sogs brjod dge slong 
[\[13a.2\]]gang /_/dga' bas dang du len byed cing /_/[(]len [,]lan [)]gyi tshig kyang smra byed na/_/dge 'dun lhag mar gsungs pa yin/_/da lta bu dang 'khrig spyad na/_/bud med rnams kyang 'bras bu che/_/'khrig pas dngos ni mi brjod kyang /_/gang de sbom po'i ming can no/_/de bzhin 'khrig pa'i chos kyis 
[\[13a.3\]]ni/_/dge slong zhes sogs snga ma bzhin/_/[(]da [,]de [)]lta bu zhes mi brjod na/_/'di yang sbom po nyid du gsungs/_/bud med kyis kyang de bzhin du/_/'khrig pa'i tshig dngos dor gnas kyang /_/khyed lta bu zhes mi brjod na/_/de bzhin gnyi ga sbom po yin/_/bsten mi shes la 'khrig tshig 
[\[13a.4\]]dngos/_/brjod na sbom po las ni dman/_/de lta bu zhes bya ba'i sgra/_/med pa la yang nyes byas 'dod/_/thams cad du yang sdom brtson ni/_/chags can la ni ltung bar 'gyur/_/'dir ni chags can zhes gsungs gang /_/sems ni chags pa dang 'brel nyid/_/gang bsten bzod la ltung 
[\[13a.5\]]ba ste/_/bsten mi bzod la sbom po yin/_/skyes pa ma ning la sogs dang /_/dud 'gro mo la nyes byas nyid/_/rang ngam gzhan byed 'jug kyang rung /_/bud med skyes pa phrad na ni/_/dge 'dun lhag ma'i ral gri yis/_/rang gi mgo ni gcod par byed/_/chu tshigs byin sogs mdor 
[\[13a.6\]]bsdu na/_/chung ma rnam pa bdun du brjod/_/mdza' na mo bcu de dag gi_/mtshan nyid yang dag bshad par bya/_/chu yis rab tu byin pa yi/_/don la de ming rab tu btags/_/bud med gang zhig nor gyis nyos/_/rin chas nyos zhes de la rig_/rgyal po'i rgyal mtshan dang ni mgos/_/
[\[13a.7\]]phrogs pa btsan phrogs zhes su brjod/_/ngas khyod nye gnas bya'o zhes/_/zer ba dang sam 'dus pa yin/_/zas dang gos kyi rgyu mtshan gyis/_/zhugs pa gos zas kyis 'tsho yin/_/gang khyod nor dang nga yi nor/_/de yi mnyam srog 'tsho bya zhes/_/de skad brjod nas zhugs pa gang /_/
[\[13b\]]
[\[13b.1\]]kha dum pa zhes bya ba'o/_/skad cig tsam zhig rtag tu min/_/de la thang 'ga' 'phrad ces bya/_/rnam pa bdun po 'di dag ni/_/slar yang so so'i dbye bar 'dod/_/'thabs pa'i mdo dang 'thabs pa'i 'og_/dum pa bcad pa rnam pa gsum/_/rde'u gsum ni gtor ba dang /_/chung ma ma yin par 
[\[13b.2\]]ni bsgrags/_/cho ga rab tu gtang ba dang /_/de bzhin dril bu bsgrags pa'o/_/dang po rnam pa gsum po la/_/bye ba nye bar sdum byed na/_/gcig gnyis gsum la rim pa bzhin/_/nyes byas sogs par 'gyur ba yin/_/bzhi pa dang ni lnga pa gnyis/_/drug ste gsum la sbom po yin/_/
[\[13b.3\]]bdun pa bsdums byas brtul zhugs can/_/dge 'dun gyi ni lhag ma thob/_/'di ru mdza' mor 'dod pa ni/_/khyo ni shi'am gzhan song tshe/_/ma yi 'bad pas bsrungs pa ni/_/ma yis bsrungs zhes brjod pa yin/_/de bzhin pha dang gyos sgyug dang /_/pha dang ma yi rang rig skyes/_/des bsrungs 
[\[13b.4\]]de ni slar yang ni/_/nye dus bsrungs zhes brjod pa yin/_/pha dang khyo ni med pa na/_/ming pos yongs su bsrungs pa'am/_/phu mo nu mo yis kyang bsrungs/_/spun zlas bsrungs zhes brjod pa yin/_/bram ze mo 'am rje rigs mos/_/bsrungs pa rigs kyis bsrungs pa yin/_/bad sa la'am 
[\[13b.5\]]ko sa la'i/_/rgyud kyis bsrungs pa rus chags bsrungs/_/chos spyod brtul zhugs can dag gi_/'khrug med mar ni shes bya ste/_/rnam pa bcu po 'di dag ni/_/mdza' na mo zhes shes par bya/_/gang 'dir mdza' na mor gsungs dang /_/bzhi pa la sogs chung ma rnams/_/thams cad du ni sdum 
[\[13b.6\]]byed pa/_/dge 'dun lhag mar nges par bstan/_/khye'u 'am ni bu mo 'di/_/ci yi phyir na khyim mi byed/_/de skad la sogs smra ba yi/_/dge slong gis ni nyes byas 'thob/_/rang gi don du khang pa ni/_/gsol ba la sogs las kyi ni/_/ched sogs yon tan ldan par bya/_/gzhan du 
[\[13b.7\]]dge 'dun lhag mar 'gyur/_/gang du nyam nga med spyod pa/_/spyod lam rnam bzhi rung ba ni/_/'dug dang 'chag dang nyal bar grags/_/bzhi pa langs pa nyid yin no/_/chur ni bde bar gshegs pa yi/_/mtho ni bcu dang gnyis yin no/_/zheng du de nyid bdun yin te/_/de dag de yi tshad 
[\[14a\]]
[\[14a.1\]]du gsungs/_/bde bar gshegs pa'i mtho yi tshad/_/gang gsungs mkhas pas shes par bya/_/'bring tshad mi yi khru dag ni/_/phyed dang gnyis ni yod pa yin/_/'bring tshad skyes bu yi ni khru/_/bco brgyad chur ni yod pa yin/_/zheng du phyed dang bcu gcig ni/_/
[\[14a.2\]]khang pa'i tshad du gsungs pa yin/_/sbrul dang sdig pa tre bu ka/_/tre la ta sogs kyis mi rung /_/rtsod bcas lam dang shing drung dang /_/lha rten rgyal po'i pho brang mtha'/_/chu dang g.yang sa khron pa'i drung /_/brtsam du rung ba ma yin no/_/skyon ni 'di dag med pa ru/_/rigs pa yi ni khang 
[\[14a.3\]]pa bya/_/bka' yis ma gnang ba dag dang /_/gzhi mi rung dang brtsam mi rung /_/ma brtan rtsod pa dang bcas su/_/byed na dge slong sbom ltung thob/_/skyon kun gyis ni rab smad par/_/dge 'dun lhag mar gyur pa ste/_/skyon rnams kun dang bral ba yi/_/sa gzhir rjes su bsngags 
[\[14a.4\]]pa nyid/_/las dang po dang de bzhin smyos/_/'khrugs dang nad kyis gdungs pa yis/_/byed kyang 'di las 'byung ba yi/_/ltung ba yod pa ma yin no/_/gtsug lag khang ni bdag bcas la'ang /_/tshad dang cho ga 'di nyid de/_/chen po nyid kyi tshad du ni/_/nor gyis 'dir ni shes par 
[\[14a.5\]]bya/_/gzhi med pa'am bag tsam gyis/_/sdom brtson nyams par 'dod phyir ni/_/phas pham pa yis smad na ni/_/gnyis ka dge 'dun lhag mar shes/_/gang zhig cung zad bden par smra/_/'di ni bag tsam yang dag grags/_/rgyu mtshan med pa gzhi med yin/_/bag tsam nyid 
[\[14a.6\]]rab bstan pa ni/_/rab dang blo ldan dge slong ma/_/ut+pal mdog can zhes bya byung /_/rdzing bu rab zhi zhes pa'i 'gram/_/gyad nor can yong nye bar 'ongs/_/de dang ha cang mi ring bar/_/sa las skyes dang mdza' bo gnas/_/der ni chu 'thungs 'ongs pa yi/_/ri dwags ri dwags mo spyod 
[\[14a.7\]]mthong /_/de mthong sa las skyes kyis ni/_/mdza' bos mdza' bshes la dris pa/_/mi tshangs spyod dang dge slong dang /_/dge slong ma yang mthong ngam ci/_/de yis mthong ngo zhes smras te/_/bag tsam 'di tsam las byung bas/_/gyad nor bdag gis brlag bya zhes/_/de yis khros nas smras 
[\[14b\]]
[\[14b.1\]]par zad/_/de la sogs pas kun bslang ba'i/_/bag tsam gyis ni bye brag dag_/yid gcugs dman pa dag gis ni/_/dran par bya phyir gsungs pa yin/_/bzhams te bsgo sogs cho ga yis/_/lan gsum bar du bzlog na yang /_/mi nyan dge 'dun mthun 'byed na/_/dge 'dun gyi ni 
[\[14b.2\]]lhag ma thob/_/gsol ba ma gtogs bzlog pa'i tshig_/bzhams te bsgo ba zhes bya ste/_/tshe ldan khyod 'dir mchog gcig la/_/'di ma byed ces rgyas par ni/_/gzhams te bsgo bas mi gtong na/_/las kyis de la bka' bsgo bya/_/de nas gsol dang bzhi las te/_/byis gang de yi 
[\[14b.3\]]grogs byed pa/_/mchog gcig bsam pa gcig pa las/_/dbye ba rnam pa gnyis su gnas/_/tha dad dbye ba yang ni 'dir/_/rnam pa bcu bzhi dag tu bzhed/_/chos la chos ma yin brjod dang /_/chos min la yang chos su brjod/_/'dul ba dang ni 'dul min nyid/_/bzhi po de la 
[\[14b.4\]]sogs pa brjod/_/'di la dbang byed mkhas pa yis/_/'gyed pa dang ni mi gdams dang /_/ltung ba dang ni bya ba ste/_/rnam pa bzhir ni shes par bya/_/sna tshogs rtsod dang tshogs pa med/_/dge 'dun bsam pa tha dad pa'i/_/rkyen la sogs pas 'byung ba ste/_/rtsod pa yi ni dbang byas 
[\[14b.5\]]brjod/_/kun nas nyon mongs pas ltung ba/_/phan tshun du ni mi gdams pa/_/de ni rtsod pa las byung ba/_/mi gdams pa zhes bya bar grags/_/lus dang ngag yid kyis phye ba/_/de ltar tha dad gcig dang ni/_/rnam pa gnyis dang rnam pa gsum/_/ltung ba phyogs tsam brjod pa yin/_/khyim 
[\[14b.6\]]gcig dge slong bud med dang /_/gal te mi shes lhan cig nyal/_/nub gsum bsnyen par ma rdzogs dang /_/gnas gcig 'di ni lus kyi'o/_/bud med dag la rkang pa ni/_/lnga'am drug gis chos ston na/_/ma bsams par ni lhag pa'i tshig_/smras pa la ni ngag gir brjod/_/
[\[14b.7\]]gso sbyong dus tshe dris na yang /_/skyon yod smra bar mi byed na/_/de la yid kyi ltung ba ni/_/nyes byas nyid du mkhas pas rtogs/_/lus dang yid gnyis 'brel pa ni/_/yang dag bsams nas srog gcod dang /_/ma byin len dang chang 'thung sogs/_/brten pa nyid la'ang 'dod 
[\[15a\]]
[\[15a.1\]]pa yin/_/yang dag bsams nas lnga dang ni/_/drug tsam smras pa lhag par brjod/_/ngag dang yid las gyur pa de/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]smra sogs la'ang 'dod pa yin/_/brdeg ces kun nas bsams nas ni/_/brjod de lus can la brdeg na/_/'di ni lus dang ngag 
[\[15a.2\]]yid kyi/_/bya ba rnam pa gsum pa can/_/dge slong la ni mi dge bas/_/ltung bar 'gyur gyi sems dag na/_/mchod rten sku la skyes pa yi/_/rtswa sngon gcod par byed bzhin du/_/ji ltar dge ba'i sems kyis ni/_/ston pa la ni mchod 'dod pas/_/me tog phreng ba spel ba'i phyir/_/
[\[15a.3\]]'thog pa de la de tsam min/_/ma bsams thub pa'i bslab pa'am/_/dge 'dun nang khrims dral gyur na/_/lung ma bstan par gsungs pa ste/_/ltung med dge ba nam yang min/_/yang dag bsams nas 'das na ni/_/mi dge ba nyid kho nar 'gyur/_/'di ni rjes su ma gnang 
[\[15a.4\]]yang /_/skabs 'ongs zhar la brjod pa yin/_/de ltar rnam pa du ma yi/_/ltung ba so so'i sgo nas 'dir/_/rtsod pa'i dbang du byas pa yi/_/ltung ba 'di ni mkhas pas gsungs/_/gsol ba la sogs las 'di dag_/rung ba min zhes smra ba rnams/_/rtsod pa dang ni 'thab mo yang /_/
[\[15a.5\]]bya ba'i yang ni rgyu ru 'dod/_/dge 'dun 'byed par gang brtson pa'i/_/blo chung gis ni nyes byas thob/_/bzhams te bsgos kyang mi gtong na/_/nyes byas nyid du rnam par gzhag_/dang po nyid du gsol ba'i las/_/brjod tshe mi gtong sbom po nyid/_/brjod pa gnyis la'ang de yin te/_/mthar 
[\[15a.6\]]ni dge 'dun lhag ma nyid/_/snga ma'i brjod pa gnyis la ni/_/sbom po'o de rjes de la ni/_/gsum pa dge 'dun lhag ma ste/_/de ltar 'di ni legs par brtag_/gang yang de rjes 'brang ba yi/_/dge slong sdig to can rnams kyang /_/bzhams te bsgo sogs cho ga yi/_/sngar bzhin 'di dag 
[\[15a.7\]]kun la'ang 'gyur/_/khyim sun 'byin pa yis ni gzhan/_/sun 'byin pa ni rnam gnyis gsungs/_/gcig ni 'grogs pas yin te gzhan/_/gnyis pa yang ni longs spyod kyis/_/'grogs pa bud med lhan cig tu/_/rtse 'jo 'phyar g.yeng la sogs bsten/_/longs spyod me tog 
[\[15b\]]
[\[15b.1\]]'bras bu dang /_/bza' btung la sogs lhan cig byed/_/mig rna yid sogs shes pa yi/_/mthong ba la sogs rnam gsum 'dod/_/bzhams bsgo la sogs rim byas kyang /_/mi gtong sngar bzhin nyes sogs lta/_/bka' blo mi bde bas kyang ni/_/dge 'dun lhag ma'i ltung bar 
[\[15b.2\]]bstan/_/bka' blo mi bde phan rnams la/_/brjod rung ma yin tshig smra'o/_/phas pham la sogs gang zhig bslab/_/de ni bslab pa'i gzhi zhes bya/_/nyar bstan 'dul bar gang gsungs pa/_/'di la chos ldan dge slong rnams/_/gang zhig ston pa'i bka' la gtogs/_/chos mthun 
[\[15b.3\]]rnams kyis nges par bsgo/_/bzhams te bsgo ba la sogs la/_/mi gtong sngar bzhin nyes bya sogs/_/bcu gsum 'di dag dge 'dun las/_/ldang phyir dge 'dun lhag ma'o/_/phyi ma bzhi ni gsum gyis te/_/snga ma dgu ni cig car du/_/mtshon pa tsam du sbyar ba yis/_/dge 
[\[15b.4\]]'dun lhag mar nges par bstan/_/ma nges pa yi chos gnyis ni/_/'dul ba las gsungs rab tu brjod/_/dge slong 'char kas sbeng ma dang /_/lhan cig nyer gnas reg pa las/_/mgon pos dge 'dun tshogs dbus su/_/ma nges dang po'i chos bcas mdzad/_/dben gnas dge 
[\[15b.5\]]slong gcig pu zhig_/bud med gcig dang 'dug byed na/_/'dod bsten rung ngam mi rung yang /_/ma nges gnyis po'i chos su brjod/_/gang du gzhan med 'di dben te/_/skyabs yod kyis kyang rnam pa ni/_/rtsig pa rib ma mun pa dang /_/yol ba thibs po zhes bya lnga/_/gnas su 
[\[15b.6\]]'gro dang 'dug pa'am/_/nyal ba la sogs gang byas kyang /_/dad pa can gyis tshig gis ni/_/dge slong de ni ldang bar 'os/_/yang na de dag 'gro la sogs/_/cung zad tsam yang ma gtogs na/_/de yi ngo bo shes bya'i phyir/_/gsol ba'i cho ga sngon 'gros byin/_/rab tu byung 
[\[15b.7\]]dang bsnyen rdzogs dang /_/de yis gzhan la nam yang ni/_/gnas bca' ba yang mi sbyin te/_/gzhan dang lhan cig 'chag pa'ang min/_/'dug stan la sogs gcig mi bya/_/de la chang 'di ma shi'i bar/_/btung ba yongs su ma chod phyir/_/'di dag ma nges chos zhes brjod/_/de 
[\[16a\]]
[\[16a.1\]]ltar ma nges chos gsungs nas/_/de las ji ltar ldang ba dang /_/gang zag lhag ma mang po yi/_/mdun du rab gnas mchog gis so/_/zhag bcu 'das par dge slong gis/_/chos gos ma brtags 'chang na ni/_/spang ba'i ltung byed sa rdul gyis/_/mgo bo 
[\[16a.2\]]g.yogs par byed pa yin/_/chos gos drubs dang tshon bsgyur ba/_/chos gos zin par gyur pa na/_/chos gos 'dzin par khas len pa/_/'dir ni blo yis khas blang ngo /_/mu bzhi can la 'dir brtag pa/_/sra brkyang dbyung ba byas nas ni/_/chos gos ma zin par ni min/_/de la sogs 
[\[16a.3\]]pas rnam par gzhag_/bal gos sha na nyi 'og dang /_/ras gos dang ni zar ma dang /_/ko tam pa dang du gu la/_/chos gos rgyu ni rnam pa bdun/_/spang bar 'os pa mi spong dang /_/ltung ba bshags par mi byed na/_/gal te phran tshegs de 'dra yang /_/nub gcig bral bar mi byed 
[\[16a.4\]]pa/_/rigs tshigs gsum po 'di las ni/_/gcig ma tshang bar ma yin te/_/'di dag ma lus byas na ni/_/ltung ba med par gsungs pa yin/_/gang yang lhung bzed dra ba sogs/_/nye bar bsags nas der yang ni/_/de tshe gzhan skyel bshags mi byed/_/spang bya'i ltung ba 
[\[16a.5\]]nyid du 'gyur/_/gang 'dir zad byed ltung ba ni/_/nges par spangs nas bshags bya ste/_/gang phyir gang gis ngan song du/_/ltung phyir ltung ba'i ming du btags/_/chos gos gsum gyi gnas bya ste/_/phyi rol 'di dag med par min/_/gzhan du dge slong nyes byas 'gyur/_/dge 'dun gyis 
[\[16a.6\]]gnas ma gtogs so/_/gnas gcig gnyis dang mang po yis/_/grong ni 'obs kyis bskor ba dang /_/rtsig pa rib ma'i khongs su chud/_/nyer spyod gcig dang tha dad pa'o/_/nyer spyod spong ba skyed mos tshal/_/lha yi gnas la sogs pa'o/_/'di la thun mong gcig nyid de/_/tha dad sna 
[\[16a.7\]]tshogs nyer spyod nyid/_/rgyun srang gcig ni btod pa dag_/gnas gcig pa zhes brjod bya ste/_/gdol pa ri khrod pa la sogs/_/rigs gcig pa yi gnas bzhin no/_/rigs gnyis gang du bram ze dang /_/gdol ba gnas pa gnas gnyis brjod/_/sna tshogs rgyun srang btod pa 
[\[16b\]]
[\[16b.1\]]la/_/mang po bar ni rab tu grags/_/nyer spyod nang gi ma lus dang /_/de yi phyi rol 'dom gang yin/_/khyim bya 'phur chags tsam dag gam/_/gang du 'thag 'tshed la sogs tshad/_/rigs dang tshong khang tshong 'dus dang /_/khang thog g.yul sa mu stegs gnas/_/gru dang shing ljon 
[\[16b.2\]]shing rta dang /_/kun dga' ra gcig sna tshogs spyod/_/spun rnams ma phye ba dang ni/_/skyes bu gcig gi yang ni rung /_/ji ltar rigs par gsungs nyid kyis/_/de yi nyer spyod gcig pa yin/_/'di yi nyer spyod snga ma ltar/_/nyer spyod sna tshogs pa yang ni/_/gang du 'di dag gi ni sgo/_/
[\[16b.3\]]tha dad pa'am thun mong brjod/_/mang po yang ni rigs bzhin du/_/tshong khang la sogs 'dod pa yin/_/nyer spyod tshul ni ma lus pa/_/gcig dang sna tshogs dag tu dbye/_/nyer spyod gcig dang sna tshogs su/_/mu stegs lta bas phye ba ste/_/gang du phyi rol spyod pa yis/_/gnas pa 
[\[16b.4\]]g.yang gzhi can la sogs/_/'khor los spyod pa la sogs khyim/_/thun mong sngags kyi khyim kun te/_/gang du phyi rol rnga zlum dang /_/gling bu la sogs 'grems pa'o/_/phan tshun yal ga ma bral na/_/shing sdong kun spyod gcig yin te/_/phyi rol nye 'khor phyed grib pam/_/gang 
[\[16b.5\]]du thigs pas slebs pa'o/_/gang du nyer spyod gcig pa der/_/chos gos gsum ni nyer bzhag nas/_/gzhan du dge slong nyal gyur kyang /_/ltung ba bskyed par 'gyur ma yin/_/lha rten dag la sogs pa yi/_/thun mong sgo drug dag tu gos/_/sbas nas gzhan du 'dug na yang /_/
[\[16b.6\]]zad byed ltung bar ldan ma yin/_/'dom ni bzhi bcu rtsa dgu'i bar/_/chos gos gsum dang bzhi gcig tu/_/nyer spyod dag tu song ba na/_/'dug 'greng 'bral ba ma yin no/_/chos gos yang dag yod na yang /_/gzhan dag bsgrub par 'dod pa yis/_/byin gyis ma brlabs ma 
[\[16b.7\]]brtags par/_/zla ba las lhag gzhan mi bya/_/smin drug nas ni khra yi bar/_/dus kyi gos ni rnam par spyad/_/de las gang lhag dus min te/_/nye du sogs la yid brtan bya/_/dur khrod pa dang mi shes dang /_/shes pa nyid kyis chos gos dang /_/song dang song ba ma yin dang /_/
[\[17a\]]
[\[17a.1\]]phyag dar khrod ni lnga pa yin/_/dur khrod pa ni dur khrod spyod/_/sbyin po mi shes shes pa nyid/_/song ba ro yi gos la sogs/_/phyag dar khrod ni bor ba'o/_/rdul gyis sbags dang dgon pa dang /_/srang bor me yis tshig pa dang /_/mi gtsang gis gos phyag dar 
[\[17a.2\]]khrod/_/rnam pa lngar ni shes par bya/_/me yis tshig dang chu la bor/_/de bzhin pha mas byin pa nyid/_/byi ba'i so yis 'chos pa dang /_/de bzhin be'us zos pa'o/_/gsar pa dang ni longs spyod bzod/_/chos gos rnam pa gnyis su 'dod/_/de yi cho ga ji skad bshad/_/de bzhin 
[\[17a.3\]]'dir ni mngon par brjod/_/gsar pa'i gos la 'di bya ste/_/thog mar snam sbyar nyis rim drub/_/de bzhin du ni gding ba ste/_/chos gos gzhan la nam yang min/_/zla ba bzhi sogs longs spyad pa'i/_/gos de sdom brtson gyis rtogs na/_/snam sbyar la ni bzhi rim ste/_gzhan 
[\[17a.4\]]dag la ni nyis rim bya/_/de las lhag pa mi sbyar te/_/rim pa de la sbyar bya yang /_/bzo 'phror gyur te bzhag pa ni/_/zhag bcu'i bar du rung ba yin/_/snam sbyar rnams kyi tshal bu ni/_/snral zhi bya'o dgu tshogs dgu/_/snam phran nyi shu rtsa lnga'i bar/_/de dag dbye ba nges 
[\[17a.5\]]par bstan/_/glegs bu phyed dang gsum pa gsum/_/gsum ni glegs bu phyed dang bzhi/_/tha ma gsum gsum glegs bu ni/_/phyed dang lnga par bya ba yin/_/sde tshan gsum po 'di dag gis/_/snam sbyar zhes byar gsungs pa ste/_/de dag tshad ni nges bstan pa/_/dang po khru ni 
[\[17a.6\]]lnga dang gsum/_/tha ma phyed dang gsum dang lnga/_/de gnyis bar ni bar ma yin/_/de bzhin bla gos nyid nga ste/_/lnga dang gsum par gsungs pa yin/_/gnyis lnga gnyis ni bzhi pa dang /_/lnga pa yang ni mthang gos yin/_/gal te de dag ma 'byor na/_/rjes bzung don du 'khor 
[\[17a.7\]]gsum khebs/_/spyir na rang bzhin tshad rnams la/_/khru yi tshad du gzhag pa yin/_/shin tu ring dang thung ba la/_/lus kyi tshad du 'dod pa yin/_/dang po tha ma dag dang dbus/_/tshal bu'i sgo ni gsum du brjod/_/de yi sor ni bzhi dang gnyis/_/de yi bar ma rim pa bzhin/_/khru yi tshad 
[\[17b\]]
[\[17b.1\]]las chung ba la/_/byin rlabs yid brtan mi dgos so/_/spu can la yang de bzhin te/_/de gyon grong du 'jug mi bya/_/dge 'dun lhan cig zan mi za/_/mchod rten la yang phyag mi bya/_/'di ni sbyin bdag gi'o zhes/_/yongs su brtags nas bcad par bya/_/ma dras chos gos gyon nas 
[\[17b.2\]]ni/_/grong du 'jug par mi bya ste/_/'jug na nges par dge slong dag_/nyes byas 'gyur te med ma gtogs/_/mi mthun byed dang 'khrug long can/_/mu cor smra dang gtum po dang /_/'gro bar 'dod dang 'gro ba la/_/mkhas pas yid brtan phyir mi gzhag_/gal te yid ches dge slong 
[\[17b.3\]]zhig_/chu yi pha rol gnas na yang /_/de phyir de la chos gos kyi/_/yid brtan bya bar gsungs pa yin/_/gzhan der gnas pa'i dge slong dag_/shi bar shes na chos gos ni/_/slar yang dge slong gzhan zhig gi_/ming gi yid brtan bya ba yin/_/chos gos yid brtan bya ba 
[\[17b.4\]]'dir/_/mi rtag la min mngon sum min/_/de bzhin dge tshul rnams la yang /_/chos gos yid brtan bya ba min/_/gang dag gzhan gyis gtad nas ni/_/chos gos byin gyis brlab pa na/_/da shi na yang dge slong nges/_/dge 'dun la ni dbul bar bya/_/chos gos gang brtags de yi 
[\[17b.5\]]mthar/_/snag tsha la sogs thig le bya/_/bye brag bdag gi mtshan ma yang /_/khyad par rtogs par bya ba nyid/_/bar ma las ni yo byad drug_/de nyid nad g.yog la phyin nas/_/shi ba'i yo byad lhag ma ni/_/dge 'dun bsod nams 'grub phyir bgo/_/chos gos dras pa gsum dang 
[\[17b.6\]]ni/_/rngul gzan sham thabs gnyis gnyis te/_/g.yan pa dgab dang gding ba dang /_/ras chen dang ni skra bzed dang /_/gdong dang lag pa phyi ba ste/_/'di dag bcu gsum dbye bar 'dod/_/chos gos lhag sogs yo byad rnams/_/'tsho ba'i yo byad bcu gsum po/_/dge slong chos gos 
[\[17b.7\]]rnams la ni/_/'chang ba'i tshul ni 'di yin te/_/nges par byin gyis brlab pa dang /_/de las gzhan pa yid brtan bya/_/rngul gzan mthang gos med par ni/_/chos gos chen po longs mi spyod/_/gang phyir longs spyod shes pa yis/_/phal ba yang ni bgo bar bya/_/kha bsgyur 'os pa kha 
[\[18a\]]
[\[18a.1\]]bsgyur zhing /_/drub par 'os pa drub pa dang /_/byin rlabs 'os pa byin gyis brlab/_/brtag par 'os pa yid brtan bya/_/sngon po btsag dang ngur smrig rtsi/_/'di dag rung ba'i tshon gsum ste/_/tshon chen dag gi chos gos ni/_/kha bsgyur byed pa bkag pa yin/_/rgya 
[\[18a.2\]]skyegs le brgan rtsi dang btsod/_/rma shing rtsi dang mthing shing mtshal/_/sin+du ra dang gur gum ste/_/'di dag tshon chen brgyad du 'dod/_/seng ba phrug dang phying pa dang /_/la ba shin tu mthug po dag_/'di dag chos gos 'dod pa yis/_/dra bar bya ba ma yin no/_/spu can rnam par spangs nas 
[\[18a.3\]]ni/_/brtul zhugs can gyi nang shun bya/_/gding ba la ni dra mi dgos/_/rtag tu 'bral ba yang ni med/_/snam sbyar nges par dras te bcang /_/gzhan dag ma nges gang gi phyir/_/'dod chung rang gi chos gos kyi/_/las kyis skyo ba skyed phyir ro/_/lnga pa sham thabs gyon nas ni/_/bya ba mtha' dag 
[\[18a.4\]]byed par rung /_/bla gos sa phyogs gtsang mar ni/_/las yongs byed na bkag pa med/_/snam sbyar dge slong 'dus pa dang /_/zas dang mchod rten phyag byed dang /_/grong sogs 'jug la bgo bya yi/_/gzhan dag tu ni bkag pa yin/_/la ba phrug dang phying ba dang /_/rnga mo'i la ba dag dang 
[\[18a.5\]]ni/_/chos gos nyis rim gyon pa ni/_/mi gtsang phyogs su bkag pa yin/_/ma g.yogs gnas mal mi rung phyir/_/gzhan med bla gos bzhi bltab tu/_/byas te bting nas dran ldan pas/_/ldang ba yang zhing nyal bar bya/_/gzhan du chos gos ma khyer bar/_/gnas na gzhan 
[\[18a.6\]]gyi dkar po'am/_/dras pa ma yin na yang rung /_/byin gyis brlab par bya ba nyid/_/zas la nad pa ma gtogs par/_/gos gcig pa ni bkag pa yin/_/sdom brtson bgo ba med par ni/_/khrus byed bkag go med na rung /_/mang pos blangs na la ba sogs/_/gcig rnyed pa ni kun gyis 
[\[18a.7\]]spyad/_/rjes su gnang ba ma gtogs par/_/gcig pus bdag gir mi bya'o/_/kha ma bsgyur bar nyin mo ni/_/phyi rol ngur smrig nges par g.yogs/_/dge 'dun gos ni dran bzhin du/_/gnyis ka nas g.yogs spyad par bya/_/la ba'i spu ni phyir bstan nas/_/gyon te bcag par mi 
[\[18b\]]
[\[18b.1\]]bya'o/_/srin sogs 'jigs pas 'dug pa dang /_/nyal ba rjes su gnang ba nyid/_/dge 'dun rnams la be'u phrug re/_/rjes su gnang ngo gang zag la'ang /_/par sig la ba dge 'dun la/_/la ba gzhan ni gang zag la'ang /_/rjes gnang kun kyang la ba ste/_/'on kyang par sig yul nas 
[\[18b.2\]]byung /_/de ni par sig la ba'o/_/smad g.yogs bsdams pa'i don du ni/_/ska rags kyang ni rung ba yin/_/lteb ma dang ni shur bu nyid/_/gsum pa shur bu phre'u nyid/_/rang gi bzang po gang gyur pa/_/thams cad dge 'dun nyid phyir spyad/_/gding g.yogs dag ni med par yang /_/
[\[18b.3\]]nam yang mal stan longs mi spyad/_/dag par bya phyir sham thabs ni/_/btso blag mkhan la 'khrur mi gzhug_/brtse ldan rang ngam gzhan gyis kyang /_/snod kyi nang du dal bus bkru/_/btso blag mkhan gyis brtse med par/_/gos dag drag tu sprugs brdabs na/_/tshags sran zad 
[\[18b.4\]]par byed pa'i phyir/_/bstan bcos las ni rab tu bkag_/tshul ldan ji srid legs par ni/_/chos gos la sogs longs spyod pa/_/de srid rgyun mi 'chad par ni/_/sbyin bdag rnams kyi bsod nams 'phel/_/bsrung ba'i don du skra bzed ni/_/zheng du khru ni phyed dang gnyis/_/srid du khru ni 
[\[18b.5\]]gsum yin te/_/lhag pa brtag par mi bya'o/_/gal te chos gos 'tsher bag can/_/gzhan las dge slong gis rnyed na/_/'tsher bag chu yis nyams byas nas/_/yongs su spyad do gzhan du min/_/'tsher bag dang bcas 'jam po yi/_/chos gos bcang bar mi bya ste/_/'chang bar 
[\[18b.6\]]byed na de dag la/_/nges par 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/mdzes mas chos gos 'tsher bag can/_/mdzes dga' la ni nan gyis byin/_/de la kun nas chags skyes pas/_/de phyir bstan bcos che las bkag_/gang des yongs su longs spyad par/_/bzod pa'i gos gzhan yod min na/_/
[\[18b.7\]]rngul gyis 'jigs pas snam sbyar la/_/rngul gzan bltabs nas bcang bya ste/_/zheng du mtho gang srid du khru/_/phyed dang do las lhag mi bya/_/snam sbyar yongs su srung byed pa/_/btsag yug rnams kyis kha bsgyur bya/_/khyad par du yang bla gos kyi/_/tha ma yongs su zegs pa na/_/
[\[19a\]]
[\[19a.1\]]smyung tshems la sogs yang dag sgrub/_/khab kyi las kyang rjes su gnang /_/phyag dar khrod la dga' ches pas/_/bsams nas dral bar mi bya ste/_/nam yang chos gos don du yang /_/rma med ro las mi blang ngo /_/srin bu sogs kyis rma gtod kyang /_/rang 
[\[19a.2\]]gnas zhag bdun bzhag nas ni/_/dur khrod pa yi dge slong gis/_/bkrus te kha bsgyur bcang bar bya/_/dge 'dun gyi ni mal cha dang /_/stan ni de la bkag pa yin/_/dge slong khyim du mi 'jug ste/_/sgo drung bsod snyoms blang bar bya/_/stan sogs 'dug par 'bod pa na/_/dur 
[\[19a.3\]]khrod pa zhes go byas nas/_/gnang phyir khyim du zhugs nas ni/_/stan la 'dug pa la sogs bya/_/mchod rten dag la phyag byed tshe/_/'dom gang nang du dor byas te/_/nya dang sha sogs mi bza' zhing /_/der ni gnas khang brtsig mi bya/_/kha ma bsgyur dang kha tshar can/_/be'u ras 
[\[19a.4\]]sogs dge 'dun rung /_/tshon can rgyal byed tshal phul bas/_/des na be'u ras sogs bkag_/spang bar bya ba'i dngos gang rung /_/de ni dge 'dun la min te/_/gang gzhan phyis ni ster ba yi/_/gzhung lugs shes pa dag la spang /_/mi shes bzhin du chags bcas gang /_/
[\[19a.5\]]spangs nas zlog par mi byed cing /_/legs bsgo byas kyang mi ster na/_/de la stobs kyis dbog par bya/_/'char ka'i khu ba'i thigs pa gang /_/gos la babs mthong sbed ma yi/_/chags khyer bu yi rgyur gyur pas/_/de yis kun bslang 'di rab bcas/_/dge slong gang zhig nye du 
[\[19a.6\]]min/_/dge slong ma la gos 'khru dang /_/'tshed dang 'chag par byed pa ni/_/nges par ltung ba dang ni ldan/_/'khru ba la sogs byed 'jug na/_/gcig gis kyang ni nye bar 'joms/_/the tshom za na nye du la'ang /_/nyes byas dri mar yongs su bsgrags/_/pha dang ma dang ma yi pha/_/nye bar 
[\[19a.7\]]bzung ste bdun brgyud ni/_/mkhyen chen gyis ni nye dur gsungs/_/de las gzhan ni gnyen mi nyid/_/tshos khu dngas ma la sogs pa/_/re re la yang 'og gzhi gding /_/bslab pa'i gzhi ni nyams byed pas/_/dge slong la ni ltung bar 'gyur/_/dge slong gang zhig nye du min/_/dge slong ma 
[\[19b\]]
[\[19b.1\]]las bsams bzhin du/_/chos gos gang rung len byed na/_/de yi bslab gzhi nyams par byas/_/'di la slar sbyin dbrog mi bya/_/brje ba la ni nyes pa med/_/ji ste brje na mnyam pos sam/_/dge slong ma yi 'dod pa bzhin/_/rgya chen nor ldan dge slong mas/_/gal te 'bul na nyes 
[\[19b.2\]]pa med/_/de bzhin legs gsungs bsnyen rdzogs byed/_/bor rlag gyur dang dbus yang ngo /_/dge slong chos gos gsum yod pas/_/nye du ma yin khyim bdag dang /_/de yi bdag mo las kyang rung /_/chos gos nam yang mi blang ngo /_/
[\[19b.3\]]kun du skyes pa khyim bdag ste/_/de yi bdag mor bud med brjod/_/bor rlag byung sogs slong ba ni/_/'di la nyes pa med par brjod/_/dbul po la ni bslangs pa yis/_/kha tshar la sogs thob pa dang /_/gar mkhan la sogs pa yi gos/_/
[\[19b.4\]]bzang po la yang nyes pa med/_/chos gos phrogs pa la sogs kyis/_/mang po rnyed par gyur na yang /_/dran pas stod g.yogs smad g.yogs las/_/lhag par blang bar mi bya'o/_/khru ni bcu gnyis stod g.yogs gos/_/smad g.yogs yang ni bdun dang gnyis/_/
[\[19b.5\]]re zhig 'di ni 'jig rten pa'i/_/stod g.yogs smad g.yogs zhes su gsungs/_/khru ni lnga gsum snam sbyar te/_/nyis rim stod g.yogs dag kyang ngo /_/khru ni lnga dang gnyis po dag/_/re zhig bstan pa'i smad g.yogs so/_/bslangs nas dngos po lhag pa dag_/gal te ga shed 
[\[19b.6\]]nas ni rnyed/_/de ni go byas bzlog byas te/_/rjes su gnang na blang bar bya/_/khyim bdag mo 'am khyim bdag gis/_/gos kyi rin ni spags na der/_/dge slong rnam par rtog bzhin du/_/slong na de ni ltung dang bcas/_/sngar bzhin nye du min zhes bya/_/chos gos rnam pa bdun po 
[\[19b.7\]]dag_/yongs su spyad par bzod pa yis/_/gos de rung ba zhes bya'o/_/rin spags pa zhes bya ba ni/_/gser la sogs pa ma smos shing /_/mgron du ma bos zhes pa ste/_/brtag btung dran par brjod pa'o/_/lnga dang bcu pa'i rin dag gis/_/nyos la byin zhes rjod byed cing /_/
[\[20a\]]
[\[20a.1\]]sngon po sogs kyi kha dog ste/_/khru yi tshad kyis khru nyid do/_/rin la sogs pa gsum dag gis/_/slong ba'i tshe na nyes byas te/_/thob na spang ba'i ltung bar 'gyur/_/ma bslangs na ni ma yin no/_/chos gos phrogs pa la sogs pas/_/gang yang rung 
[\[20a.2\]]ba blang bya ste/_/dge slong rtags kyis lus la 'dir/_/ma g.yogs nam yang mdzes ma yin/_/so sor dpag pa la yang ni/_/thams cad sngar bzhin kho na ste/_/ji ltar byas pas ltung ba dang /_/ltung ba med pa yang ni grags/_/bdag cag mi rung gnas brjod nas/_/pho nya las 
[\[20a.3\]]ni rin mi blang /_/legs sbyin chos gos bdag cag rung /_/zhal ta pho nya de la brjod/_/de yis gang tshe chos gos rin/_/zhal ta byed la gtad pa na/_/dge slong chos gos don gnyer des/_/lan drug bar du dran par bya/_/der ni mngon par grub na ni/_/der ni blang bar rung ba nyid/_/
[\[20a.4\]]de las lhag par brtson byed na/_/ltung ba'i tshang tshing nang du ltung /_/'di la lan drug bskul nas ni/_/rang nyid 'ongs nas ster byed na/_/dge slong gis ni 'di skad brjod/_/bdag gis 'di spangs dgos pa med/_/de lta na yang gal te de/_/shin tu phyag sogs sngon 'gro bas/_/ster na 
[\[20a.5\]]dge slong len pa la/_/ltung ba med par gsungs pa yin/_/bud med dang ni rgyal srid la/_/dbang bskur gyur tam bram ze'am/_/rgyal po'i gnang chen pa la gtogs/_/blon po'am dmag dpon la sogs 'dod/_/'dug sa tshong khang chu gnas dang /_/'bod gnas dang ni khyim gnas so/_/
[\[20a.6\]]tshong khang gang du nyo tshong byed/_/chu gnas chu snod sbed pa'i sa/_/'bod gnas 'breg mkhan dag dang ni/_/rdza mkhan la sogs ming can gyi'o/_/khyim gnas khyim du rab brjod pa/_/'di dag 'bod gnas bzhir gsungs so/_/tshur spyon legs par byon nam khyod/_/gdan 'di la yang 
[\[20a.7\]]bzhugs nas ni/_/phyag zhabs legs par bkra ba dang /_/bza' dang btung ba dgyes bzhin gsol/_/lan drug bskul pa'i sgo der yang /_/ma thogs par ni rjod byed na/_/ltung med thogs nas rjod byed pa/_/ltung bar 'gyur ba kho na'o/_/gal te pho nya mi ma yin/_/sbyin bdag dang 
[\[20b\]]
[\[20b.1\]]ni gnyis pa'ang /_/sdom brtson nyer byas gsum gyi ni/_/dbyug pa rnams kyis mgo bo 'joms/_/sbyin bdag mi yin pho nya dang /_/zhal ta pa ni mi min na/_/nyes byas gnyis so zhal ta tsam/_/mi ma yin na nyes byas gcig_/gang du sbyin bdag pho nya dang /_/zhal ta pa yang mi yin na/_/
[\[20b.2\]]de ni nges par ltung byed bskyed/_/khrims ldan ngan song gsum du 'phen/_/dge slong gang zhig phan byed pas/_/dge slong gang la chos gos bskur/_/de nyid la ni yid brtan pas/_/yongs su spyod pa nyes pa med/_/gang der shi na chos gos ni/_/gsal por de yi 'gyur ba yin/_/ma 
[\[20b.3\]]thob pa las spang ba dag_/gnyis ka la yang med pa ste/_/snga mas yongs su btang ba dang /_/gzhan gyis bdag gir de ma byas/_/de la zhag bcu 'das na yang /_/gnyi ga la yang ltung ba med/_/gang yang dge slong srin bal gyis/_/gsar pa'i stan dag byed na ni/_/de la ltung ba'o de yang 
[\[20b.4\]]gnyis/_/g.yogs pa dang ni blings pa'o/_/byin par gyur dang de bzhin du/_/longs spyad pa la nyes med do/_/sbyin bdag bsod nams 'dod pa'i phyir/_/dge slong gis ni blang bar bya/_/lug bal nag po 'ba' zhig gis/_/gsar pa'i stan ni mi bya ste/_/de tshol ba yi sdug bsngal 
[\[20b.5\]]gyis/_/klog dang kha don nyams par byed/_/dkar nag dang ni 'khob bal gyi/_/cha gnyis cha gcig bsres nas ni/_/blings pa nges par byed na ni/_/stan des ltung ba med pa yin/_/nag po rang bzhin gyis gnag nyid/_/mgrin pa rgyab dang glo gnyis dkar/_/spyi bo lto dang tshe'u 
[\[20b.6\]]'gab/_/'khob bal dkar nag zhes su brjod/_/dkar nag 'khob bal dkar po ni/_/srang phyed tsam gyis nyung ba zhig_/byas nas gsar pa rtsom byed na/_/de la ltung bar the tshom med/_/de las gzhan pa ma rnyed na/_/nag pos kyang ni bya ba yin/_/gzhan las rab tu thob 
[\[20b.7\]]pa ni/_/ci bde bar yang yongs su spyod/_/lo drug bar du nan tan gyis/_/byas pa'i stan de bcang bar bya/_/lo drug tshu rol de byas ltung /_/nyes med tshogs kyis gnang ba yin/_/lo gcig la yang gnyis pa dag_/gal te dge slong rtsom byed na/_/rtsom la nyes byas gcig yin te/_/
[\[21a\]]
[\[21a.1\]]yongs su rdzogs la dngos gzhir gzhag_/de bzhin gnyis gsum bzhi dang lngar/_/brtsams pa la yang nyes byas 'thob/_/lo drug bar du zhugs gyur nas/_/rtsom pa la ni nyes pa med/_/gding ba gsar pa byed pa na/_/rnying pa la yang nges par mtha'/_/
[\[21a.2\]]kha dog ngan par byed 'dod pas/_/bde bar gshegs pa'i mtho gang glan/_/gal te mtho gang tshad de las/_/bsams bzhin cung zad tsam spyod kyang /_/de skabs ltung ba'i dbyug pa yis/_/mgo bo gcog par byed pa yin/_/gal te shin tu zegs pa'am/_/spyad mi bzod cing 
[\[21a.3\]]dengs pa na/_/de tsam nyid dam chung ba gang /_/glon pa la ni nyes pa med/_/sdom brtson rang gis bal dag ni/_/dpag tshad gsum las lhag mi 'khur/_/nyung [(]du [,]ngu [)]zhwa la sogs pa'i phyir/_/mngon par khyer yang nyes pa med/_/grong las rgyang grags phyed du yang /_/sdom 
[\[21a.4\]]brtson khur na nyes byas te/_/grong khyer nas ni grong khyer du/_/rgyang grags gcig khur ltung ba yin/_/dge slong gang zhig dge slong ma/_/nye du min la bal dag ni/_/rmel ba la sogs byed 'jug na/_/de yang bslab las nyams ldan 'gyur/_/rmel ba la sogs re res kyang /_/mtha' 
[\[21a.5\]]dag gis kyang kun nas bskyed/_/sdom brtson ltung ba dang bcas na/_/nyes byed bzhin du 'og tu 'gro/_/bcom ldan brtse bas dge slong dag_/sa le sbram len bkag pa yin/_/chos gos lam rgyags nad pa yi/_/sman gyi rgyur ni ma gtogs so/_/gser dngul mu tig nor bu dang /_/
[\[21a.6\]]mu sal gar b+ha g.yas su 'khyil/_/dmar dang shang ka she la dang /_/byi ru la sogs reg na ltung /_/thub pa'i dge slong don nyung dang /_/bya ba nyung ba mdzes pa ste/_/mang pos dben pa dang ni dga'/_/nyung [(]du [,]ngu [)]tsam zhig bcang bar bya/_/sdom brtson gang zhig nor ldan pas/_/skyed kyi don 
[\[21a.7\]]du gtong byed na/_/skyed thob ltung ba'i rdo rje yis/_/bdag nyid 'joms par byed pa yin/_/sbyor ba dang ni rab sbyor dang /_/bsgrub dang brje ba de bzhin de/_/byed pa mi byed zhes bya dang /_/mngon mtshan can gyi rnam pa bzhi/_/shing rta la sogs snod 'grems shing /_/yul phyogs zong ni 'dri 
[\[21b\]]
[\[21b.1\]]byed pa/_/grags po dag ni 'tshol byed pa/_/'di dag sbyor bar 'dod pa yin/_/rab sbyor bdun sde la sogs pa'i/_/skyed phyir nor ni gtong byed pas/_/dbang po gro ga snag tsha dang /_/smyu gu tshol ba dag la 'dod/_/'dod pas rnyed la chags pa yi/_/nyis 'gyur la sogs spel ba'i phyir/_/
[\[21b.2\]]gro ga la sogs sgrub byed pa/_/de ni bsgrub par gsungs pa yin/_/nor bu mu tig byi ru sogs/_/rdzas rnams yongs su brje ba na/_/snag tsha gro ga la sogs tshol/_/de ni brje bar yongs su grags/_/skyed ma thob kyi bar du 'dir/_/nyes byas dri mar yongs su grags/_/gang tshe 
[\[21b.3\]]skyed thob de nyid tshe/_/gsal por ltung ba nyid du grags/_/byed pa mngon mtshan can la sogs/_/gtong bar brjod do mi byed pa/_/slar yang gser sogs mi gtong ba'o/_/cho ga 'di yang sngar bzhin te/_/dkon mchog gsum gyi ched du ni/_/rnyed pa 'dod pas sbyor ba dag_/'jig 
[\[21b.4\]]rten spyod pas gzhan min brtag_/rigs ldan zhal ta la rung ste/_/rgyal po dang ni rgyal po'i 'khor/_/sbyin bdag rnams kyi drung du ni/_/nam yang sbyor ba mi bya'o/_/'di dag rnams las rnyed med dam/_/'on te dka' bas rnyed kyang rung /_/khyer nas mi ster gta' mi blang /_/khyer 
[\[21b.5\]]yang star nus de lta min/_/nyo byed shes rab can med na/_/gang nyo 'dod pa'i dge slong gis/_/rdzas rnams rjes su brtags nas ni/_/nges par lan gsum bar du brjod/_/rnyed pa'i don du mi btsong zhing /_/nam yang nyo na ltung ba nyid/_/dgos pa rjes 'brel dor byas pas/_/
[\[21b.6\]]nyo tshong la ni nyes med de/_/sangs rgyas chos dang dge 'dun phyir/_/'jig rten chos lugs rjes 'brang ba'i/_/zhal ta pa yis nyo tshong bya/_/gzhan du gzhan gyi ma yin no/_/mchod phyir gus pas rtag tu ni/_/gtsug lag khang du tshong byed na/_/de la skyed dang bcas sbyin te/_/sbyin 
[\[21b.7\]]bdag dad pa skyed phyir ro/_/so ba til dang mud ga sogs/_/srog chags dang bcas ma brtags pa/_/bsil grib kyi ni sa phyogs su/_/de ni rab tu bkram ste bzhag_/de lta na yang de nas de/_/gal te bral bar ma gyur na/_/de tshe slar yang gnas khang du/_/bzhag nas sgo 
[\[22a\]]
[\[22a.1\]]ni rtag tu bsdams/_/zhag bcu las 'das lhung bzed ni/_/brtul zhugs can gyis bcang mi bya/_/lcags dang sa las byas rang bzhin/_/lhung bzed rnam pa gnyis su brjod/_/tshad ldan chung ngam chen po ni/_/bsnyen par rdzogs pa'i don phyir yang /_/
[\[22a.2\]]mang po yin na yang ni rung /_/'chang ba la ni smad pa med/_/lhan pa gcig gnyis gsum dang bzhis/_/lhung bzed gzhan dag bslang mi rung /_/dge ba'i sems kyis bag yod par/_/rin gyis nyos la nyes pa med/_/sdom brtson mi brtan ldan pas ni/_/lhung bzed bug rdol 
[\[22a.3\]]glan mi bya/_/rgya skyegs tshig ma tshon mo steng /_/spra tshil bu ram sas ma yin/_/brtan po lhan [{]rings [,]ring [}]gang brjod dang /_/phye ma yang na lhan pa nyid/_/tham phran dang ni lhan thabs nyid/_/de bzhin chu srin so yang ngo /_/chos kyis yongs su brje ba la/_/nyes pa med do gzhan du 
[\[22a.4\]]slar/_/slong ba'i tshe na nyes byas te/_/thob pa na ni spang bar 'gyur/_/grub na dge bas lhung bzed de/_/dge 'dun la ni brim par bya/_/de yang lhung bzed tha ma ni/_/de yi lung bzhin du ni [(]bcong [,]btsong [)]/_/bre chung do zan tshod mar bcas/_/blugs nas bzhag pa de la ni/_/lag 
[\[22a.5\]]pas bkan tshe mi reg pa/_/de ni chen po'i tshad du brjod/_/bre chung gang zan tshod mar bcas/_/shong ba chung ngur rab tu grags/_/'di dag gnyis kyi bar dag ni/_/'bring por brjod de lhung bzed gsum/_/gang zhig lhung bzed gas dang rdol/_/de ni gus pas glan par bya/_/de byed pa
