[\[22a.6\]]yang tshog chas kyang /_/dge 'dun gnas khang gzhag par bya/_/'gro na lhung bzed bcas yin te/_/gal te lhung bzed med nyid dam/_/gzhan du rkun pos 'jigs pa'am/_/slar log 'dod pa yin na rung /_/thags mkhan nye du ma yin la/_/gla rngan med par 'thag 'jug na/_/
[\[22a.7\]]ltung bcas ltung ba med pa ni/_/nye du yin nam gla byin na'o/_/nges par dge slong ched du ni/_/khyim bdag chos gos 'thag 'jug na/_/de yis der song thags mkhan la/_/snyan tshig bsngags te grub na ltung /_/zheng che thar tshags bzang sogs don/_/bsod snyoms kyis ni 'dod mi 
[\[22b\]]
[\[22b.1\]]bskyed/_/de ltar byed na sdom brtson la/_/thob pa'i tshe na spang bar 'gyur/_/zheng che ba ni tshad chen te/_/mnyen 'gyur 'jam po zhes bya'o/_/thar gyis zhag pa 'jur mdud med/_/tshags thar bzang ba dam pa yin/_/bsod snyoms kyang ni 'dir shes bya/_/bza' sogs lha yi rigs las 
[\[22b.2\]]byung /_/ston mo lha yi rigs dag ni/_/de yi rgyags su rnam par shes/_/chos gos dge slong la byin nas/_/gzhan du khro dang chags pa yis/_/de phrogs na ni nyes byas te/_/phan par 'dod pas nyes med do/_/lus kyi rab g.yo lus kyis ni/_/byed pa'o ngag gi g.yo ba yang /_/brjod 
[\[22b.3\]]pa yis ni 'phrog byed na/_/gnyis sam gcig gis kyang ni rung /_/ji srid chos gos gra yang ni/_/lus dang bral bar ma gyur pa/_/de srid nyes byas tsam yin te/_/bral na spang ba'i ltung ba yin/_/dbyar ni dbyar gyi rnyed pa nyid/_/rab tu 'gyed par byed na ni/_/nyes byas 'di ni 'das 
[\[22b.4\]]nas sam/_/de ma yin la ma yin no/_/dbyar gang bdag gir byed mi 'dod/_/chos gos sbed pa med par ni/_/chos gos gtad pa las byung ba'am/_/dbyig la sogs pa rung ba yin/_/'chi phyir dang ni 'chi bas gtad/_/nad pas nad pa nyid phyir ro/_/'gro bar chas pas gang 
[\[22b.5\]]byin pa/_/'di dag gtad pa las byung lnga/_/mtha' yi zhag bcu'i nang du ni/_/bgos kyang rung ba nyid yin te/_/sbyin pa'i dus las 'das par ni/_/nam yang bcangs na dngos gzhi'o/_/chos gos byin pa'i dus gang ni/_/mkhas pas yang dag bstan pa 'dir/_/dgag dbye bya ba'i 
[\[22b.6\]]nang rol du/_/zhag bcu'o de las lhag pa min/_/'dir bdag rang gi lag nyid kyis/_/rab tu dbul zhes sbyin bdag la/_/gal te 'jog par byed na ni/_/nam yang dge slong skyon yod min/_/dgag dbye'i dus kyi rnyed pa gang /_/thams cad dbyar gnas gang de dbang /_/gang zhig 
[\[22b.7\]]dbyar gnas ma bcas pa'am/_/gang dang lhan cig dgag dbye byas/_/'jigs bcas dgon par gnas pa yis/_/chos gos gsum po dag las ni/_/gcig ni khyim du bzhag bya ste/_/bag yod dge ba 'dod pas so/_/dgos pa'i ched du gzhan du ni/_/zhag drug 'das kyang 
[\[23a\]]
[\[23a.1\]]nyes med de/_/der yang mtshams gcig chos gos kyis/_/bdun pa'i skya rengs shar na ltung /_/dbyar gyi nang du zla shol thob/_/dbyar gnas phyi mar rab tu grags/_/'jigs bcas zhes brjod gang du 'dir/_/nges par rkun po'i 'jigs bcas 'dod/_/seng ge 
[\[23a.2\]]stag sogs 'jigs pa ni/_/dogs bcas 'dod par shes par bya/_/sha sbrang la sogs 'jigs pa dag_/'jigs pa tha dad yin par gsungs/_/so ga'i zla ba lhag ma la/_/khrus don lus rab sba ba'i phyir/_/dbyar gos ras chen ming can gyi/_/chos gos brtul zhugs 
[\[23a.3\]]can gyis btsal/_/'di ni dbyar zla gsum dag las/_/zla phyed bar du bcang bya ste/_/snga bar tshol lam lhag par ni/_/'chang ba la yang ltung bar 'dod/_/dgag dbye'i dus kyi gos bzang ba/_/sra brkyang gding ba'i don du ni/_/tshogs la gsol ba sngon 'gro bas/_/las byed 
[\[23a.4\]]pa la yang dag bsgo/_/tshogs kyis yang dag gnang nas ni/_/'phags pa'i tshogs kyis gus byed la/_/gsol ba rkyang pa nyid kyis gtang /_/de yis ji ltar rigs par ni/_/kha bsgyur la sogs byas nas ni/_/song nas tshogs kyi dbus su zhu/_/de nas cho ga bzhin du 'dir/_/
[\[23a.5\]]gding bya sra brkyang bting ba yi/_/dge slong rnams la phan yon mang /_/phan bzhed ston pas yang dag gsungs/_/zhag bcu zla ba nges pa dang /_/ma brtags par ni 'chang ba dang /_/snam sbyar med par grong sogs 'jug_/yang yang za ba nyid dang ni/_/'dus shing za ba nyes pa med/_/
[\[23a.6\]]bsgo ba ma byas par yang 'gro/_/smin drug dang ni khra yi bar/_/sra brkyang gding ba dus su gsungs/_/mgu ba spyad dang mgu ba byas/_/spo ba dang ni de las langs/_/dbyar ma rdzogs dang dbyar bral dang /_/dbyar ni phyi mar gnas de bzhin/_/lkog tu gyur pa'i dge tshul dang /_/bslab 
[\[23a.7\]]pa spyod par byed pa ste/_/'di dag la ni phan yon med/_/rnyed pa kho na ma bkag go/_/lta dang tshul khrims dri ma can/_/gang zhig dge 'dun gyis rab spangs/_/chos ma yin pa'i phyogs lhung dang /_/de bzhin gzhan du dbyar gnas te/_/lnga po 'di la rnyed pa dang /_/phan yon 
[\[23b\]]
[\[23b.1\]]yang ni med par gsungs/_/'dir ni bzang dang mkhas kun la/_/yon tan dang ni rnyed pa yod/_/dge 'dun la bsngos rnyed pa gang /_/brtul zhugs can dag dbang byed na/_/spong ba'i ltung byed lcags tshan gyis/_/sdug bsngal nyams su myong bar 'gyur/_/rnyed pa gos dang dbyig 
[\[23b.2\]]la sogs/_/bza' ba la sogs zang zing yang /_/gnyis ka'ang bsngos par bsgyur ba ni/_/rab tu bkag pa gsungs pa yin/_/tshogs kyi gzhan la gzhan gyi der/_/rnyed pa phan tshun sgyur byed na/_/nyes byas dang ni yang dag ldan/_/dngos gzhi ru ni mi brtag go/_/dge 'dun mthun 
[\[23b.3\]]pa nyid kyi ni/_/dge 'dun gzhan la bsngos pa yi/_/rnyed pa bdag gir slong byed na/_/thob pa'i tshe na spong bar 'gyur/_/gang zag sku gzugs mchod rten dang /_/dud 'gro rtswa dang nam mkha' la/_/bzhag sogs sa la ka ba ste/_/khang [{]bzangs so[,]bzang ngo [}]gas la nyes byas yin//_//
[\[23b.4\]]bam po gsum pa/_dang por byin len byas nas ni/_/mar sogs dge slong byin gyis brlabs/_/rang gis blangs nas spyad bya ste/_/zhag bdun de las lhag par min/_/sdom brtson de don la mkhas pa/_/spang bar 'os pa spangs nas ni/_/de nas ltung de las ldang phyir/_/nub kyis chod nas 
[\[23b.5\]]bshags pa bya/_/mkhas pas bskul ba byas nas ni/_/gnyis pa la ni blang bar bya/_/ji ltar gsungs pa'i brtag pa dag_/mi byed pa las ltung bar 'gyur/_/ltung ba bcos pa'i don du ni/_/bdag gi don du chos gos sogs/_/cung zad tsam yang de dag gis/_/gzhan las blang bar 
[\[23b.6\]]mi bya'o/_/ltung 'di spang bar ma byas na/_/gzhan du grol bar 'gyur ba min/_/spang bar yang ni shes bya la/_/chos gos la sogs spang bar brjod/_/spang ba'i ltung byed sum cu po/_/bslab bya dang bcas bshad nas ni/_/de rjes ltung byed 'ba' zhig pa/_/dgu bcu rab tu gsal bar bya/_/rgyal 
[\[23b.7\]]po'i khab tu bcom ldan 'das/_/blta 'dod khyim pa mang rnams kyis/_/bcom ldan 'das nyid gang na zhes/_/sgra gcan 'dzin la dris pa dang /_/gzhan na bcom ldan bzhugs bzhin du/_/gzhan du nye bar bstan las bcas/_/de phyir mdo las gsungs pa gang /_/ston pas tshigs bcad 
[\[24a\]]
[\[24a.1\]]gnyis 'di gsungs/_/chos gcig las 'das gang yin pa/_/lus can [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]du smra ba ste/_/'jig rten pha rol spangs pa yin/_/'di yis sdig pa mi bya med/_/lcags gong me lce 'bar ba dag_/zos par gyur pa mchog yin gyi/_/tshul 'chal yang dag 
[\[24a.2\]]mi sdom pas/_/yul 'khor bsod snyoms za ba min/_/bslab pa'i gzhi ni bsgrub pa'i phyir/_/nges par [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]tshig bkag pa ste/_/lag brgyud smra ba'i khyu mchog nas/_/brtsams te [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]du smra rnams la/_/shes bzhin du ni [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]smra ba'i/_/dge slong la ni ltung byed brjod/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]tshig 
[\[24a.3\]]rnam pa dgu yin te/_/rnam pa gnyis kyi bar du brjod/_/sdom brtson dag la drungs 'byin chos/_/phas pham la sogs rnam pa lnga/_/tshul khrims lta ba cho ga dang /_/'tsho ba yi ni smra ba dgu/_/mthong sogs rnam pa gsum yin te/_/de dag gis ni smra ba la/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]tshig rnam pa brgyad 
[\[24a.4\]]ces 'dod/_/tshul khrims lta spyod cho ga 'tsho/_/mthong dang thos dang dogs pa yis/_/sdom brtson [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]du smra ba la/_/gang yang rnam pa bdun nyid brjod/_/smra 'gyur de bzhin smra ba dang /_/smras pa zhes bya tshig gsum dang /_/mthong ba la sogs tshig gsum ste/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]du smra ba drug 
[\[24a.5\]]tu brjod/_/de ltar re re dor ba yis/_/tshig gis 'dir ni shes bya ba/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]tshig rab tu shes pa la/_/brjod nas go ba rnam pa gnyis/_/slar yang phas pham la sogs lngas/_/phas pham 'gyur bar shes bya ste/_/phas pham gnas su gang brjod pa'i/_/sngar smras mtha' ni phas 
[\[24a.6\]]pham nyid/_/gzhan la nam yang mi bzhed do/_/dge 'dun lhag mar 'gyur ba ni/_/bag tsam gyis sam rtsa bas skur/_/chos la chos min la sogs des/_/gang zhig dge 'dun dbus smra na/_/de la sbom por 'gyur ba yi/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]tshig ces ni rnam par gzhag_/gso sbyong dus 
[\[24a.7\]]kyi las dag la/_/'di la dag gam zhes brjod tshe/_/kha rog nyes pa sbed byed na/_/nyes byas dri mar 'gyur bar brjod/_/de ltar de dag gang bstan pa/_/[{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]gyi tshig ni rnam pa bzhis/_/de las gzhan pa ltung byed du/_/'gyur ba'i [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]tshig ces byar bstan/_/ma mthong sogs 
[\[24b\]]
[\[24b.1\]]la mthong sogs dang /_/mthong sogs la'ang de las bzlog_/shes bzhin du ni smra byed na/_/don rig pa na ltung byed 'dod/_/dud 'gro la yang rwa chag sogs/_/skyon nas tshig tu smras pa na/_/sems zhum las byed mi brtson na/_/shes ldan mi rnams smos ci dgos/_/de phyir 
[\[24b.2\]]sems can thams cad la/_/phan bzhed bcom ldan 'das kyis bkag_/mi rnams la ni skyon brjod pas/_/dge slong la ni ltung byed yin/_/skyon brjod bsam pa'i dge slong gis/_/khyod ni bram ze rab byung zhes/_/bram ze la ni rigs dris na/_/de la nyes byas ltung bar 'gyur/_/de 'dra'i 
[\[24b.3\]]sems kyis rgyal rigs la'ang /_/smra byed nyes byas kho na ste/_/de bzhin rje rigs dmangs dag la/_/smra byed sdom brtson ltung byed yin/_/thags mkhan lha rten lha mkhan dang /_/shing mkhan tshem bu smyig ma mkhan/_/de la sogs pa'i rigs rnams kyis/_/yongs su smra na ltung byed 
[\[24b.4\]]brjod/_/rdza mkhan dang ni mgar ba dang /_/bran dang ko lpags mkhan dang ni/_/bya shor nya ba rngon pa dang /_/gtum po sogs brjod nyams par 'gyur/_/khyod ni bram ze yin te de'i/_/rigs kyi bya ba gtsang sbra sogs/_/bslab bya khyod la dge slong gis/_/ci bya zhe na'ang nyes byas nyid/_/khyod 
[\[24b.5\]]ni rgyal rigs khyod kyi las/_/mtshon cha'i rigs pa mdung dang ni/_/mda' sogs slobs shig ces smra na/_/gang yin de la nyes byas so/_/de bzhin rje rigs dmangs rigs kyi/_/las la nges par sbyar yang ngo /_/thags mkhan smyig ma mkhan sogs la/_/ltung byed kyi ni ltung bar brjod/_/
[\[24b.6\]]mchod sbyin dang ni gdol pa sogs/_/khyod kyis rang las byos shig ces/_/smra yang mi mkhas de dag la/_/snga ma bzhin du nyes byas yin/_/'thang po zhar ba lag rdum dang /_/sgur po mi thung long dang 'on/_/zhes smra na yang ltung byed kyi/_/me yi nang du sreg pa 'thob/_/khyod ni mdze can 
[\[24b.7\]]khrag nad skyug_/nyam chung g.yan pa 'og grol dang /_/de la sogs brjod ngan tshig rnams/_/sdom brtson la ni ltung byed 'dod/_/khyod ni ma dag lhung bas bcom/_/gdug pa dang bcas 'gyod pa can/_/khyod ni khro bas sems bsregs pa/_/zhes smras sogs la'ang de nyid do/_/
[\[25a\]]
[\[25a.1\]]dge slong skyon brjod bsam pa yis/_/bong bu sogs kyis gshe byed cing /_/rgya gad la sogs kun ldang bas/_/spyo na ltung byed kyis reg 'gyur/_/de ltar rigs dang bzo dang ni/_/las rnams dang ni rtags dang nad/_/ltung dang khro ba la sogs pa'i/_/tshig gis 
[\[25a.2\]]smra bar mi bya'o/_/the tshom bsal ba'i don du ni/_/sangs rgyas gdung ni gang yin pa/_/rgyal rigs las gang rab byung ba/_/zhes sogs smra la nyes pa med/_/dge slong phra ma smra na yang /_/ltung ba 'di nyid yang dag ldan/_/phra ma pha rol dbye ba'i phyir/_/nyon mongs can gyi tshig yin 
[\[25a.3\]]te/_/dper na 'breg mkhan gyol po khyod/_/de skad su zhig smra zhe na/_/smras pa dga' byed kyis smra zhes/_/'di ni phyogs tsam bstan pa ste/_/'dir yang thams cad snga ma bzhin/_/rigs dang bzo sogs smras pa las/_/nyes byas la sogs shes bya ste/_/blo ldan gyis ni spang bar bya/_/
[\[25a.4\]]dge 'dun mchog gcig pa yis ni/_/gang zhig mthun par gyur pa na/_/las la skyo sngogs byed pa des/_/zad byed ltung ba thob par 'gyur/_/mchog gcig bsam pa gcig pa yis/_/chos bzhin cho ga bzhin du ni/_/'gyed pa la sogs rnam bzhi yi/_/dbang byas bstan pa 
[\[25a.5\]]de dag gi_/las la skyo sngogs zhes bya ba/_/'di la legs zhes brjod pa yi/_/rdzogs na the tshom gyis kyang ni/_/skyo sngogs byed na ltung bar bcas/_/byas sam 'on te ma byas zhes/_/the tshom gyis kyang skyo sngogs na/_/nyes byas 'gyur te de la ni/_/cig shos min zhes mkhas pas 
[\[25a.6\]]gsungs/_/gzhan dag gi ni dbang byas la/_/gzhan gyi dbang byas 'du shes sam/_/rdzogs la ma rdzogs zhes pa la/_/ltung ba'i tshul ni sngar bzhin no/_/gnyug mar gnas dang las byed dang /_/'dun pa phul dang lta ba smra/_/glo bur ba ste 'di dag ni/_/gang zag lnga zhes yongs su 
[\[25a.7\]]grags/_/thog mtha' dbus su gang rig de/_/gnyug mar gnas pa zhes bya'o/_/las kyi dbang du byas pa las/_/gang zhig de byed de las byed/_/der ni gang zhig 'dun pa phul/_/de ni 'dun pa phul ba'o/_/lta ba smra ba zhes bya gang /_/gang dag dge 'dun dbus su ni/_/zhu tshe 
[\[25b\]]
[\[25b.1\]]bdag gis 'di ltar mthong /_/'di ltar min zhes smra ba'o/_/thog mtha' dbus su mi shes pa/_/de ni glo bur 'ongs par 'dod/_/dang po rnam pa gsum po yis/_/las la rtsod na ltung byed de/_/dam pa'i las la rtsod pa la/_/tha ma gnyis kyis nyes byas so/_/tshig lnga'am drug las 
[\[25b.2\]]phar lhag par/_/rig pa'i skyes pa med par ni/_/bud med dag la chos ston na/_/ltung byed skyon du 'gyur ba yin/_/mtha' dag gzugs ni mi rtag cing /_/de bzhin tshor dang 'du shes dang /_/'du byed dang ni rnam par shes/_/mkhas pas tshig lngar nges rtogs bya/_/mig dang rna ba sna 
[\[25b.3\]]dang lce/_/lus dang yid kyang mi rtag ces/_/tshig drug brjod pa 'di yin par/_/mkhas pa rnams kyis gsungs pa yin/_/tshig lnga bstan par bya'o zhes/_/shes bzhin tshig drug rjod byed na/_/ltung ba'o de bzhin drug pa yang /_/bdun pa rjes su ston na'o/_/'brel med smra ba nyes 
[\[25b.4\]]med de/_/shin tu smra ba myur yang ngo /_/dris lan mkhan gyi bud med ni/_/rigs ldan la ston nyes pa med/_/bsnyen par ma rdzogs pa dang tshig_/mnyam por chos brjod pa las ltung /_/rjes su 'brang la nyes med de/_/'don dang kha ton sbyong ba la'ang /_/gnas ngan len dang ldan 
[\[25b.5\]]pa'i tshig_/tshogs kyis gnang med brjod mi bya/_/de yang phas pham pa dang ni/_/dge 'dun lhag ma'i gnas ngan len/_/yang dag bla mar gyur pa'i chos/_/bsnyen par ma rdzogs gang zag la/_/sdom brtson gang zhig smra byed na/_/'di la yang ni ltung byed 'gyur/_/'dir ni sgrib pa rnam 
[\[25b.6\]]pa lnga/_/mi rnams kyi ni chos su thos/_/de zad bla ma'i chos su 'dod/_/bsam gtan rnams ni bla ma'o/_/ma gnang bar ni dge 'dun gyi/_/gang zag dag la ster byed ces/_/byin nas rtsod pa rtsom na ni/_/sdom brtson de yang zad ltung 'gyur/_/zla ba phyed phyed gso sbyong dus/_/so sor thar 
[\[25b.7\]]pa 'don pa'i tshe/_/gang zhig khyad gsod de la yang /_/zad byed ltung bar 'dod pa yin/_/phra zhing phra ba 'di dag 'dir/_/nyer bstan dgos pa ci zhig yod/_/gang dag nyon mongs dbang gis ni/_/bslab pa khyad gsod 'di skad smra/_/rtsis kyi phyir dang khyad gsod phyir/_/bsreg phyir yongs 
[\[26a\]]
[\[26a.1\]]su gdung ba'i phyir/_/de bzhin rma 'gegs don du zhes/_/tshig 'dir re res zad pa ste/_/phran tshegs lung sogs las gsungs pa/_/ji srid 'dul ba dang 'brel bas/_/der ni dge slong khyad gsod na/_/de nyid ltung ba dang bcas 'gyur/_/dge slong rang ngam 
[\[26a.2\]]gzhan gyis kyang /_/sa bon tshogs ni ma lus dang /_/'byung po'i gnas ni 'jig byed na/_/gsal por ltung byed du ni 'dod/_/gang zhig rtsa ba'i ngo bor skye/_/de ni rtsa ba'i sa bon te/_/yungs dang sge'u gsher gla sgang sogs/_/sdong bu'i sa bon de yang ni/_/sa gang la ni legs 
[\[26a.3\]]btsugs pas/_/slar yang sdong bu skye ba ste/_/n+ya gro d+ha dang u dum bAr/_/a shod ka ra bI ra sogs/_/gang zhig bcad pa'i rtse mo yang /_/'phel zhing skye bar 'gyur ba gang /_/ka shi bur shing 'dam bu smyig/_/de dag thog dgu'i ming can yin/_/bkas pas skye ba bkas pa 
[\[26a.4\]]yin/_/sa bon ji lta bu zhe na/_/a dza ka dang sam bu dang /_/dzam b+ha ri dang yan dzi ka_/sa bon sa bon til mud ga_/gang gzhan de 'dra'i sa bon yang /_/'byung po'i sgra gcan sems can gyi/_/rigs kun la ni nges par brjod/_/'bu srin phye ma leb la sogs/_/de yi gnas 
[\[26a.5\]]ni shing sogs grags/_/de la der 'du shes pas sam/_/the tshom gyis kyang gcod na'o/_/de ltar sa bon rtswa sngon dag_/de 'joms na ni ltung bar 'dod/_/sa bon gzhan la gzhan 'du shes/_/the tshom gyis kyang ltung bar 'dod/_/gtun du sa bon rnam pa lnga/_/blugs nas nyams par 
[\[26a.6\]]bya'o zhes/_/dge slong lhur len nyams na ni/_/ltung byed lnga nyid 'dod pa yin/_/ma nyams na ni de dag la/_/nyes byas kyang ni rnam pa lnga/_/mer 'jug na yang tshul de nyid/_/khu tshan la sogs par yang rung /_/sems de lta bus rtswa sngon la/_/'chag par byed pa dag kyang ngo /_/nyams 
[\[26a.7\]]na dge slong ltung byed de/_/ma nyams na ni nyes byas so/_/shing dang re lde la sogs pa/_/sngo sar la ni 'drud pa dang /_/khu tshan thug pa la sogs pa/_/'bod pa la yang 'dod pa yin/_/sbyor ba gcig nyid kyis kyang 'dir/_/shing gcig gcod par byed pa na/_/de la nyes 
[\[26b\]]
[\[26b.1\]]byas gcig dang ni/_/zad byed ltung byed gcig yin no/_/sbyor ba yang ni gnyis dag gis/_/sdom brtson shing sogs gcig gcod na/_/nyes byas gnyis dang ltung byed gcig/_/de ltar sbyor ba ji snyed pa/_/de snyed du ni nyes byas 'dod/_/rdzas kyi tshad kyi dbye ba yis/_/zad byed 
[\[26b.2\]]ltung byed tha dad 'dod/_/'dab sngon me tog 'bras bu dang /_/kha 'bus pa dang rtsa ba nyid/_/sdong bu dang ni chu ba gcod/_/nya lcibs 'don yang ltung byed nyid/_/shun pa dang ni 'dab skya dang /_/mtsho skyes dag ni rgyas pa nyid/_/gcod pa la ni nyes byas gsungs/_/'di dag mdor 
[\[26b.3\]]bsdus tshul yin no/_/so shing rtsa ba shun pa dang /_/'dab ma me tog sogs 'dod na/_/bslang bya ri las chod dang shogs/_/zhes sogs tshig ni spangs nas so/_/sbrang [(]tshad [,]tshang [)]dang ni sha bal nyid/_/sha mo 'bu srin tshang de bzhin/_/bya tshang dang ni speng sha dang /_/pa rgya yang ni dbyung mi bya/_/
[\[26b.4\]]rung bas gcod dang 'don pa la/_/thams cad du yang nyes spyod min/_/'di dag shes par gyis shig ces/_/brjod na 'di ni rung ba yin/_/gsar pa'i las la las byed kyis/_/shing gi lha la gsol btab cing /_/gtor ma me tog la sogs pas/_/cho ga bzhin du 
[\[26b.5\]]mchod nas bya/_/rgyud chags gsum pa 'don byas nas/_/dam chos nyan pa sbyin byas ste/_/de la las lam bcu po ni/_/dge dang mi dge brjod par bya/_/dge bas 'bras bu bde ster te/_/cig shos kyis ni ngan song yin/_/ser sna'i skyon dang bcas dag la/_/
[\[26b.6\]]sbyin pa'i yon tan legs brjod bya/_/gang zhig dga' tshal la sogs su/_/lha mo'i tshogs dang lha rnams kyis/_/rtsed 'jo yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]bde myong ba/_/de ni sbyin pa'i 'bras bur gsungs/_/bkres dang skom sogs me dag gis/_/lus [(]ring [,]rid [)]lce mgrin skams pa yi/_/skal 
[\[26b.7\]]ngan 'khor byed yi dwags gang /_/mi yi gzugs 'dra sdug bsngal can/_/de ni thog med srid pa yi/_/bag chags kun khyab mthu ldan pas/_/mchog tu don med 'gyur ba ste/_/ser sna'i 'tshe ba yin par 'dod/_/khrag 'jag pa la sogs pa yis/_/zhag bdun rnam 'gyur mi ston na/_/
[\[27a\]]
[\[27a.1\]]tshul 'di legs par rig nas ni/_/shing chen gcod pa la sogs bya/_/dge slong la ni 'phya ba dang /_/gzhogs 'phyas kyang ni rnam pa mang /_/byed na'ang ltung ba sngar ji bzhin/_/'dir yang ltung ba kho nar brjod/_/'di dag 'phya ba nyid dbang byas/_/dge slong 
[\[27a.2\]]gzhogs 'phyas mkhan yang 'dir/_/gsol ba'i las ni sngon 'gro bas/_/dge 'dun gyis gang bskos pa yi/_/gus pas las ni nges ldan pa/_/de la dge slong gang zhig 'phya/_/khro sems ldan na ltung byed do/_/cig shos nyid ni nyes byas nyid/_/gtsug lag khang gtong zas dang 
[\[27a.3\]]ni/_/'o thug 'bras bu 'gyed pa dang /_/phyi de nyin par yang ni rung /_/phran tshegs dag ni 'drim pa dang /_/snod spyad 'drub dang sra brkyang gi_/chos gos dag ni sbed pa dang /_/chos gos rnams ni 'gyed pa dang /_/dbyar gyi ras chen sbed pa dang /_/khang skyong de yi mngag gzhug dang /_/
[\[27a.4\]]de bzhin sder spyad zhal ta ba/_/rtsod pa 'gegs dang chu 'drim dang /_/bya rog la sogs skrod pa dang /_/mal stan 'dug stan stobs pa dang /_/zas 'dun 'jig rten bzlog pa dang /_/gsol dang gnyis kyi las gang yin/_/'di dag dge 'dun gyis ni bsko/_/gang phyir dang po bcu 
[\[27a.5\]]gnyis la/_/'phya dang gzhogs 'phyas [(]gyis [,]kyis [)]ltung 'gyur/_/gzhogs 'phyas brtse ba med pa'i tshig_/'phya ba pha rol la smod pa'o/_/bsgo ba rna gzon ltung ba'o/_/bsgo ba zhes bya dri bar brjod/_/rna la gzon pa ngan song kyis/_/nges pas lan gzhan smra ba'o/_/gzhan dris pa 
[\[27a.6\]]na gzhan du ni/_/gzhan gdung bya phyir smra yang yin/_/gzhan du gsad bya'i gang zag ni/_/dgra bos dris tshe 'di skad du/_/nam mkha' 'am sor mo bdag mthong gi_/dam pa'i don du sems can min/_/zhes ni kha nas bslu tshig dag_/brjod pa la ni bkag pa med/_/rna la gzon 
[\[27a.7\]]pa'i bsam pa yis/_/dris tshe sdom brtson mi smra 'dug_/nyes byas kyis ni ngan song du/_/sdug bsngal mthar ni smra bar 'dod/_/dge 'dun mal stan khri'u sogs/_/bla gab med par bor nas kyang /_/gzhan la ma bcol bar yang ni/_/'gro na zad byed ltung ba 'thob/_/brtul 
[\[27b\]]
[\[27b.1\]]zhugs can ni 'gro ba na/_/ji srid khri'u la sogs pa'i/_/phyi yi mtshams ni ma 'das pa/_/de srid du ni nyes byas so/_/slar log tshe na ma nyams na/_/nyes byas dang ni ldan 'gyur te/_/ji ste phan tshun gyur na ni/_/de la zad byed ltung byed do/_/me dang rlung dang char pa yi/_/dbye 
[\[27b.2\]]bas phan tshun gsum du gsungs/_/rim pa gnyis par tshig na ni/_/me yi phan tshun zhes bya'o/_/rim pa gnyis dag bzlog na ni/_/rlung gi phan phun nyid ces bya/_/char pa zhes bya char chu yis/_/rim pa gnyis par phigs na yin/_/gtsug lag khang du zhugs pa na/_/
[\[27b.3\]]me la sogs pa'i phan phun la/_/nyes byas la sogs rnam gzhan dag_/snga ma bzhin du yang dag bshad/_/lam du rab zhugs de lta'i gzhi/_/gal te dran par gyur na de'i/_/mod la byis pa'i rang bzhin zhes/_/bdag nyid la ni yang dag smad/_/de yis ji srid dge slong 
[\[27b.4\]]gzhan/_/mthong nas nye bar song nas ni/_/gnas mal mal stan sba ba'i phyir/_/gus pa sngon 'gros gsol ba gdab/_/gal te de yis thos nas kyang /_/de dag sba btul mi byed na/_/de yang ltung byed mda' yis ni/_/phog pa'i sdug bsngal nyams myong 'gyur/_/khyim 
[\[27b.5\]]pas mgron du bos pa'i tshe/_/'dug stan la sogs g.yar ba na/_/de tshul dag gam khyim pa yis/_/bkur bar bya yis rang gis min/_/gtsug lag khang du khyim pa dag_/zan za'i tshe na rdzogs kyi bar/_/'dug stan sbyin bya de dag la/_/rang don ma nyams pa yis bsrung /_/
[\[27b.6\]]nad pa dang ni nad g.yogs dag/_/rgan por gyur dang tshul khrims 'chal/_/nad bcas bsnyen par ma rdzogs la/_/nam yang yid brtan mi bya'o/_/stan gcig la ni 'dug pa na/_/gsar bus de dag brtul bya zhing /_/gnyi ga gsar bu yin na ni/_/stan gang phyi nas dong pas 
[\[27b.7\]]yin/_/dam pa'i chos nyan la sogs dus/_/gnas brtan mkhrang mi tshugs pa yis/_/gang du 'dug der 'dug stan ni/_/gzhag bya 'gyel ba nyid phyir yang /_/de phyir mkhyen mchog bcom ldan gyi/_/bsnyen bkur cho ga thams cad ni/_/gnyi ga la yang phan 'dod pas/_/
[\[28a\]]
[\[28a.1\]]bla ma la brten bya bar gsungs/_/rings pa'i rkyen ni byung ba na/_/rtsig pa'i drung ngam shing drung du/_/bzhag pa la ni nyes med de/_/glo bur zas ni gcad mi bya/_/gang du song nas mi lta ba'i/_/ji srid chos gos nye 'khor ni/_/sngar gsungs de srid mal 
[\[28a.2\]]stan gyi'ang /_/shin tu bde ba sgrub byed pa'i/_/rin chen chos ni ston pa na/_/ston pa'i bka' ni mchod byas pas/_/stan mtho mdzes la 'dug par bya/_/gru bzhi rab mnyam bde ba'i stan/_/rgyab rten rkang rten skas dang bcas/_/chun 'phyang bla res legs bkod pa'i/_/seng ge'i 
[\[28a.3\]]khri yang bcang bar bya/_/nor yod de tshe rjes gnang phyir/_/'dus byas mi rtag yid ches pas/_/rin chen kun gyis mdzes par ni/_/legs byas de la 'dug bya nyid/_/de ltar yid la byed pa dang /_/sbyin bdag la yang phan pa'i phyir/_/sdom brtson rin chen sna tshogs kyis/_/mdzes 
[\[28a.4\]]pa'i khri la 'dug par bya/_/gnod sbyin gnas dang klu yi yul/_/lha min lha yul skyon med de/_/gang 'di de dag bsod nams las/_/legs grub phyir zhes thub pas gsungs/_/dge 'dun gtsug lag khang dag tu/_/sdom brtson rtswa sogs bting nas ni/_/ma bsdus ma bcol 
[\[28a.5\]]gyi nar yang /_/rkyen med 'gro bar byed na ltung /_/mal stan gyi ni rigs pa yis/_/rjes la bstan pa 'di la yang /_/'dul ba'i cho ga la mkhas pas/_/nges par ltung ba nyid du bshad/_/gtsug lag khang du thob pa yang /_/chu yi nang du dor nas sam/_/khyim gzhan du yang mi brtul 
[\[28a.6\]]lam/_/ci gar ma brtul nyes byas so/_/bsam gtan pa yi 'chag pa'i sa/_/khru ni bco brgyad dag tu bya/_/kha ton pa yi khru bcu gnyis/_/rtsub na par thang la sogs gding /_/gzhan du nad bskyed 'gyur ba ste/_/sa gzhi dag tu gding bar bya/_/bag yod shes bzhin dang 
[\[28a.7\]]bcas bcag_/zla ba phyed cing skyang nul bya/_/dge 'dun gtsug lag khang nas ni/_/sdang bas dge slong phyir skrod na/_/dge slong la ni ltung byed gsungs/_/de 'dra'i rkyen can ma gtogs so/_/gal te rang min dge slong gzhan/_/sdang bcas skrod du 'jug na yang /_/de dag 
[\[28b\]]
[\[28b.1\]]gnyi ga la yang ni/_/ltung byed kho nar gsungs pa yin/_/dge tshul la sogs rnams kyis kyang /_/sdom brtson skrod du 'jug byed na/_/dge slong la ni ltung byed de/_/de dag la ni 'gal tshabs can/_/kha zas ngan dang bzang pos sam/_/grang dang dro sogs kyis kyang rung /_/
[\[28b.2\]]dge slong la ni tho 'tshams na/_/ltung byed kho nar 'gyur ba yin/_/bsnyen bkur ba dang khrus khang dang /_/khang pa'i sgo mo nyid dang ni/_/gnas khang mdzod dang khang [{]bzangs su[,]bzang du[}]/_/dge slong nyid la gtad nas ni/_/'dug stan gnas dang gnas mal la/_/dge slong snga nas gnas pa 
[\[28b.3\]]la/_/'tshe ba phyis gnon ltung byed yin/_/gtsug lag khang steng gnas su ni/_/spang leb sogs kyis phub pa la/_/'dug par bya ste khri khang dag_/'byung ba la ni ltung byed 'dod/_/de bzhin legs par gnas bcas dang /_/rtsa ba 'byung ba zhes bya gang /_/gtsug lag khang 
[\[28b.4\]]gi steng gi thog_/shin tu mkhrang pa ma yin la/_/mnan pas rtsa ba phyir 'byung ba'o/_/rkang rten byas pa nyid dang ni/_/bkan te bzhag nas 'dug pa la/_/'dir ni bkag pa yod min te/_/nyal ba la yang tshul 'di nyid/_/lcags kyi gzer bus bsdams pa dang /_/rtsa ba brtan po 
[\[28b.5\]]dag la yang /_/bal ba dza sogs bcings kyang rung /_/phur bu lta bu bkag pa yin/_/srog chags bcas pa'i chu yis ni/_/rtswa shing sogs la 'debs byed dam/_/'debs su 'jug pa nyid kyang rung /_/sdom brtson la ni ltung byed yin/_/srog chags bcas la der 'du shes/_/the 
[\[28b.6\]]tshom gyis kyang sdom can ltung /_/med par 'du shes nyes med de/_/the tshom gyis kyang nyes byas nyid/_/bum pa dpyang thag bcas par ni/_/slong la ster byed nyes pa med/_/rnyog pa ka ta ka sogs kyis/_/dngas par byas te brtag par bya/_/rnyog pa nyid dngas byad bzhin gyi/_/gzugs 
[\[28b.7\]]brnyan gang du ltar yod tshang /_/de ni 'bad pas btsags nas ni/_/shin tu dag pas chu btung ngo /_/khron pa bkod mas rnam dag dang /_/zhabs tshags can sogs dam po dang /_/dge 'dun dge slong gis dag pa/_/rung ba'i chu lnga yang dag gzung /_/ril tshags zhes bya'i 
[\[29a\]]
[\[29a.1\]]rnam pa dang /_/de bzhin grol bar byed pa'i ras/_/gru gsum pa dang zhabs tshags te/_/bum tshags gce'u can dang lnga/_/ji srid dag par nges pa'i bar/_/rtse gcig pa yi rgyun du ni/_/'bad pas chu rnams yongs brtag ste/_/'on kyang bla 'og yang 
[\[29a.2\]]yang min/_/ma ga d+hA pa'i smyug mas gang /_/ba lang drug pa'i shing rta ni/_/yongs su 'khor ba'i dus gang gi/_/tshad der chu de yongs su brtag_/snod kyi skyon la sogs pa yis/_/gal te the tshom 'gyod skyes na/_/de ni slar yang brtag bya ste/_/the tshom med par yongs su 
[\[29a.3\]]spyad/_/rgyang grags lnga yi bar dag tu/_/song nas phyin pa de na ni/_/grol byed ras la sogs pa yi/_/chu tshags yod pa yin na 'gro/_/slar log pa yi bsam pa yis/_/dpag tshad phyed 'gro nyes med de/_/don mthun la ni yid brtan pas/_/shin tu rgyang ring song yang ngo /_/
[\[29a.4\]]chu bo'i rgyun bzhin 'gro ba la/_/brtags pa'i chu 'thung nyes pa med/_/chu gzhan dang ni ma 'dres na/_/rgyang grags re rer brtag par bya/_/rgyun las bzlog phyogs 'gro ba yis/_/'jug ngogs re rer brtag par bya/_/chu gzhan dang ni 'dres pa la'ang /_/sngar bstan tshul 'di nyid shes 
[\[29a.5\]]bya/_/khron pa la sogs chu blangs nas/_/chu de gang gi yin pa dang /_/de yi lhag pa'i lha yi phyir/_/bde gshegs kyis gsungs tshigs bcad 'don/_/bum pa'i kha ni bzlog pa yis/_/nam yang chu btung mi bya ste/_/lo ma la sogs ma rnyed na/_/dben par 'thung la nyes pa 
[\[29a.6\]]med/_/ril tshags la sogs kha dag ni/_/'dab ma sogs kyis nges par dgab/_/chu 'thungs de rjes bum pa sogs/_/ma bkab yal dor nyes byas yin/_/bum pa la sogs dri ma ni/_/rab tu 'bad pas bkru bar bya/_/bskam 'os dus na ji srid du/_/rung ba'i don du nye bar 
[\[29a.7\]]bskam/_/khyim pas khyim pa nyid la ste/_/dge tshul gyis ni mi rung ngo /_/dge slong gis ni khyim pa dang /_/dge tshul gzhan la rung ba yin/_/dge slong de yis rnam brtags pa/_/gang phyir khyim pa la yang rung /_/dge slong yang dag gnas pas brtag_/gzhan du bslab la g.yel 
[\[29b\]]
[\[29b.1\]]bas min/_/khron pa rdzing bu la sogs pa'i/_/chu rnams zas sogs dri ma can/_/the tshom med par btsags nas ni/_/de yis rtog pa de med bya/_/khyim pa rnams kyis khron pa sogs/_/yongs su brtags pas dag pa yin/_/de yi gnas skabs shes nas ni/_/dus ma yin par yang 
[\[29b.2\]]ni rung /_/ba lang lhas la sogs par ni/_/dge slong song tshe chu med dam/_/nyung na zho ga chu'am ni/_/'o ma sogs kyis rkang bkrur rung /_/bshang ba chang dang gci ba yi/_/snod dag rnam par spang bar bya/_/mar khu la sogs pa yi snod/_/me dang chu yis yongs su spyad/_/nya dang rus 
[\[29b.3\]]sbal gyis bldags pa'i/_/gsher gyi dri ma spang ba'i phyir/_/rung ba'i chu ni skom pa la/_/dus ma yin par yang ni spyad/_/bud med dag la dge slong gis/_/chu sogs blud par 'dod pa na/_/rgyun gyis sbyin par mi bya ste/_/kun nas chags sems kyis kyang min/_/sgo leb ngos khung 
[\[29b.4\]]dag dang ni/_/smad du chu khung btod pa dang /_/skar khung 'bab stegs bcas pa yi/_/gtsug lag khang chen bya ba yin/_/char dang rlung ni bzlog pa'i phyir/_/steng nas mnan par bya ba ni/_/rtswa bcas 'jim pa'i gong bu dag_/gnyis sam gsum gyi bar du'o/_/de las lhag 
[\[29b.5\]]par gang 'jog pa/_/nges par ltung byed 'gyur ba yin/_/so phag rdo ba dag gis sam/_/thams cad shing byed nyes pa med/_/tshul khrims ldan dang thos pa 'dzin/_/gnas brtan rgyal tshabs lta bu dang /_/phun tshogs tshig ldan lus kyis ni/_/dge slong ma ni sun ma phyung /_/chos 
[\[29b.6\]]lnga 'di dang ldan pa la/_/dge 'dun gyis ni bskos pa dag_/dge slong ma la 'doms pa ste/_/gzhan du ltung ba nyid yin no/_/mdo sde 'dul ba chos mngon dang /_/'brel pa'i don ni rab ston pa/_/gdams pa yin par 'dren pa yis/_/rtsod pa med par gsungs pa yin/_/
[\[29b.7\]]gnas brtan gyis ni bstan pa'i tshig_/'phags pa'i tshogs kyis bzhed pa ni/_/slar yang gdams pa gzhan 'di ni/_/gnyis par yang ni gsungs pa yin/_/dge slong ma yi dge 'dun khyed/_/thams cad yongs su dag gam dri/_/khyed la rjes su ston pa ni/_/dge slong 
[\[30a\]]
[\[30a.1\]]su yang spro ba med/_/ngan song gsum 'gro 'jigs byed pas/_/ji ltar bslabs pa mi 'khrugs pa/_/thub pa'i bstan la rtag tu ni/_bag yod sgrubs shig sring mo rnams/_/gdul bya'i 'gro la rjes brtse bas/_/'dul ba gang de rgyal srid brjod/_/de 
[\[30a.2\]]ltar 'di ni legs pa ste/_/dge slong rgyal po zhes bya'o/_/re shig gdams par 'dod pa ni/_/tshul ldan rnams kyis bskos pa yang /_/nyi ma 'jig rten pa yi mig_/ji srid ma nub bar du'o/_/nub la de ni 'du shes sam/_/the tshom gyis kyang gdams ngag dang /_/rjes su ston na 
[\[30a.3\]]zad pa yis/_/ltung byed dang ni yang dag ldan/_/ma nub nub par 'du shes sam/_/the tshom gyis kyang nges par ni/_/nyes byas mda' yis phog pa yi/_/sdug bsngal chen po myong bar 'gyur/_/sgo 'am sgo dang nye bar yang /_/gal te char ni 'bab gyur na/_/shin tu yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]
[\[30a.4\]]'doms pa la/_/nub nas kyang ni ltung ba med/_/dge slong ma yi mkhas rnams kyi/_/bla ma ci nus bsnyen bkur bya/_/de yi spro dang bsod nams dag_/spel phyir bla ma yis kyang spyad/_/phrag dog dri mas bsgos sems kyis/_/de la smod byed dge slong ni/_/ltung bas kun nas 
[\[30a.5\]]bslang ba yis/_/dmyal me sbor bar byed pa yin/_/yang na zang zing 'dod pa yi/_/sred pa kho na'i dbang gyur pas/_/gdams pa byed na bden par ni/_/smra byed nyes pa yod ma yin/_/nye du ma yin dge slong ma'i/_/chos gos byed na ltung byed do/_/'di la ster na'ang ltung byed du/_/
[\[30a.6\]]rjes su bstan pa kho na'o/_/dge slong 'gron lam 'gro ba na/_/dge slong ma yi grogs byed bkag_/chom rkun la sogs 'jigs bcas pa'i/_/lam du ltung bar mi 'dod do/_/dge slong rnams kyis dge slong ma'am/_/dge slong yang ni dge slong mas/_/lam du na na bskur bya 
[\[30a.7\]]ste/_/khur byed ye med gyur na'o/_/dge slong ma yis 'ongs pa yi/_/lam rgyags dge slong la ni rung /_/dge slong gis 'ong de la yang /_/rnam pa kun tu bkag pa med/_/dge slong dge slong ma dang ni/_/lhan cig rgyun dang bzlog phyogs su/_/grur 'jug pa ni bkag pa yin/_/
[\[30b\]]
[\[30b.1\]]'jigs bcas thad 'gro nyes pa med/_/dben par bud med gcig dang ni/_/dge slong gcig pu lhan cig tu/_/'dug pas gnas par mi bya ste/_/khyad par dge slong ma dang 'greng /_/dge slong gi ni zang zing zas/_/dge slong ma yi sbyor bcug pa/_/sngar ni mgron du gnyer ba yis/_/
[\[30b.2\]]yongs su spyod la nyes pa med/_/sgrub pa dang ni thos pa yis/_/sbyor bcug pa ni rnam pa gnyis/_/sgrub pa rgyun du zhugs pa nas/_/ji srid dgra bcom gyur gyi bar/_/thos pa mdo sde 'dul ba dang /_/ma mo 'dzin pa nyid kyang ste/_/de nyid bden pa yin na ni/_/yongs su spyad kyang nyes 
[\[30b.3\]]pa med/_/mi bden yon tan smra ba yis/_/rnyed pa sbyor bcug zos na ltung /_/nad dang chos gos las dang lam/_/dus ma gtogs par sdom brtson ni/_/zas ni gzhan du 'dod pa yis/_/yang yang za na ltung byed yin/_/lan cig zos pas mi 'tsho ba/_/de ni nad pa zhes 
[\[30b.4\]]bya'o/_/yul bzhir gru gang tsam gyis kyang /_/chos gos kyi ni dus su 'dod/_/gtsug lag khang ngam mchod rten la/_/tha na re lde tsam yang rung /_/byug gam kun nas snyoms byed na/_/las byed dus su brjod pa yin/_/dpag tshad phyed du song nas ni/_/sdom brtson slar 
[\[30b.5\]]yang log pa na/_/de ni lam dus shes bya ste/_/yang yang za la nyes pa med/_/chos gos bcas par rjes gnang bas/_/chos gos med par khas mi blang /_/phyi ma'i za ba 'ba' zhig la/_/dang du blangs na nyes byas yin/_/dang por gos ni med pa dang /_/phyi ma'ang gos bcas 
[\[30b.6\]]yin grang na/_/gang phyir ston mo gnyi ga yang /_/za ba la ni bkag pa med/_/dang po yang ni gos bcas la/_/phyi ma'ang gos can yin na ni/_/gnyi ga la yang ci 'dod du/_/za ba bkag pa yod ma yin/_/chos gos bor nas med gyur te/_/gos bcas dang du len pa la/_/
[\[30b.7\]]kun tu gos ni mi rnyed phyir/_/kun mkhyen thugs rje can gyis gnang /_/grong du mgron du gnyer ba na/_/dge 'dun mtha' dag go byas nas/_/rgan po gtsug lag khang skyong gis/_/dus su gaN+DI brdungs byas na/_/dge slong rang gi sde pa dang /_/glo bur lhag pa mthong 
[\[31a\]]
[\[31a.1\]]ba la/_/'gro ba'i ched du bskul bya ste/_/gzhan du 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/dge slong mu stegs 'dug gnas su/_/'dug nas der ni g.yos pa'i zas/_/zhag gcig las lhag zos na ni/_/nad pa las gzhan ltung bar 'gyur/_/nad pa min pas 
[\[31a.2\]]nyin zhag ni/_/gnyis par gnas na nyes byas yin/_/bza' ba zos na ltung byed de/_/gzhan nas der za na yang ngo /_/spyir na rje bos gsungs pa ni/_/nye du 'brel pa kho na la/_/lan brgya bza' ba zos kyang ni/_/ltung ba'i dri ma yod ma yin/_/ci bder stobs pa ma yin par/_/
[\[31a.3\]]dge slong phye dang khur ba yis/_/gang ba'i lhung bzed gnyis gsum las/_/lhag pa'i len na ltung byed yin/_/lhung bzed chen po gnyis dang ni/_/chung ngu gnyis dang chen po phyed/_/lhung bzed chung ngu gsum dang ni/_/'bring po gnyis su gang bstan pa/_/'bring po gnyis dang chung ngu gcig_/
[\[31a.4\]]chen po gcig dang chung ngu gcig_/len la ltung ba yod min te/_/'bring po gsum du len pa la/_/nyes byas nges par thob pa yin/_/bstan bzhin lhung bzed gang ba las/_/gzhan len dge slong ltung byed yin/_/gzhan du lhung bzed gang bar ni/_/len par byed la nyes pa med/_/phan sems nye du'i khyim las 
[\[31a.5\]]gang /_/len la nyes pa yod min te/_/de blangs lhan cig gnas pa yi/_/dge slong rnams la bgo bsha' bya/_/bza' ba spangs nas gang za ba/_/dge slong rnams la ma brtags yin/_/lhag por legs par ma byas pa/_/de la nges par ltung byed 'dod/_/bza' bar bya ba 
[\[31a.6\]]spangs nas 'dir/_/nam yang rjes su ma gnang ste/_/bza' ba spangs nas slar yang 'dir/_/bca' ba 'cha' ba nyid kyang ngo /_/sna tshogs chan dang zan dang sha/_/phye dang khur ba bza' ba ste/_/rtsa ba sdong bu me tog 'bras/_/lo ma yang ni bca' ba lnga/_/gang du zang zing zhes byar 
[\[31a.7\]]brjod/_/bza' ba rnams su shes bya ste/_/de ster de yi rnam 'gyur dang /_/spangs dang stan ni bor ba ste/_/gnas ni lnga po 'di dag la/_/dge slong spangs pa zhes byar brjod/_/gzhan du 'di dag min pa la/_/spangs pa yod pa ma yin no/_/re zhig tshig dang ldan pa yis/_/
[\[31b\]]
[\[31b.1\]]tshim par gyur pa'i mthar phyin te/_/de bzhin song shig brjod pa yis/_/spang bar gyur la nyes pa med/_/glo nas ma yin rgyab nas min/_/pang du ma yin nam mkhar min/_/dkyil 'khor dag gi steng du yang /_/nam yang lhag por mi bya'o/_/'bad pas lag pa rab bkrus nas/_/kha zas 
[\[31b.2\]]de ni yongs su blang /_/'dug sa mang por dge slong gis/_/gang zos de ni lhag por min/_/gus pas de yi mdun du ni/_/tsog pur 'dug nas 'di skad brjod/_/btsun pa dgongs shig bza' ba dag/_/lhag por byas pa bza' bar 'tshal/_/'di ni bdag gis phun tshogs pa'i/_/
[\[31b.3\]]bza' ba la sogs rung ba ste/_/lhag por mdzad nas gsol cig ces/_/gus dang bcas pas brjod par bya/_/des kyang de la kham gnyis sam/_/kham gsum zos nas de la khyod/_/song shig ji ltar bde bar ni/_/'di dag rnams ni bza' bar gyis/_/de ni zos nas spangs pa dang /_/
[\[31b.4\]]gnas las ma langs pa yis ni/_/yon tan can gyi mdun 'dug nas/_/cho ga snga ma bzhin du bya/_/de yis ma zos pa na yang /_/de la de ni bya ba ste/_/song la 'di ni ci bder zo/_/tshul 'di lhag por cho gar brjod/_/sdom brtson mgul par btung bya ba/_/'o ma zho la sogs pa 
[\[31b.5\]]la/_/tshul 'di nges par brtag mi dgos/_/nas la sogs pa'i thug pa ni/_/thug pa sor rjes yod pa dang /_/gang du steng ni sla bar gnas/_/gting na ska bar gnas mthong nas/_/ska de mdzub mos ldag byed la/_/spangs pa yod pa kho na ste/_/spangs pa der ni 'du shes 
[\[31b.6\]]pas/_/thos sam the tshom gyis kyang rung /_/ltung byed dmyal ba'i lam rnams spyod/_/gang du ma spangs par yang ni/_/gang phyir spangs par 'du shes sam/_/the tshom kho na yis kyang rung /_/nyes byas sdug bsngal ster byed yin/_/dge slong gang zhig rab spangs nas/_/lhag por ma 
[\[31b.7\]]byas dge slong gis/_/gang phyir ngan sems bsams pa yi/_/bza' ba stobs pa ltung byed do/_/lhung bzed rkang pa gnyis dag gis/_/mnan nas nam yang bza' mi bya/_/bder gnas yin na ltung 'gyur te/_/nad can dag la nyes pa med/_/mchil lham gyon nas bza' ba dag_/mi rung 
[\[32a\]]
[\[32a.1\]]dge slong nad ma gtogs/_/des kyang mchil lham steng du ni/_/rkang pa bzhag gi gzhan du min/_/'dren byed rgyab dang rgyang ring dang /_/glo dang cho ga mi shes dang /_/dge slong gis bskyod blang mi bya/_/lag pa ma bkan pas kyang min/_/'dren pa nye bar gnas pa 
[\[32a.2\]]dang /_/mdun du 'dug dang cho ga shes/_/lag pa dag ni bkan pa yis/_/dge slong gis ni legs par blang /_/lhung bzed byin gyis brlabs par ni/_/bza' ba gang zhig lhung gyur pa/_/the tshom med pa'i sems kyis ni/_/byin len byas yin bza' bar rung /_/rdul ni me tog 
[\[32a.3\]]'bras bu zan/_/ltung ba dang ni gos kyang rung /_/gang zhig sog 'jog de las gzhan/_/rung ba dang ni mi rung gang /_/brtags pa 'di dag dgu po ste/_/bcu po rdul gyi rdul yin no/_/gzugs ni gsal po ma yin pa/_/de dag la ni nyes pa med/_/sog 'jog byas pa de dang 
[\[32a.4\]]gang /_/byin len ma byas pa yi zas/_/de yis gos sogs 'bags gyur na/_/bkru'o gzhan du nyes byas yin/_/lan tshwa la sogs brims nas ni/_/gsar bus blang bar mi bya ste/_/zas la mi mthun med par ni/_/ji bzhin dus su nges par bstan/_/gang zhig rgan po'i gong 
[\[32a.5\]]du za/_/nges pa'am the tshom gyis kyang ni/_/de la nyin zhag re re la/_/nyes byas nges par 'phel ba yin/_/brtags pas nges par gzhan phyir byin/_/de ni sred pas cung zad cig_/byin len byas kyang de la de/_/de tshe spyad par rung ma yin/_/sbyin pa po yis ltos med par/_/gang 
[\[32a.6\]]gtad len pas thob pa ni/_/de ni byin len dag pa ste/_/zos pa na yang nyes pa med/_/nga la 'di las 'di byin zhes/_/'dren pa po la smra mi rung /_/rigs pas thob par bza' bya yis/_/gzhan min nad pas nyes pa med/_/sdom brtson zos nas lhag ma yi/_/chang bu yang ni ci bder gzhag_/
[\[32a.7\]]bya rog sogs la dmigs pa yi/_/kun slong khyad par mi bya'o/_/glo bur lhags dang 'gro 'dod dang /_/dge skos dang ni nad pa dang /_/de yi nad g.yog nyid la yang /_/bza' ba sngar ni sbyin par bya/_/gro bzhin skyes kyi dbang byas nas/_/btung ba dge 'dun la yang gnang /_/de yi phyir na 
[\[32b\]]
[\[32b.1\]]zhang dag ni/_/gzugs can snying pos phul bar bshad/_/'bras chan thug pa zan dron dang /_/nya dang sha ni rung ba ste/_/'tsho ba srung phyir 'di dag ni/_/bza' bar bya ba'i sman du gsungs/_/rgun 'brum a shwad 'bra go dang /_/ku shu chu shing rtsub 'gyur dang /_/u dum wA ra rgya shug 
[\[32b.2\]]dag_/btung bar rjes su bsngags pa yin/_/khyim pa dang ni dge tshul gyis/_/legs par mnye zhing yongs su btsags/_/'di dag btung ba thun tshod pa/_/skyur bo zho ga chu de bzhin/_/'bru mar bu ram sbrang rtsi dang /_/mar dag zhag bdun par ni gsungs/_/sha khar bye ma ka ra dag_/
[\[32b.3\]]'di dag chu la gdus pa ste/_/gzhan yang ji srid 'tsho ba'i sman/_/rtsa ba sdong bu sogs sbyar ba/_/tshul bzhin du ni byin brlabs te/_/yongs su spyad par rung ba yin/_/rtsa ba gla sgang sge'u gsher sogs/_/sdong bur yongs su grags pa ni/_/tsan+dan thang shing sle tres dang /_/
[\[32b.4\]]spur dang pad+ma dkar la sogs/_/'dab bdun pa to la sogs ni/_/lo ma'o de dag me tog kyang /_/'bras bu na le sham dang ni/_/skyu ru a ru ba ru sogs/_/shing kun spra tshil rgya skyegs dang /_/de bzhin thang chu dag kyang ste/_/til tshigs la sogs 'gyur byed lnga/_/de bzhin du 
[\[32b.5\]]yang tsha lnga'o/_/a mra a mra'i nang shun dang /_/'dab bdun pa dang shi ri sha'i/_/shun pa dag gi sman rnams ni/_/ji srid 'tsho ba zhes bya'o/_/gang gzhan de 'dra'i rigs dag ni/_/bza' ba'i don du bcang min pa/_/zos kyang bkres pa mi sel phyir/_/'di dag ji srid 'tsho ba'i 
[\[32b.6\]]sman/_/rgun 'brum bal se'u 'bra go dang /_/a mra la sogs 'bras bu ste/_/ut+pal la sogs rtsa ba dag_/mkhas pas dus su rung bar gsungs/_/dus su rung sogs 'di dag ni/_/phan tshun 'dres par gyur pa na/_/snga ma snga ma'i dbang byas nas/_/yongs su spyad la nyes 
[\[32b.7\]]pa med/_/nya dang rus sbal phag pa dang /_/nya sgong chu srin byis pa gsod/_/dom dang ri bong phag rgod dred/_/tshil zhag byin len byas spyod rung /_/sha rjen za ba dag la 'dir/_/dri zhim rdzas kyi phye mar bcas/_/sman pa'i ngag gis nad sos gnang /_/de las phar ni ma yin 
[\[33a\]]
[\[33a.1\]]no/_/sha ni bza' bar ma yin pa/_/mi dang lto 'phye glang po sogs/_/'drim na dge slong mkhas rnams kyis/_/'di ni gang gi sha zhes dri/_/mchod rten nye 'khor bla gab med/_/sgo dang bang rim chu yi gnas/_/khyams dang gnas khang dag tu ni/_/
[\[33a.2\]]bza' sogs 'tshed pa rnam par spangs/_/sems bsdus la sogs cho ga yis/_/rung ba'i gnas ni rnam pa lnga/_/'dren pas gtsug lag khang du ni/_/zas gnas dge 'dun don du gsungs/_/gtsug lag khang gi thig brkyang nas/_/so phag nye bar 'jog pa na/_/lag gi gla 
[\[33a.3\]]ni mkhas pa yis/_/phan 'dod sems bskyed bya ba nyid/_/tshogs kyi rung ba'i gnas su ni/_/sa phyogs 'di ni byin gyis brlabs/_/ngag tu yang ni lan gsum brjod/_/de ltar sems bskyed sngon 'gro bas/_/gtsug lag khang phyed zin pa la/_/lag blas dge 'dun mdun 'dug nas/_/
[\[33a.4\]]'di la sems rab sdus shig ces/_/lan gsum bar du brjod par bya/_/'di nyid zas kyi gnas nyid du/_/mdzes pa'i dngos por byin gyis brlabs/_/de ltar byed la sems bsdus pa'i/_/mtha' las byung bar mkhas pas gsungs/_/gtsug lag khang chen mang po dag_/
[\[33a.5\]]sna tshogs sgo ni tha dad na/_/gcig tu legs par bya ba ste/_/kun nas ba lang lhas bzhin no/_/gtsug lag khang rnying nyams gyur cing /_/dge slong rnams kyis bor ba na/_/'phral byung gyur la tshig gi mthar/_/gang de byin gyis brlab par brjod/_/gzhan yang 'dod don gcig pa 
[\[33a.6\]]dang /_/las brjod pa ni sngon 'gro bas/_/dge 'dun mthun pas las dag gi/_/cho ga zhes bya rdzogs la'o/_/de ltar zas kyi gnas gang dag_/sdom brtson gyis ni ma byas na/_/gang zhig btsos pa'i zas dang ni/_/zhag lon pa yang rung ma yin/_/sen mo ne tso me mtshon sogs/_/
[\[33a.7\]]rnyings dang dbug dang brtol ba dang /_/bcad dang gshegs pa'i ming can gyi/_/sa bon bcas pa'ang rung ba yin/_/rgan po'i mdun du 'dren pa yi/_/sam pras ta zhes brjod bya ste/_/de sgra bza' dang btung ba rnams/_/shin tu dag par bya phyir yin/_/gnas brtan gyis kyang bden 
[\[33b\]]
[\[33b.1\]]par ni/_/snyoms par brims zhes brjod par bya/_/shes bzhin ldan pas bza' bya zhing /_/zos nas kyang ni yon bshad bya/_/yon bshad la ni 'dug pa na/_/dge slong gang gis bzar mi rung /_/de ma thos la nyes med de/_/gzhan der ci bder bza' bar bya/_/tshigs bcad dang po 
[\[33b.2\]]brjod pa na/_/nye bar gtad de bsdad nas kyang /_/tshigs bcad mthar yang nyen byed na/_/de za ba la bkag pa med/_/dge 'dun gyi ni rgan po'am/_/nus ldan gzhan gyis kyang ni brjod/_/sbyin bdag rnams kyi 'dod pa dang /_/bsam pa ji bzhin chos bstan to/_/chos ni 'chad pa po la 
[\[33b.3\]]yang /_/nye gnas g.yog 'khor don du ni/_/dge slong rnams ni sbyin bya ste/_/gcig pu grong du mdzes ma yin/_/dga' ston dus tshe sku gzugs ni/_/rgan po'i gong du rtag tu bzhag_/ston pa dang ni dge 'dun bcas/_/'jig rten gsum snang khyab bdag yin/_/mtshan mo sgron me 
[\[33b.4\]]med par ni/_/chos nyan pa dang bshad mi bya/_/dbyar gyi dus su rtag tu ni/_/'od khang mig brgya pa yis dgab/_/khyim nas khyim du dge slong rnams/_/dgos pas mgron du bos pa na/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]gzhan du nam yang ni/_/de nas de dag 'gro mi bya/_/'phags pa'i dge 'dun 
[\[33b.5\]]tshul bzhin du/_/ci bder bza' ba zos nas ni/_/sbyin bdag gis ni gnang ba dang /_/'gro bar byed la nyes pa med/_/sha dang nya sogs 'jig rten na/_/dam pa'i skye bos ma smad gang /_/lhung bzed 'ongs pa de bza' ste/_/de 'dras ltung bar mi 'gyur ro/_/gang zhig ched du byas pa'i sha/_/
[\[33b.6\]]mthong ngam thos sam dogs pa ni/_/nam yang bza' bar bya min par/_/'gro kun phan par bzhed pas gsungs/_/ro la brkam pas gsod byed pa/_/snying rje med pa'i bsam pa spang /_/gang zhig srog gnas ched du ni/_/sha za ba la nyes pa med/_/stag gis mche bas bzung ba ni/_/
[\[33b.7\]]mthong ngam thos pa nyid na yang /_/de ni bza' bar mi bya ste/_/de yis nam yang mi gtong phyir/_/sgog skya la sogs sman rnams ni/_/nad pa dag la rjes gnang ste/_/gang phyir tshe srog dge ba yi/_/chos rnams kyi ni snod yin phyir/_/gang du dge slong rnams kyi dri/_/ma dad pa yis mi 
[\[34a\]]
[\[34a.1\]]snom na/_/de la nyes sbyor rnams kyis kyang /_/gnas kyi bya ba'i rgyur shes bya/_/bya ba rdzogs pa'i mthar yang ni/_/lus ni rnam par dag bya ste/_/dri ma lhag ma med pa na/_/slar yang gtsug lag khang 'jug bya/_/nub mo bdun dang nub mo gsum/_/de 
[\[34a.2\]]bzhin nub gcig rim bzhin du/_/sgog skya dang ni breg tsong dang /_/ke'u yang ni dag par gsungs/_/bsod snyoms dag la 'jug pa na/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]sgo mang rgyu bya ste/_/zas la sogs pa'i mtshan ma ni/_/sgor yang phyir ldog 'dod pas bzhag_/sgra ldan khar gsil khyer bya ste/_/
[\[34a.3\]]de yis sbyin bdag go bar bya/_/khyi dang ba lang la sogs bsdigs/_/rnam yang brdeg par mi bya'o/_/rtsa ba 'bras bu bza' bya dag_/dal gyis dal gyis dum bur bya/_/mnyes sam dum bur bsil nas ni/_/grong du dge slong dul bas bza'/_/mgron du bos tshe gos med par/_/
[\[34a.4\]]'chol pa'i gral gyis zas mi bza'/_/dus ma yin pa'i zan dag kyang /_/sman pa la dris gnang na rung /_/mu ge'i dus na mgron gnyer ba/_/shin tu mang yang dang du blang /_/sbyin bdag gis ni gnang ba na/_/bgo bsha' yis ni yongs su spyad/_/gtso bor mgron du bos pa yis/_/
[\[34a.5\]]phyed zos kyang ni sbyin bya ste/_/tshangs par spyod la rab mkhas des/_/nyam nga ba dag yongs su bskyang /_/thub pa'i bstan pa dang phrad srog/_/nyi ma gcig kyang rnyed dka' bas/_/yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]'tsho bar 'dod pa gang /_/de dag gis kyang yongs su spyad/_/gal te lhung bzed 'phan can du/_/
[\[34a.6\]]khu tshigs dag ni mthong ba na/_/dngos po gang gis de bsal nas/_/lan gsum bar du bkrus te spyad/_/zos nas kha yi dri ma ni/_/sa dang lci ba sogs kyis sbyang /_/chu khyor gsum mam lhag pas kyang /_/bkru bshal yongs su dag par bya/_/dge slong min pas reg gam 
[\[34a.7\]]zhes/_/blta bya the tshom med na bza'/_/gzhan du reg par gyur lta na/_/slar yang byin len ltos pa yin/_/lam du song tshe lam rgyags dag_/khur khyer su yang med pa na/_/brje ba yi ni 'du shes kyis/_/rang nyid kyis ni khur bar bya/_/brje ba gzhan dag med pa na/_/nyi ma dang po 
[\[34b\]]
[\[34b.1\]]zan bcad bya/_/gnyis pa la ni spar gang tsam/_/bza' yi de las lhag pa min/_/nyi ma gsum par spar ba gnyis/_/de las phar ni ci bder bya/_/rang nyid 'bad pas bsgrubs nas kyang /_/srog bsrung 'dod pas bza' bar bya/_/sa brkos nas ni rtsa ba'am/_/shing 
[\[34b.2\]]la 'dzegs nas 'bras bu blang /_/dge slong 'chi la thug pa yis/_/bkres pa bsal ba'i don phyir spyad/_/bcas pa'i kha na ma tho 'dir/_/gnas skabs rkyen dbang gis rjes gnang /_/rang bzhin kha na ma tho 'dir/_/tshul ldan srog la thug kyang min/_/'dod na nye du dang lhan 
[\[34b.3\]]cig_/legs blos bzar rung kho na ste/_/gal te lam 'jigs snod med na/_/dge tshul dang yang rung ba nyid/_/dge sbyong za ba'i dus kyi tshe/_/lhung bzed lag pa g.yon pas bzung /_/kham gcod la sogs byed pa'i dus/_/lag ni rtag tu dran pas bsrung /_/gang du bag med 
[\[34b.4\]]khwa dag gis/_/zas la rig par gyur pa na/_/mchu reg nye 'khor dor nas ni/_/bza' ba de la brtag mi dgos/_/mar dang 'bru mar bu ram sogs/_/gzhan yang dge 'dun gyi ni rdzas/_/khyer nas dran pa brjed pa yis/_/gang lus de ni dor mi rung /_/tshogs sam gang zag 
[\[34b.5\]]gis kyang rung /_/rung ba de ni yongs su blang /_/las kyi dus la bab pa na/_/legs par zos nas las 'jug bya/_/bu ram 'bras phye 'dres gyur na/_/gtsang ma'i chu yis yongs sbyangs nas/_/dus min par yang spyod pa la/_/zas kyi nyes pa yod ma yin/_/thun tshod rung ba la 
[\[34b.6\]]sogs sman/_/dus su rung dang 'dres pa na/_/dus ma yin par mi spyad cing /_/reg nas byin len ma byas min/_/thun tshod rung ba'ang de las gzhan/_/zhag bdun pa dang 'dres na ni/_/thun tshod las gzhan sdom brtson gyis/_/spyad min byin len ma byas min/_/zhag bdun pa 
[\[34b.7\]]yang ji srid du/_/'tsho bar dmigs dang 'dres na yang /_/zhag bdun par ni spyad bya ste/_/lhag par ma yin byin len bya//_//bam po bzhi pa/_dge slong gcig gis bu ram gang /_/rang gis spyod phyir byin brlabs te/_/phan tshun yid gcugs gyur pa yi/_/
[\[35a\]]
[\[35a.1\]]lnga po rnams la rjes su gnang /_/nad pa nyid dang zan chang dang /_/dge skos dang ni lag gi bla/_/lam du zhugs pa 'di lnga las/_/gzhan dag la ni rung ma yin/_/zang zing phra mo 'dres pa yis/_/bu ram chu shing mnyes nas ni/_/btsags nas sdom 
[\[35a.2\]]brtson btung ba ni/_/dus min par yang rung ba'o/_/rnyed dka'i 'bras bu me tog sogs/_/yid 'phrog bza' bar bya ba gang /_/rnyed na spang bar byas kyang ni/_/'dod na bza' bar gnang ba yin/_/bsod snyoms yang dag thob pa'i tshe/_/bsod snyoms pa yi spyod yul du/_/
[\[35a.3\]]khyim gzhan gnas te za ba ni/_/sbyin bdag bsod nams spel phyir rung /_/slar yang za bar 'dod lta na/_/spyod yul dag nas 'ongs nas ni/_/bza' bya mu ge'i dus dag tu/_/sngar blangs bsam pas rung ba yin/_/khyim du zos pa'i lhag ma dag_/gal te sbyin bdag 
[\[35a.4\]]gis bkur na/_/sngar blangs pa yi bsam pa yis/_/nyam nga ba na de yang ngo /_/gtsug lag khang gnas kun tu yang /_/dus gsum kun tu gtor ma sbyin/_/dus dang dus min za ba nyid/_/gnod sbyin zas phyir 'dod rnams sbyin/_/bu dang bcas pa'i 'phrog ma la'ang /_/
[\[35a.5\]]gtsug lag khang ni bsrung phyir dang /_/bstan pa yang ni phun tshogs phyir/_/bde gshegs kyis ni gtor ma gsungs/_/na sogs dus ni ma gtogs par/_/'dus shing za na ltung byed do/_/dge 'dun la ni gang cung zad/_/byin blangs de la ltung ba med/_/glo bur gnyug ma'i 
[\[35a.6\]]bza' ba gang /_/de yang 'dus shing bza' bar 'dod/_/na dang las dang lam gyi dus/_/sngar ni rab tu bsnyen pa nyid/_/gru dang gru chen zhugs la sogs/_/dpag tshad phyed lam song nas ni/_/log nas gal te za byed na/_/dge slong ltung ba yod ma yin/_/gal te sbyin bdag 
[\[35a.7\]]mang po yis/_/dge slong rnams la so sor gnang /_/sbyin bdag dag ni nus pa'i ngor/_/sbyin pa'i dus ni lnga po'o/_/mu stegs dge sbyong gis zhus nas/_/sbyin bdag de yis gal te 'dren/_/de dag dad pa khyad par du/_/spel phyir bza' bar bya ba nyid/_/mtshams nang der 'du 
[\[35b\]]
[\[35b.1\]]shes pa'am/_/the tshom gyis kyang ltung byed yin/_/gsum po la ni nyes med do/_/so sor ma 'dus 'dug la sogs/_/so sor nges pas thob pa dang /_/gtsug lag khang du tha dad za/_/sbyin bdag rnams kyis 'dod pa'i ngor/_/bkag pa yod pa ma yin no/_/nyi ma phyed kyi 
[\[35b.2\]]dus nas phar/_/phyi ma'i skya rengs ma shar bar/_/dge slong bza' ba za byed na/_/ltung byed 'khor los rnam par 'joms/_/bza' ba la sogs dge slong gis/_/sog 'jog byas pa zos na ltung /_/snga phyi gnyis su spyod pa yis/_/de ni rnam pa gnyis su gsungs/_/sngar spyod 
[\[35b.3\]]byin len byas pa ni/_/phyi dro spyod pa bkag pa yin/_/phyi dro byin len byas pa ni/_/thun tshod 'das pa rung ma yin/_/chos gos lde mig kyog po dang /_/lhung bzed lhung bzed dman pa la'ang /_/lag pa 'bags pas mi reg ste/_/tshul de'ang rkyen dbang ma gtogs so/_/sdom 
[\[35b.4\]]brtson gang zhig bza' ba sogs/_/byin len ma byas za byed na/_/de yang bslab pa zad byed de/_/chu dang so shing ma gtogs so/_/gang zhig ma tshos tshos pa 'dra/_/bsod snyoms pa yis rnyed pa na/_/sra ba nyid ni sum cha bangs/_/chos la byin len byas pa rung /_/nya dang sha dang 
[\[35b.5\]]'bras bu sogs/_/kha dog yongs su gyur pa na/_/ba 'o la sogs lan gsum khol/_/byin len bya bar rung ba nyid/_/reg nas byin len mi bya ste/_/nyam nga'i dus na bkag pa med/_/'dab ma can dang so shing dag_/'o ma can ni yongs su spang /_/dge slong rnams la sbyin bdag 
[\[35b.6\]]gis/_/bza' ba gal te bor na ni/_/sgra mi snyan pa'i dbang byas nas/_/dus yol nas ni bza' bar bya/_/mgul du mid pa ma yin na/_/'di la byin len mi bya zhing /_/ji srid 'tsho bar bsgrubs pa ni/_/byin rlabs ma byas mi bcang ngo /_/tshang ni sog par mi nus pa/_/sbrang ma'am 
[\[35b.7\]]ti tam ka la sogs/_/de ni dor nas spyad bya ste/_/byi ba sogs kyis nges par 'jig_/lag pa gnyis ni lag pa dang /_/de dang 'brel la des byin dang /_/lag pa de dang 'brel pa yis/_/de dang 'brel pa dag tu yang /_/byin len bya'o mi bde na/_/rgyang ring na yang rung ba nyid/_/
[\[36a\]]
[\[36a.1\]]'di la cang shes glang po dang /_/spre'u yis kyang byin len rung /_/mar dang 'o ma nyid dang zho/_/sha dang nya sogs bdag nyid kyi/_/don du dge slong der gnas pas/_/blang bar bya ba min pa ste/_/nad pas ston mo'i dus su ni/_/slong bar byed la ltung ba med/_/
[\[36a.2\]]mi na ba yis slong ba na/_/nyes byas zos na ltung byed yin/_/bsod snyoms dag la rgyu ba na/_/kha rog 'dug ste spyod yul du/_/ci byin zhes ni 'dri ba la/_/ji ltar 'dod pa blang bar bya/_/srog chags bcas pa'i chu rnams la/_/phyi rol khrus la sogs spyod dang /_/nang du 'thung bar brjod 
[\[36a.3\]]pa yis/_/longs spyod nam yang bkag pa yin/_/'dir ni ltung byed ltung ba yi/_/rnam pa gzhan ni yongs btsags pa'o/_/rtswa sogs dag la 'debs pa rnams/_/gang 'dir slar yang ma bris so/_/longs spyod bcas pa'i khyim gang zhig_/skyabs bcas par ni 'dug pa dang /_/
[\[36a.4\]]'greng ba gnas skabs dge slong ni/_/rkyen dbang las gzhan ltung ba yin/_/gang du skyes pa bud med bcas/_/chags ldan 'dod pa spyod gyur pa/_/bud med skyes pa longs spyod pas/_/de ni longs spyod ces byar bstan/_/mu stegs rnams la rang nyid kyi/_/lag gis bza' sogs 
[\[36a.5\]]mi sbyin te/_/lta las slar ldog don du ni/_/dum bur byas te rjes su gnang /_/snod ni sa la bzhag pa la/_/snying rje ldan pas sbyin bya ste/_/de ni rjes su gzung phyir yin/_/nam yang gus phyir ma yin no/_/'thab tu bcas pa'i dmag tshogs la/_/nam yang lta phyir mi 'gro ste/_/
[\[36a.6\]]de lta bu yi rkyen dag gis/_/gyur pa las ni bkag pa med/_/gal te dgos pas song yang de/_/nub gnyis las ni phar mi gnas/_/yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]dus su nang du ni/_/gnas na'ang gang zhig 'jigs pas sam/_/rgyal po 'am ni blon po sogs/_/mdza' bshes tshig gis kyang 
[\[36a.7\]]rung ste/_/gnas pa na yang cho ga ni/_/rta dang glang sogs ched mi lta/_/dmag tshogs rnams kyi bkod pa dang /_/dpung dang rgyal mtshan mchog gi yang /_/rgyal po dang ni blon po yis/_/bshams pa dkrug par mi bya'o/_/de la dpung mchog gang du dpung /_/de yi bkod pa 
[\[36b\]]
[\[36b.1\]]spras la bya/_/gang du gnas pa'i rgyal mtshan mchog_/de ni rgyal mtshan mchog ces bya/_/khros pa'i sems kyis dge slong la/_/sdom brtson rdeg par byed mi rung /_/'dren pas bla ma'i dbang byas nas/_/brdeg par gzas pa'ang de bzhin bkag_/'dir ni sor mo gcig gis 
[\[36b.2\]]kyang /_/rdeg par byed na zad par 'dod/_/mgo bo khu tshur rkang dang lag_/rdo dbyug bong ba sogs brdeg ltung /_/phyags ma la sogs kyis brdeg na/_/de yi nyag ma ji snyed pa/_/de snyed lus la reg pa'i grangs/_/sdom brtson dag la ltung byed gsungs/_/de bzhin sran ljang drug 
[\[36b.3\]]sogs kyis/_/brdeg na'ang ltung byed kho nar brjod/_/de ni lus la ma phog par/_/'phangs pa yang ni nyes byas nyid/_/ma rungs gdul phyir ltung med de/_/gzhan par sngags grub mthu ldan gyi/_/dge slong dag gis brdeg pa dang /_/gzas pa'ang ltung bar mi 'gyur ro/_/
[\[36b.4\]]dge slong rnams kyis gnas ngan len/_/shes nas 'chab pa yang bkag ste/_/'jigs pas ma gtogs 'jigs gyur na/_/gnas ngan len ni bcab bya nyid/_/phas pham pa nyid gtsor byas nas/_/ji snyed btung bar brjod pa dag_/de snyed bslab pa'i gzhi 'di la/_/
[\[36b.5\]]mkhas pas gnas ngan len ces gsungs/_/nad pa ma yin dge slong la/_/nyon mongs bcas pa'i sems kyis der/_/nam yang zan gcad mi bya'o/_/ji ste byed na zad byed ltung /_/dus ma gtogs par me dag gis/_/nye bar spor byed smad par gsungs/_/me gsod pa dang 
[\[36b.6\]]bsro ba dag_/nyam nga'i dus na nyes pa med/_/skra sen spu dang ngar snabs dang /_/mchil ma lud pa 'jug pa dang /_/me lce me mar mur la reg_/nyes byas 'gyur ro dus ma gtogs/_/dkon mchog gsum dang pha ma dang /_/tshangs par mtshungs spyod bla ma dang /_/chos kyi don 
[\[36b.7\]]du nyes med de/_/reg pa'i dus su brjod pa yin/_/'dun pa dbul zhes smras nas ni/_/dge 'dun las ni nye ba na/_/sdom brtson slar yang mi dbul na/_/de ni ltung bas 'joms pa yin/_/bsnyen par ma rdzogs gang zag dang /_/nub gnyis lhan cig nyal rung ste/_/de las 
[\[37a\]]
[\[37a.1\]]phar ni nub gsum sogs/_/ma yin gal te nyal na ltung /_/thams cad g.yo zhing thams cad bskor/_/gnas khang bskor zhing g.yogs pa bzhin/_/phal cher g.yogs shing kun nas bskor/_/ngos bzhi pa yi khyams bzhin brtag_/thams cad 
[\[37a.2\]]g.yogs shing phal cher bskor/_/ngos gsum pa yi khyams bzhin te/_/phal cher g.yogs pa'i khang pa dang /_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]bzhin te rim bzhin no/_/de ltar khyim ni bzhi po ru/_/sdom brtson lhan cig nyal byed na/_/nyi ma gsum pa'i skya rengs 'od/_/'char tshe 
[\[37a.3\]]gsal por ltung ba yin/_/sngo dang ser dang dmar po ste/_/skya rengs rnam pa gsum du 'dod/_/sngon po nyid tshe'ang ltung 'gyur na/_/gzhan dag tshe na smos ci dgos/_/gang du sgra don so sor go_/khyams dang khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]la sogs par/_/de 'dra der yang sdom brtson ni/_/
[\[37a.4\]]nyal du rung ba ma yin no/_/gnang ba dang bcas gnas su ni/_/nad pa dag la rjes gnang ste/_/de yi nad g.yog dag kyang ngo /_/de las gzhan gyis nam yang min/_/stobs chung mi na ba la ni/_/nyin mo nyal ba bkag pa yin/_/bsam gtan klog dang ldan pa la/_/
[\[37a.5\]]yud tsam rjes su gnang ba yin/_/gnyis sam gsum po dag gis kyang /_/khri gcig la ni nyal mi bya/_/rgya chen yin na gos sogs kyis/_/bar chad nyal ba rjes su gnang /_/mun khung dag tu sa la mgo_/gtugs nas bla mar phyag mi bya/_/der ni tshig gi bya ba'am/_/
[\[37a.6\]]yid kyis nges par phyag bya'o/_/dge tshul dang ni lhan cig tu/_/lam du nyal tshe gnyid mi log_/mi nus ldang blos skyil krung dag_/bcings te cung zad gnyid log rung /_/ma nus pa na nyes pa med/_/thams cad du ni 'bad pa yis/_/dge tshul dag ni bskyang bar bya/_/
[\[37a.7\]]sangs rgyas ljon shing myu gu ste/_/de ni dgra bcom sgo nga yin/_/dge slong blo gros dman gang dang /_/dge tshul gang yang 'di skad chos/_/'dod pas bar du gcod bstan mod/_/bar du gcod pa min ston na/_/de 'dir lan gsum par bzlog ste/_/'on kyang lta de mi gtong na/_/
[\[37b\]]
[\[37b.1\]]'di las lhag sdig yod min pas/_/de yang rgyang ring spang bar bya/_/nyams pa de dang smra ba dang /_/nam yang yang dag smra mi bya/_/gnas dang longs spyod la sogs kyang /_/mi bya gal te byed na ltung /_/yang dag smra ba kha ton yin/_/smra ba klog pa yin par 'dod/_/gnas sa 'dug 
[\[37b.2\]]sa gcig tu ste/_/longs spyod chos dang zang zing ngo /_/lta ba bzlog pa'i ched du 'am/_/nye du'am nad pa yin na ni/_/smra la sogs pa rjes su gnang /_/longs spyod nam yang ma yin no/_/chos gos gsar pa rnyed pa na/_/kha dog ngan par byas te bcang /_/de yang dkar po gsar par 
[\[37b.3\]]dgongs/_/kha dog ngan pa rnam pa gsum/_/rang bzhin dang ni bcos ma yin/_/sngon po dang ni btsag tu grags/_/me tog 'bras bu 'dab ma dang /_/shun pa'i khu ba ngur smrig 'dod/_/mdog [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]gzhan ni ma yin no/_/drag po sbrul gyi gdug pa yi/_/dug la rtag tu reg 
[\[37b.4\]]bla yi/_/rin po che la reg pa dang /_/rin chen smos pa de lta min/_/nor bu mu tig byi ru sogs/_/rin chen de ru smos pa yang /_/g.yul 'gyed yan lag mtshon sogs dang /_/rol mo'i yan lag rnga zlum sogs/_/gtsug lag khang dang skyed mos tshal/_/thams cad 
[\[37b.5\]]nang gir brjod pa yin/_/nang ma yin par slar yang ni/_/phyi rol nye 'khor yin par gsungs/_/phug pa'i nor bu mu tig sogs/_/reg pa la ni ltung byed de/_/ma phug pa la reg pa dang /_/mching bu dag la nyes byas nyid/_/pi wang gling bu rnga zlum sogs/_/mtshon cha 
[\[37b.6\]]mda' dang gzhu sogs kyang /_/yan lag tshang dang ma tshang la/_/reg pa ltung byed nyes byas yin/_/gzhan 'dir go cha la sogs la/_/dge slong reg na nyes byas yin/_/mda' dang gzhu sogs chas pa yi/_/mtshon cha la reg smos ci dgos/_/sku gdung dang bcas mchod 
[\[37b.7\]]rten dang /_/sku gzugs la reg ltung byed de/_/sku gdung med la gang reg pa/_/de la nyes byas mda' yis reg_/gar dang rol mo glu sogs dag_/mig dang rna ba'i yul du bkag_/'thab mo bre mo'i gtam sogs kyis/_/nyes zhi mi byed 'ba' zhig zad/_/gar 
[\[38a\]]
[\[38a.1\]]dang glu sogs chags pa yis/_/gang zhig mchod rten phyag byed na/_/der 'gro gom pa re rer yang /_/nyes byas phung po re re sogs/_/de phyir srid pa zad 'dod pa'i/_/dge slong rnams kyis gzugs sdug dang /_/sgra snyan la sogs chags pa yi/_/sems 
[\[38a.2\]]su nam yang mi bya'o/_/sa le sbram sogs smos pa gang /_/skyed tshal gang du rnyed pa na/_/rtswa sogs dag gis bkab nas ni/_/zhag brgyad bar du bsrung bar bya/_/su dag don gnyer byung na de'i/_/mtshan ma dris nas bstan par bya/_/med na dge 'dun nang nyid du/_/zla 
[\[38a.3\]]ba drug tu brtag phyir bzhag_/gzhan gyi kha dog mtshan ma dang /_/khyad par rtags kyis mtshon la sbyin/_/min na dge 'dun longs spyod phyir/_/de ni brtan pa'i dngos por bya/_/gal te gdod dbang byas nas kyang /_/de thob blo chung slong byed na/_/de la de nor rin gyis sam/_/sbyin 
[\[38a.4\]]bya skyed ni nam yang min/_/sdom brtson zla phyed nang rol du/_/dus ma gtogs par khrus byed bkag_/na dang las dang lam dang rlung /_/char dang de dag tshogs skyon med/_/na ba'i dus ni gang du de/_/khrus byas sos par gyur na'o/_/las lam dus ni sngar bshad bzhin/_/rlung 
[\[38a.5\]]du grags pa'i dus bstan pa/_/gang du chos gos sne'u ni/_/rlung gis zlog par byed pa'o/_/lus la char thigs gnyis phog pa/_/char pa'i dus su grags pa yin/_/rlung dang char pa 'dres pa ni/_/de dag tshogs pa'i dus zhes bstan/_/gzhan yang tsha ba'i dus zhes pa/_/
[\[38a.6\]]zla ba phyed dang gsum pa'o/_/chos gos ma lus bzhag nas khrus/_/byed 'dod lte bar ma sleb byas/_/nyes byas lte ba nub par ni/_/khrus byed de ni nyes par zad/_/chu bo pha rol rgal ba dang /_/rgyal ba sogs la 'debs byed rung /_/'ga' zhig tu yang chu klung dag_/zab mo 
[\[38a.7\]]brgal la nyes pa med/_/dge slong grur zhugs pha rol du/_/chu bo dag la 'gro ba'am/_/gzhan du rgya mtsho la yang rung /_/bshang gci sogs byed bkag pa med/_/'jug ngogs gang du bud med dam/_/rgyal po 'am ni de yi dmag_/skyes bu g.yon can khrus byed pa/_/de 
[\[38b\]]
[\[38b.1\]]ni rgyang [{]rings [,]ring [}]spang bar bya/_/'og tu gting ring byi zul lam/_/yang na steng du nya brgal lam/_/chu la rkyal ba la sogs kyang /_/rtsed mo'i don du mi bya'o/_/gal te rkyal ni slob pa'am/_/nad dang bral bar 'dod pa yis/_/shin tu dben pa'i gnas su ni/_/rkyal la sogs pa 
[\[38b.2\]]kun du spyad/_/shes bzhin rang ngam gzhan gyis kyang /_/sdom brtson dud 'gro'i skye gnas gang /_/gsod na ltung byed me lce yis/_/reg pa'i sdug bsngal nyams myong 'gyur/_/shes bzhin nyon mongs sems kyis ni/_/tshangs spyod dang ni bcas rnams la/_/'gyod pa 
[\[38b.3\]]skyed byed nges bkag ste/_/byed na ltung byed gzhan du min/_/khyod lo nyi shu ma lon na/_/khyod kyi bsnyen rdzogs gang na ste/_/mkhan po'ang tshul khrims 'chal pa yin/_/de ni mtshams kyi phyi rol yin/_/'gyod pa skyed pa'i don du ni/_/de la sogs pa smra byed na/_/de la ltung 
[\[38b.4\]]byed bden pa yi/_/skyon 'gyur brjod na nyes pa med/_/blo chung sor mo gcig gis kyang /_/dge slong rtse phyir g.ya' sgog na/_/ltung byed sme ba nag po dag_/ston pa la ni nyes pa med/_/dga' g.yengs chu la rtsed mo dag_/byed na zad ltung 'gyur ba yin/_/de yang pha rol tshu rol 
[\[38b.5\]]du/_/phar brgal tshur brgal byed sogs so/_/ma bsrungs bud med lhan cig tu/_/gal te nang ngam phyi rol du/_/dge slong nyal du mi rung ste/_/sgo bcings pa la nyes pa med/_/'dir ni legs dang nyes smras don/_/gang zhig so sor rtogs dang ldan/_/mi mo de ni bud med ces/_/
[\[38b.6\]]shes rab ldan pas yang dag gsungs/_/thams cad g.yog dang yongs bskor zhes/_/bya ba 'di yang snga ma bzhin/_/nyal bar brjod pa gang yin pa/_/glos ni sa la phab pa'o/_/sdom brtson gnyid kyis non dus su/_/reg pa'i rlung gis sbar ba yi/_/ltung byed me ni gang yin pa/_/
[\[38b.7\]]kun nas sreg par byed pa yin/_/gnas khang sogs su bud med dag_/gang na yod pa'i skas sogs brtul/_/srung byed bzhag nas dge slong ni/_/nyal ba la ni smad pa med/_/khyad par nyin mo nyal ba yi/_/cho ga yang ni 'di yin te/_/mdud pa bor nas rjes su gnang /_/gzhan 
[\[39a\]]
[\[39a.1\]]du nyes byas 'gyur ba yin/_/dge slong rang ngam gzhan gyis kyang /_/dge slong sngangs par byed pa bkag_/sems can kun la mi 'jigs mdzad/_/bla ma sangs rgyas la dmigs nas/_/sha za gnod sbyin la sogs pa'i/_/sdig can gzugs ni bzung 
[\[39a.2\]]nas kyang /_/de yi gzugs sgra dri rnams kyis/_/sngangs par byed la zad byed ltung /_/gya nom lha dang dri za dang /_/bram ze la sogs 'jigs pa yis/_/bzung nas khyod ni bsad ces brjod/_/gal te brda phrad nyes byas yin/_/phan par 'dod pas mi bzad pa'i/_/sdug bsngal 
[\[39a.3\]]ltung ba'i 'jigs skyed phyir/_/dmyal ba yi dwags dud 'gro'i gzugs/_/gal te 'jigs ston nyes pa med/_/dge slong gzhan gyi lhung bzed sogs/_/yo byad dngos po nam du yang /_/bzhad gad kyi ni dbang byas nas/_/dge slong gis ni sbed pa bkag_/dge slong chos 
[\[39a.4\]]gos byin pa la/_/gdeng med par ni longs spyod na/_/ltung byed gal te mdza' bo 'am/_/mchod 'os bla mas nyes pa med/_/sdom brtson dge 'dun lhag ma yis/_/dag pa'i dge slong skur 'debs na/_/ltung byed nyid du nges bstan te/_/phyi ma rnams kyis nyes byas so/_/lam du 
[\[39a.5\]]skyes pa med par ni/_/bud med dang ni mi 'grogs te/_/'grogs na de yi spyi bor ni/_/ltung byed gnam lcags 'bab par 'gyur/_/grong der sdom brtson nam son dus/_/nges par ltung byed nyid 'gyur te/_/grong gi lam phyed phyin pa na/_/nyes byas tsam du brjod pa yin/_/rang nyid 
[\[39a.6\]]snga phyi byas nas ni/_/lam mi shes pa bstan par bya/_/nyam nga'i lam du bud med dag_/khrid na ltung bar mi 'gyur ro/_/gang zhig rkun ma'i grogs dang 'grogs/_/de la'ang ltung byed kyis reg 'gyur/_/'di la yang ni nyes med [(]da[,]de[)]/_/gal te sngar bzhin song na'o/_/tshangs 
[\[39a.7\]]pa'i sho gam la sogs pas/_/'phrog par byed de rkun mar 'dod/_/grong dang lam la sogs pa dag_/gang 'joms drag po smos ci dgos/_/lo ni nyi shu ma lon 'di/_/gang phyir bkres skom la sogs gnod/_/mi bzod mi brtan lus blo'i phyir/_/bsnyen par rdzogs par mi 
[\[39b\]]
[\[39b.1\]]byar brjod/_/ma tshang lo la der shes bzhin/_/gang zhig smra yang bsnyen rdzogs na/_/rdzogs byed de dag zad par ldan/_/the tshom gyis kyang ltung bar brjod/_/gang zhig ma tshang tshang ba'i blos/_/lo ni tshang bar smra ba la/_/rdzogs byas ltung ba yod ma yin/_/tshang la tshang bar 'du 
[\[39b.2\]]shes gang /_/tshang la nyid smra dag kyang ste/_/the tshom gyis kyang smra ba la/_/de la ltung ba med pa nyid/_/de ltar 'dir ni phyogs tsam zhig_/mdor bsdus nas ni bstan pa yin/_/brtul zhugs dang bcas blo ldan rnams/_/shin tu nges dang bcas par bya/_/dge slong gis ni 
[\[39b.3\]]shes bzhin du/_/sa mi brko'o brkor mi gzhug_/so phag 'ba' zhig pa dag dang /_/kun nas bye ma yin nyes med/_/skyed byed skyed byed ma yin pa'i/_/sa ni rnam pa gnyis su grags/_/char 'bab rjes su gyur pa la/_/zla ba drug gis skye ba ste/_/skyed byed la ni 'dir 
[\[39b.4\]]nges yin/_/sa mang bye ma nyung na ltung /_/nyes byas 'gyur ro bye ma dang /_/gseg ma dang ni rdo ba dag_/lhag par bcas la mi skye'i phyir/_/ltung byed dang ni nyes byas su/_/'dul ba'i de nyid rig pas bshad/_/sa gzhi dang ni rtsig ngos la/_/phur pa brdeg na ltung byed 
[\[39b.5\]]yin/_/de nyid 'don dang gnon pa'am/_/'dam bskyod pa la nyes byas so/_/rtsig pa'ang de bzhin zhal zhal lam/_/chu ngogs rtib na zad ltung 'gyur/_/chad pa 'am ni gas pa la'ang /_/rko bar byed na nyes byas 'dod/_/chu 'gram 'dam ni bskyod pa la/_/'gro bar 'dod pas 
[\[39b.6\]]nyes pa med/_/rtsis kyi don du ri mo yang /_/sa'am rtsig ngos 'dri ba dang /_/skyed mos tshal sogs rgyu ba na/_/'jigs min dus su rung ba nyid/_/dbyar gyi zla ba bzhi las ni/_/mgron du bos pa khas mi blang /_/yang yang rtag tu ba dang dus/_/so sor mgron du bos ma 
[\[39b.7\]]gtogs/_/rtag tu mgron bos gang yin pa/_/gang du rtag pa nyid du 'bod/_/so sor mgron du bos pa ni/_/so so tha dad nyid la brjod/_/dus ni ji srid 'dod pa des/_/yang yang zhes brjod yang yang dang /_/mgron bos 'di dag ma gtogs pa/_/gsal por ltung byed nyid 
[\[40a\]]
[\[40a.1\]]du brjod/_/gya nom pa la bos bzhin du/_/sdom brtson dman pa slong ba na/_/slong ba'i tshe na nyes byas te/_/de longs spyod na'ang nyes byas so/_/dman pa la ni bos pa na/_/bzang po slong ba dus ji srid/_/nyes byas longs spyod pa la ni/_/zad byed ltung bar 
[\[40a.2\]]brjod pa yin/_/'o ma la bos sha dang zho/_/yang na 'o ma la ni mar/_/slong ba'i tshe na nyes byas te/_/zos na nad pa ma gtogs ltung /_/dad ldan yo byad phun tshogs shing /_/sbyin phyir gtong la 'jigs med pa/_/de ni yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]bsod nams phyir/_/dang du len pa bkag 
[\[40a.3\]]pa med/_/gang tshe bslab pa 'di la khyod/_/bslab par bya zhes dge slong gis/_/rab bskul de tshe lan du gang /_/de la byis pa sogs brjod smod/_/blo chung de ni ltung byed kyi/_/dbyug pas phog nas nges par 'gyel/_/'jigs ltung 'bar ba'i sgo nas ni/_/dmyal 
[\[40a.4\]]ba'i me yis g.yengs par gyur/_/byis pa zhes bya ngan sems can/_/rmongs pa mdo sogs mi mkhas pa/_/mi gsal 'dul ba'i tshig la ste/_/mi mkhas de yi don la'o/_/byis pa rmongs sogs tshig las ni/_/re re yis kyang bzlog pa na/_/dmyal ba'i mer ni 
[\[40a.5\]]'jug byed pa'i/_/ltung byed kyis ni dge slong ltung /_/de yis de tshig bzung nas ni/_/mkhas pa dag la dri bar bya/_/mkhas pa'i tshig gis bya ba ste/_/long ba rnams kyis tshad ma de/_/yang na [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]pa nyid du ni/_/byis pa la sogs tshig gis kyang /_/mkhas pa min yang 
[\[40a.6\]]bsgo ba na/_/bden don smra na nyes pa med/_/phan tshun mi mthun dge slong gang /_/glags skabs tshol zhing co 'dri na/_/bya rog nyan rnas 'dug na ni/_/de yang ltung byed yang dag gnas/_/gnas khang la sogs bsam gtan dag_/byed tshe sgra brda ma byas par/_/
[\[40a.7\]]zhugs pas sgra don gtso bo ni/_/ma thos sgra nyan nyes byas 'gyur/_/de ltar khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]skas dang sgo/_/lam du 'jug tshe tshul ldan la/_/ltung byed sngar bshad ji bzhin te/_/zhi bar bya phyir nyes pa med/_/gsol ba'i las la sogs pa yi/_/chos ni gtan la 'bebs 
[\[40b\]]
[\[40b.1\]]pa'i gtam/_/byed tshe gzhan la ma smras par/_/sdom brtson de bor 'gro mi bya/_/las kyi gegs su ma gyur tam/_/zhes bsams rab tu bsgo bar bya/_/de ltar mi byed dge slong ni/_/myur du ltung byed rgya mtshor ltung /_/ma gus pa la ltung byed de/_/dge 'dun 
[\[40b.2\]]dang ni gang zag gnyis/_/dge 'dun la ni ltung byed brjod/_/gang zag dag la nyes byas so/_/dge 'dun nang na dge skos kyi/_/tshig ni rab tu bzlog pa yang /_/ltung byed bla ma'i dge 'dun dang /_/rgan po gang zag gzhan la min/_/don med pa 
[\[40b.3\]]yi tshig la ni/_/rigs pas shad kyis sbyang ba yis/_/zlog byed smra ba po la 'dir/_/ltung ba med par gsungs pa yin/_/'bru 'am bcos pa'i chang dag ni/_/ku sha'i rtse mo tsam yang spang /_/'di dag 'thung ngam ster na yang /_/gnyis ka bag med khang chen yin/_/
[\[40b.4\]]kha yi nad la mkhur phug tu/_/bsrel ba tsam la nyes pa med/_/sman pa'i ngag gis 'thung bar ni/_/srog la bab kyang 'dod mi bya/_/phabs dang 'bras phye zan gyi tshogs/_/rnam 'gyur bye brag las skyes pa/_/'jig rten btung ba'i khyad par gang /_/de ni 'bru yi chang zhes bya/_/
[\[40b.5\]]sdong bu me tog 'bras rnams kyi/_/khu ba rnams ni yongs gyur pa/_/bcos pa'i chang du nges bstan te/_/sbrang rtsi rgun sogs las byung ba'o/_/gang tshe ma red nyams pa yis/_/myos byed nus pa zad pa na/_/chang gi rgyu mtshan spangs pa'i phyir/_/'thungs kyang de tshe nyes pa 
[\[40b.6\]]med/_/su lu ka dang zho ga chu/_/sug ta skyur po btsags nas ni/_/chu bsres btung ba'i don du ni/_/dus min par yang bkag pa med/_/'bru chang skyur por gang gyur pa/_/su lu kar ni yongs su grags/_/bcos chang skyur por gang gyur pa/_/sug ta zhes byar brjod pa'o/_/
[\[40b.7\]]ko tra ba sogs bkag pa'i phyir/_/'bru chang bcos pa'i chang zhes smos/_/ma red nyams la chang gi ni/_/dngos po bzlog phyir myos 'gyur smos/_/chang gi mdog dang de bzhin dri/_/ro dang myong ba dag gis kyang /_/gal te myos zad chang gi ni/_/dngos med nyes byas gzhir 'dod do/_/
[\[41a\]]
[\[41a.1\]]de ltar kha dog la sogs pa/_/gsum gnyis gcig 'thung myos nus zad/_/gsum gnyis de bzhin gcig pu la/_/myos pa'i nus med nyes byas rnams/_/rtsa ba sdong bu me tog 'bras/_/'dab ma yang ni myos byed dang /_/phabs dang zan chang bsres pa dag_/zos na 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/
[\[41a.2\]]sdom brtson grogs yod ma smras par/_/sdom gnas dus min grong 'jug na/_/de 'dra'i rkyen la nyes pa med/_/gzhan du zad pa thob par 'gyur/_/nyi ma phyed kyi mtshams gnas par/_/phyi ma'i skya rengs ma shar bar/_/dus ma yin zhes 'dod pa ste/_/dus dang dus min 
[\[41a.3\]]mkhas pas so/_/dge slong dus min 'jug byed cing /_/dus min dus su 'du shes dang /_/the tshom gyis kyang rnam par 'joms/_/dus la dus min 'du shes dang /_/the tshom za na nyes byas yin/_/khyim pas khyim du dge slong rnams/_/gang phyir mgron du bos pa na/_/
[\[41a.4\]]nam yang khyim gzhan dag tu ni/_/de ni de ltar 'gro mi bya/_/'phags pa'i tshogs la ci bder ni/_/bden pas gdugs tshod gsol smras nas/_/khyim bdag gis kyang gnang na der/_/song ba la yang nyes pa med/_/gang zhig rgyal po'i sgo gtan nam/_/de yi nye 'khor 
[\[41a.5\]]nyid dang yang /_/skya rengs tha ma ma shar 'das/_/bder gnas de yang sdig byed yin/_/grong khyer rgyal po'i pho brang dang /_/gsum pa btsun mo'i 'khor dag gi_/sgo gtan de dang nye ba na/_/nye 'khor zhes ni brjod pa yin/_/dge slong nam ni ma langs par/_/ma langs 
[\[41a.6\]]pa yi 'du shes kyis/_/grong khyer sgo gtan 'das pa la/_/sdom brtson nyes byas bskyed par 'gyur/_/mi yi bdag po'i sgo 'am ni/_/btsun mo'i 'khor du 'das pa la/_/ma langs de ru 'du shes sam/_/the tshom gyis kyang zad lhung reg_/cig shos dag tu 'du shes pas/_/
[\[41a.7\]]thams cad la ni nyes byas so/_/rin chen ma bsdus zhes bya ni/_/sba brtul ma byas pa la brjod/_/zla ba phyed phyed yang dang yang /_/so sor thar pa thos nas kyang /_/'di dag mdor ni gtogs pa dang /_/chos su rang byung gis bstan pa/_/ji bzhin rnam gzhag de byas dag_/ma 
[\[41b\]]
[\[41b.1\]]lus bdag gis da gdod shes/_/de ltar go byed tshul 'chos can/_/sdom brtson de ni ltung byed 'dod/_/rus pa ba so rwa yi ni/_/rang bzhin khab ral gang zhig byed/_/blo ngan de 'dir ltung byed kyi/_/chu srin kha ru ltung 'dod yin/_/de yis khab ral bcag nas ni/_/ltung ba de ni 
[\[41b.2\]]bshags bya ste/_/rigs pa'i khab ral byed de la/_/bya ba'i cho ga 'di yin te/_/snu gu dang ni chang ba yi/_/rnam pa de dang 'tsham pa'i tshad/_/khab dang gri gu yang dag ldan/_/de las gzhan du nam yang min/_/khab kyang zangs ma 'khar ba dang /_/ra gan lcags dang rnam pa bzhi/_/gri 
[\[41b.3\]]gu lcags nyid kho nar gsungs/_/de la rab 'bring tha mar 'dod/_/rab tshad sor ni brgyad shes te/_/tha ma yang ni sor bzhi yin/_/bya gag gshog pa'i rnam pa ni/_/gri gu'i dbyibs su bya ba yin/_/gang tshe grong gzhan 'gro ba na/_/dge slong rings par gyur kyang der/_/khab kyi bslab bya 
[\[41b.4\]]bsrung ba'i phyir/_/gcig tsam nges par khyer bar bya/_/dge 'dun khri dang khri'u sogs/_/byed na bder gshegs sor gyis ni/_/rkang pa la ni sor brgyad bya/_/lhag par dge slong mi bya'o/_/bde bar gshegs pa'i sor brgyad la/_/khru gang tshad du 'dod pa yin/_/de las lhag pa dge slong 
[\[41b.5\]]gis/_/bcad nas ltung ba so sor bshags/_/tho mtshams don du dge slong gis/_/dge 'dun mal stan la sogs la/_/shing bal la sogs bdal mi bya/_/bdal na de zad ltung bar 'gyur/_/ra bal lug bal ka sha dang /_/shal ma la yi bal yang ste/_/dngos po de ni bsdus nas 
[\[41b.6\]]su/_/ltung ba bshags par bya ba yin/_/steng du 'grems par byed pa gang /_/de 'dir bdal bar shes par bya/_/gang dang 'brel pas dge slong gi_/gos dag mdzes par mi 'gyur ba'o/_/srid du bde bar gshegs pa yi/_/mtho gsum de las lhag par min/_/zheng du phyed dang gnyis yod 
[\[41b.7\]]de/_/brtul zhugs can gyis gdeng ba bya/_/lhag pa bcad nas ltung ba ni/_/gang la bshags par byed pa na/_/tshul ldan des kyang khyod kyi 'di/_/bcad dam zhes ni dri bar bya/_/'di nyid dbang byas bzhi dang gnyis/_/chu dang zheng du bsngags pa ste/_/dge slong g.yan pa dag pa 
[\[42a\]]
[\[42a.1\]]ni/_/de las lhag na ltung byed de/_/dbyar gos ras chen zhes bya ba/_/srid du drug yin shes par bya/_/rgyar ni phyed dang gsum yin te/_/gang phyir ston pa'i mtho yi'o/_/bde bar gshegs pa'i chos gos kyi/_/tshad du chos gos mi bya ste/_/mnyam pa 'am ni 
[\[42a.2\]]lhag par yang /_/de la mtho ni bcu dang drug_/bslab pa nyams byed byis pa gang /_/mi zad ngan song gsum dag tu/_/des ltung 'jigs brgya bskyed pas na/_/de phyir ltung byed ces bya'o/_/ltung byed yang dag bshad nas kyang /_/de yi rjes thogs brjod par bya/_/rang gi mtshan 
[\[42a.3\]]nyid dbye ba yis/_/so sor bshags pa rnam pa bzhi/_/sdom brtson rang gi spyod yul du/_/nye du ma yin dge slong ma/_/de las bsod snyoms blangs nas ni/_/zos na so sor bshags pa yin/_/khyim gang dag tu dge slong rnams/_/gsol tshe dge slong ma gang zhig_/'di la mar 
[\[42a.4\]]sbyin 'di dag la/_/zas sogs byin zhes gal te smra/_/dge slong rnams ni zan za'i bar/_/sring mo ma smra zhes ni bzlog_/de las gcig kyang mi smra na/_/kun la so sor bshags pa yin/_/nang dang bar dang phyi gnas ni/_/sum char gdugs tshod gsol rnams te/_/dge 
[\[42a.5\]]'dun gyis ni gnas brten nam/_/yang na gsar bu kun gyis brjod/_/dge slong rnams ni gsol gyi bar/_/'di ni ma smra 'dug ces so/_/dge slong ma la ma bzlog gam/_/zhes ni phyi rol gnas pas dri/_/bar nas za ba rnams kyis ni/_/ma dris zos na ltung ba yin/_/phyi rol ba la nyes 
[\[42a.6\]]byas te/_/gal te gcig kyang mi smra na/_/dge 'dun gnas brtan la sogs pa/_/kun la so sor bshags pa yin/_/de yang ltung byed ji bzhin du/_/sdom brtson gcig gi mdun du bshags/_/dge slong ma yi dbyar khang du/_/bsko sham byed la bkag pa med/_/de ni de yi rang 
[\[42a.7\]]rdzas te/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]rgya chen 'gar yang ngo /_/dge 'dun mthun pas slob pa yi/_/khyim gang bslab pa'i sdom can gyis/_/dge slong bru bas nyen na yang /_/mgron du ma bos zan mi bza'/_/mar dang zho dang 'o ma dang /_/sbrang rtsi bu ram btung ba dang /_/nya sha sha dang sha skam dang /_
[\[42b\]]
[\[42b.1\]]mar dang bcas pa bkag pa yin/_/lo ma me tog 'bras bu sogs/_/dang du len pa 'gal ba med/_/dam pa'i chos ni ston pa na/_/mal stan spyod pa la yang ngo /_/sdom brtson khyim gzhan zos nas ni/_/bza' lhag khyim du byis pa la/_/dum bur bgos te sbyin bya ste/_/tshul ldan 
[\[42b.2\]]la ni de dag dga'/_/gang zhig 'jigs dang bcas pa yi/_/dgon pa'i gtsug lag khang gnas pa'i/_/dge slong phyi rol 'ongs nas ni/_/bza' ba len pa bkag pa yin/_/de thob der ram gzhan du 'am/_/gtsug lag khang du mi za yang /_/nags nyul nas ni ma gtogs 
[\[42b.3\]]te/_/dge slong la ni ltung bar 'gyur/_/de ltar byas na ci rigs par/_/kun dga' ra bar sdom brtson mdun/_/bdag la mi rigs smad pa'i gnas/_/byung ngo zhes ni so sor bshags/_/de ltar so sor bshags rnam bzhi/_/gang du ji bzhin 
[\[42b.4\]]bshad nas ni/_/da ni gang zhig bslab pa'i chos/_/mang po'i skabs son bstan par bya/_/sham thabs steng gi gos gyon pa/_/de ni shin tu zlum por bgo_/ha cang rtsengs pa ma yin dang /_/ha cang 'jol ba ma yin brjod/_/glang po'i sna ltar ma yin dang /_/ta la'i lo ma ltar 
[\[42b.5\]]mi 'dod/_/sbrul mgo'i gdengs ka lta bu dang /_/sbrul gyi mgo bo nyid ltar min/_/brtul zhugs ldan pas sham thabs ni/_/'bru yi phur ma ltar mi bgo_/stod g.yogs stobs kyi lha bzhin du/_/bgo bar yang ni mi bya'o/_/shin tu bsdams dang legs par bgo_/gnya'
