[\[42b.6\]]shing gang tsam lta ba dang /_/lta ba mi yengs pa yi ni/_/brtul zhugs can gyis grong 'jug bya/_/mgo bo g.yogs dang brdzes pa yis/_/grong du 'jug par mi bya'o/_/mi gzar ba dang gnya' gong gzed/_/cog pu de bzhin dkur mi brten/_/sdom brtson mchong sogs mi bya zhing /_/
[\[42b.7\]]lag pa mi sbrel phrag mi sprad/_/lus dang lag pa mgo bo dag_/bsgyur zhing kyogs pas grong mi 'jug/_/ma brtags pa dang ma bsgo dang /_/lus kun gyi ni ljid mi dbab/_/rkang pa yis ni rkang pa dang /_/long bus long bu bsnol mi bya/_/rkang pa mi rkyong mi dgug cing /_/mi gdang 
[\[43a\]]
[\[43a.1\]]byin pa bsnol mi bya/_/brla yang phan tshun bsnol mi bya/_/khyim du mnyam gzhag min mi 'dug_/lhung bzed la lta mthar chags dang /_/tshod ma mnyam dang byur bu yis/_/yongs su blang bar mi bya zhing /_/zas steng lhung bzed mi gzhag go/_/bza' ba nye bar 
[\[43a.2\]]mi stobs par/_/lhung bzed sngar bzed mi bya'o/_/slar yang 'dod pas tshod ma dang /_/zan ni phan tshun dgab mi bya/_/ro la chags pas mi bza' zhing /_/shin tu mang po ma yin no/_/zas la ma gus brnyas bcas dang /_/kham ni che min chung ma yin/_/kham ni ma bzas bar du 
[\[43a.3\]]ni/_/kha gdang mi bya 'gram mi bskyod/_/dge slong kha bkang smra mi bya/_/zas la sgra ni gsal ches min/_/tsug tsug zhes kyang mi bya ste/_/phu phu la sogs kun mi bya/_/dge slong zas la 'phyas mi gdags/_/tha dad phye nas mi bza' ba'o/_/'gram pa zas kyis mi dgang zhing /_/
[\[43a.4\]]kham ni 'phro yis gcad mi bya/_/nam yang zas ni za ba'i tshe/_/lce brkyang ba yis ma yin no/_/lag pa dag ni mi ldag cing /_/lhung bzed kyang ni byog mi bya/_/lag pa sprug par mi bya ste/_/lhung bzed bskyoms par mi bya'o/_/mchod rten rnam par byas nas ni/_/bcom ste bza' 
[\[43a.5\]]bar mi bya'o/_/drung na 'dug pa'i lhung bzed la/_/smad pa'i don du mi lta'o/_/lag pa zas dang bcas pa yis/_/chu snod dag la gzung mi bya/_/zas dang bcas pa'i chu yis ni/_/drung na gnas la gtor mi bya/_/zas bcas chu dang zas lhag ni/_/lhung bzed kyis ni dor mi bya/_/
[\[43a.6\]]khyim pas rjes su gnang ba na/_/khyim gzhan du ni nyes pa med/_/gad kha dang ni g.yang kha dang /_/dkan ngos lhung bzed mi bzhag cing /_/langs nas bkru bar mi bya'o/_/'bab chu drag tu 'bab pa yi/_/rgyun las bzlog nas lhung bzed kyis/_/chu ni blang bar mi bya'o/_/lhung bzed 
[\[43a.7\]]kun tu mi rnyed phyir/_/'dug la 'greng bas chos bshad min/_/nyal ba la ni 'dug pas min/_/'dug stan dma' mos mthon po dang /_/phyi bzhin 'ongs pas chos mi bstan/_/de bzhin lam min 'dug nas kyang /_/lam nas 'gro ba la yang min/_/mgo la zhwa 'am cod pan 
[\[43b\]]
[\[43b.1\]]thogs/_/phreng ba'am gtsug tor can la min/_/glang chen rta dang khyogs la zhon/_/mchil lham gyon pa dag dang ni/_/gdugs 'dzin pa dang dbyug thogs dang /_/mgo bo g.yogs pa la sogs pas/_/gnas pa la ni mi rung ste/_/dge slong dag gis chos bshad pa/_/nad pa 
[\[43b.2\]]la ni 'gal ba med/_/sdom brtson rkyen med langs nas ni/_/bshang gci dor ba'i bya ba dag_/mi bya lus ni bder gnas pas/_/byas nas 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/gang yang sdom ldan der gnas pas/_/chu 'am rtswa sngon dag la yang /_/bshang gci dang ni skyugs pa dang /_/
[\[43b.3\]]mchil ma ngar snabs mi dor ro/_/ci ste 'dor byed de la 'dir/_/nyes byas bskyed par the tshom med/_/de 'dra'i rkyen dag byung na ni/_/sdom brtson ltung ba yod ma yin/_/rkyen ma gtogs par mi gang las/_/lhag par shing la 'dzeg mi bya/_/kun mkhyen yab kyis bstan pa yi/_/spyod 
[\[43b.4\]]pas rtag tu gnas pa ste/_/'greng dang nyal dang 'dug pa dang /_/'chag pa la yang de bzhin te/_/gang phyir bdag ni mtha' yas yon tan 'byung gnas kyi/_/yab gcig bde gshegs sras yin des bstan spyod yul ni/_/chos kun rang bzhin de sems zhi byed rtogs par yang /_/
[\[43b.5\]]'dod pas bdag nyid phyi rol g.yo ba bzlog par bya/_/de ltar de dag thams cad du/_/ji skad gsungs pa'i bslab pa spyad/_/'di ni bslab par bya ba'i chos/_/mang po mdor bsdus bstan pa yin/_/de rjes bdun gyi dbang byas nas/_/zhi byed bstan pa de 'dir brjod/_/rtsod pa 
[\[43b.6\]]'khrug long rtag tu brjod/_/zhi byed de yi rjes kyi chos/_/thams cad gang zag khyad par las/_/zhi byed de ni rnam pa bdun/_/gang zhig lus ngag bsdams pa yis/_/gzu bo yang dag spro bya ste/_/dge 'dun mtha' dag dbus su de/_/tshul bzhin legs par 'grang bar bya/_/'dun 
[\[43b.7\]]dang zhe sdang gti mug 'jigs/_/rnam spang yang dag rtsod pa dag_/byed 'dod de la sbed pa dang /_/sbed pa yang ni shes pa'o/_/gzu bo rtsod pa mngon sum dang /_/gang zag de yang mngon sum brjod/_/tshul bzhin de chos mngon sum dang /_/ston pa yi ni bka' bzhin te/_/mngon 
[\[44a\]]
[\[44a.1\]]sum du ni zhi byed 'di/_/mngon sum 'dul bar brjod de gcig_/blo dman rnams kyis dran pa'i phyir/_/'di ni phyogs tsam bstan pa yin/_/gang yang dgu 'am bcu yis sam/_/lnga 'am de las lhag pas kyang /_/gang mang gis ni rtsod pa dag_/zhi byed de ni 
[\[44a.2\]]gang mang brjod/_/gal te gzu bo gang mang dang /_/gang la mdo sde 'dul ba yi/_/bstan bcos shes pa'i gnas brtan nam/_/gzhan gyis kyang rtsod ma zhi na/_/tshogs la dbul lo tshogs kyis kyang /_/chos lnga ldan pas yang dag bsko/_/sngar bzhin 'dir yang tshul shing brim/_/
[\[44a.3\]]gang des tshul shing bya ba ni/_/chos dang chos min ston byed gnyis/_/chos kyi tshul shing mchod 'os te/_/drang po dri zhim reg na bde/_/cig shos de las bzlog pa ste/_/yon po dri ngan reg bya rtsub/_/chos kyi lag pa g.yas su bcang /_/cig shos steng nas bkag ste'o/_/dge 
[\[44a.4\]]'dun yang ni 'dug pa na/_/nan tan ldan pas lan gsum par/_/chos kyi tshul shing dang po ste/_/gnas brtan smos nas rab tu brim/_/gal te 'dir ni chos kyi brda/_/dge ba rnams ni mang gyur na/_/tshul shing de yis chos kyi don/_/rtsod pa de yis go bar bya/_/de la skyor 
[\[44a.5\]]byed dge slong la/_/sngar bshad pa yis ltung bar 'gyur/_/slar yang cig shos de lta na'ang /_/de ni zhi bar brjod par bya/_/chos min las skyor dge slong la/_/de lta na yang nyes byas te/_/gang mang zhes ni grags pa yi/_/'dul ba 'di ni dpa' bos gsungs/_/'di ni 
[\[44a.6\]]rtsod dang 'khrug long las/_/brtsams pa'i 'dul ba gnyis par grags/_/gsum pa mi gdams rtsod zhi byed/_/'dul ba rig pa rnams kyis gsungs/_/mngon sum 'dul dang de bzhin du/_/dran pa ma smyos pa yis 'dul/_/mngon sum 'dul ba snga ma bzhin/_/
[\[44a.7\]]snga ma ji lta de ltar brjod/_/dran 'dul shes par bya ba ni/_/gyad bu nor la sogs pa'i tshig_/dge slong ma ni mdza' mos sngon/_/ston pa la zhus ji bzhin te/_/tshul 'dis dge slong khyed cag gis/_/gyad bu nor la dran 'dul sbyin/_/'di ltar yang ni sbyin bya ste/_/des ni 
[\[44b\]]
[\[44b.1\]]rgan pa'i mtha' logs brjod/_/gson cig dge 'dun btsun pa rnams/_/bdag la tshul min sogs kyis bskur/_/de ni tshogs la dran pa yis/_/'dul ba bdag la gnang zhes gsol/_/de ltar lan gsum brjod rjes la/_/dge slong gcig gis gsol byas nas/_/chos 
[\[44b.2\]]dang ldan zhing cho ga bzhin/_/dran pas 'dul ba de la sbyin/_/ma smyos 'dul ba gsungs pa ni/_/bye skyes bzhin du de tshe dag_/sems 'khrugs pa'am smyos pa'am/_/blo ni tshor bas gzir ba ste/_/des na sdom brtson rnams kyis bdag_/dman phyir yang dang yang du bskul/_/
[\[44b.3\]]ma myos 'dul ba dge 'dun la/_/gsan du gsol zhes lan gsum brjod/_/ma myos 'dul ba 'di la yang /_/gsol ba sngon brjod las kyis sbyin/_/de ltar tshul des rtsod zhi ba/_/ma myos pa yi 'dul bas so/_/ltung phyir rtsod pa dag la yang /_/'dul ba bzhi 
[\[44b.4\]]yis myur du zhi/_/yang dag khas blangs mngon sum dang /_/rtswa bkram de nyid tshol la ni/_/ltung bar bcas pa glengs pa'am/_/ma glengs na yang dge slong gis/_/dge slong mdun na tsog pu yis/_/'dug ste thal sbyar phyag byas nas/_/brjod de dgongs shig khyod kyi mdun/_/
[\[44b.5\]]ltung ba bshags brjod des kyang der/_/mthong ngam zhes dri mthong lan smra/_/phyin ci ma log de yi tshig_/phyin chad ltung la dga' min dri/_/dga' bar mi byed ces tshig brjod/_/thabs yin zhes kyang nges brjod de/_/khas blangs rtsod de zhi ba'o/_/phas pham la sogs 
[\[44b.6\]]tshogs mchog la/_/khas blangs 'dul ba gsol ba na/_/de ltar rtsod de zhi don du/_/khas blangs nyid du mkhas pas brjod/_/'di ltar yangs pa can du sngon/_/dge slong nag pos chos gos dag_/khyer song dge slong yangs can rnams/_/mnyan yod 'ongs pa las gyur 
[\[44b.7\]]te/_/mngon sum rtsod pas 'gro ba la/_/mngon sum 'dul ba yang dag bstan/_/mngon sum 'dul ba'i de nyid 'di/_/slar yang rab bshad bzlas pas so/_/phan tshun rtsod pas 'grel pa la/_/rtswa bkram lta bur rab tu gsungs/_/gnas brtan gzhan zhig song na'ang 
[\[45a\]]
[\[45a.1\]]der/_/'di ltar yang dag rjes bstan bya/_/khyed cag rtsod la mngon dga' zhing /_/slar ldog byed pa'ang dka' bar gnas/_/khyod 'dir legs pa rnyed min te/_/'di 'dra dge slong yin nam ci/_/gzhan yang khyed dang bdag cag la/_/nges par ltung ba gang byung 
[\[45a.2\]]ba/_/sbom po'am mthar thug spangs nas ni/_/de ni nyer zhi spro bas gnas/_/de la des smras mi nyan pa'ang /_/min na de tshe rang bzhin gnas/_/cig shos kyi ni phyogs la yang /_/de ni sngar bzhin brjod par bya/_/de yang gal te ma zhi ba'i/_/sems ldan de tshig nyan min na/_/
[\[45a.3\]]rtsod de nye bar zhi ba ni/_/rtswa bkram tshul gang nyid kyis so/_/ltung ba khas blangs pa la yang /_/slar yang log par shes pa yis/_/tshogs nang de slar khas mi len/_/dge slong me bzhi ji bzhin du/_/'dir yang de yi ngo bo nyid/_/tshol bas de 'dul dge slong la/_/sbyin zhes 
[\[45a.4\]]gzhan la phan byed pa'i/_/'gro ba rnams kyi rtsa lag gsungs/_/des kyang de ltar rgan pa yi/_/mthar ni kun la gsol bar bya/_/de la de bzhin tshogs kyis kyang /_/cho ga thams cad snga ma bzhin/_/bya phyir rtsod la dge slong gi/_/tshogs kyis kun gyi zhi ba thob/_/mi 
[\[45a.5\]]mthun byed min kha cig la/_/zhi byed de 'dir shes par bya/_/'di ni so sor thar pa yi/_/mdo don mdor bsdus tsam zhig ste/_/gzhi la sogs par gang gsungs pa/_/de yang ci nus brjod par bya/_/rigs ngan reg 'os ma yin dang /_/chang 'tshong khyim dang rgyal po'i khab/_/
[\[45a.6\]]smad 'tshong khyim du brjod pa ste/_/lnga po dge slong spyod yul min/_/grangs can la sogs [{]brdzun [,]rdzun [}]pa yi/_/bstan bcos rgyun du bden pa'i blos/_/klog par byed na de dag la/_/bsod snyoms dug tu 'gyur ba yin/_/mi bzad ngan song nad kyi sman/_/bder gshegs bstan 
[\[45a.7\]]bcos shes nas ni/_/sun dbyung ched du gzhan dag gi_/bstan bcos kyang ni blta bar bya/_/gang phyir kun mkhyen dag pa'i bka'/_/sbrang rtsi'i tshogs chen yod bzhin du/_/su zhig mu stegs kyis bstan pa'i/_/nim pa tsha ba'i ro myong byed/_/khri'u rkang pa ri mo can/_/mu 
[\[45b\]]
[\[45b.1\]]stegs rgyal mtshan nyid kyang ste/_/snod kyang ha cang mang ba dang /_/sna tshogs bkra ba spang bar bya/_/skyob pas na dang rgan po la/_/bzhon pa thams cad rjes gnang mdzad/_/chad pa'i las kyi dus dag la/_/gsang ba'i gnas ni bkri ba yang /_/khur gyi rim pas 'gro ba dang /_/
[\[45b.2\]]dbyig pa dang ni dra ba sogs/_/bsam gtan klog dang ldan rnams la/_/bcas pa gus pas sngon 'gro bya/_/mig sman mchu yi byi dor dang /_/de bzhin chos gos dri zhim dgod/_/sen mo 'chos dang mgo bo'i khrus/_/gdugs dang mchil lham khra bo bkag_/lo ma 'am 
[\[45b.3\]]ni smyig ma nyid/_/'di nyid sdom brtson dag gis gdugs/_/rtag tu grong gi dbus su ni/_/de yi yu ba bsgreng mi bya/_/yu ba gdugs kyi tshad nyid de/_/nam yang de las ring du min/_/kha gdang tshe ni nam du yang /_/rang gi lag la sogs pas dgab/_/sdom brtson so yi 
[\[45b.4\]]rus pa ni/_/bstan te bgad par mi bya'o/_/sgra dbyangs chung ngus ston pa yi/_/yon tan brjod bya dam chos kyi/_/dbyangs ni mngon par mi shes na/_/dben par slob pa bkag pa med/_/ston pa'i dam chos mdzes phyir te/_/nyon mongs bcas pa'i sems kyis min/_/dge slong 
[\[45b.5\]]dge slong ma la sogs/_/rnam pa lnga la ston pa yis/_/dbyar gnas pa dang dbyar gyi mthar/_/dgag dbye bya bar rjes su gnang /_/bslab pa mtha' dag blangs pa gang /_/dge slong dge slong mar shes te/_/bcu yi gnas la dge tshul nyid/_/dge tshul ma yang de bzhin no/_/gcig 
[\[45b.6\]]pu lam du mi 'gro zhing /_/chu bo'i pha rol rkyal mi bya/_/skyes pa la ni mi reg cing /_/de dang lhan cig 'dug mi bya/_/kha na ma tho mi bcab cing /_/smyan du 'gyur ba mi bya ste/_/'di dag dge slong ma rnams kyi/_/chos drug ces ni yongs su bsgrags/_/sa le sbram ni len pa dang /_/
[\[45b.7\]]gsang ba'i gnas kyi spu 'dreg dang /_/sa rnams brko bar byed pa dang /_/rtswa sngon la sogs gcod pa dang /_/byin len ma byas zas len dang /_/gsog 'jog byas pa za ba las/_/ldog pa zhes bya de bzhin gzhan/_/rjes mthun chos ni drug tu 'dod/_/gsung rab las ni 
[\[46a\]]
[\[46a.1\]]gsungs pa yi/_/dge chos bcu gnyis ldan pa yis/_/'jig rten mgon po'i bstan pa la/_/dge slob ma yi ming can yin/_/dge 'dun dag gi rgan rabs kyis/_/'bad pas tshes grangs dran par bya/_/gtsug lag khang skyong dag gis kyang /_/nyin re tshogs 
[\[46a.2\]]kyi thad du brjod/_/dus rnams yang dag 'pho ba na/_/zla phyed gsum gsum rjes la chad/_/lo la zhag res ma tshang ba'i/_/zla ba drug tu bstan pa yin/_/lo dang zla ba zla shol dag_/rgyal po'i bka' yis kyang ni bsngags/_/bka' btsan grags ldan shes rab can/_/dpung 
[\[46a.3\]]ldan de yi rjes 'brang bya/_/snod dang lag pa legs bkrus nas/_/brtul zhugs can gyis byug ris bya/_/ci nas nyi ma'i dkyil 'khor dang /_/mchod rten lta bur mi bya'o/_/sdom brtson lam du 'gro ba'i tshe/_/gang du lha yi gzugs dag mthong /_/de bzhin gshegs pa'i 
[\[46a.4\]]tshigs bcad ni/_/gus pas se gol gtogs te brjod/_/de dag phan dang gnod pa can/_/rgyal bas nyes byas dag tu bcas/_/des na bslab pa rjes bsrung bas/_/rtag tu dran par bya ba yin/_/lha yi gzugs ni bya ba ste/_/de yi mchod pa rang nyid kyis/_/bslab pa yang 
[\[46a.5\]]dag thob pa'am/_/sdom gnas dge slong gzhan gyis so/_/[(]dgos [,]dngos [)]grub ched du gzugs de la/_/gtor ma me tog la sogs sbyin/_/lha rnams rang gi gnas su ni/_/mi mthun pa dag mi ston phyir/_/'dus pa chen po'i mdo zhes par/_/lha rnams kyis ni rjes bsngags 
[\[46a.6\]]pas/_/sangs rgyas 'khor du gtogs pa la/_/ci nas gnod pa mi byed gsungs/_/mgon pos sems can thams cad la/_/kun du byams pa rab bsngags te/_/gang la sa bon de yod na/_/sred pa las byung sdug bsngal med/_/gang zhig lha ni gang dag la/_/gus pas 
[\[46a.7\]]mchod byed de la 'dir/_/'jig rten gnas pa gang yin du/_/srid pa'i longs spyod 'dod thob byed/_/gang zhig sangs rgyas skyabs song nas/_/rnam pa gzhan gyis gzhan bsten na/_/gsal por sems ni g.yo ba can/_/de brten 'bras bu ga la yod/_/yul 'khor dag gi 
[\[46b\]]
[\[46b.1\]]bsod snyoms kyang /_/'bar ba'i me mdag lta bur spyod/_/ngur smrig cha lugs tsam gyis ni/_/dge slong rtags sun 'byin pa yin/_/blo ngan dran pa'i don du ni/_/'dul ba la sogs bri bya ste/_/rang ming bdag gi ba dag ni/_/dgag phyir sbyin pa'i chos zhes bri/_/ma 
[\[46b.2\]]dad pa'am dad chung dag_/tshul khrims can gyi bsro khang dang /_/lus kyi dril phyi bsku mnye yi/_/las dag la ni rab tu bkag_/khrims ldan gyis ni khrims 'chal la/_/bsnyen bkur phyag sogs mi bya'o/_/'di ni nye gnas skal med do/_/'di nyid bstan pa'i tsher ma yin/_/
[\[46b.3\]]mkhan po dang ni slob dpon dang /_/pha ma de bzhin nad pa dag_/tshul khrims nyams kyang bsnyen bkur bya/_/de la med na nye gnas kyang /_/bsod snyoms dngos po phyed dag kyang /_/sdom ldan rnams kyis pha ma bgo_/de dag dka' ba byed pa'o/_/rtag tu phan 'dogs kyang yin 
[\[46b.4\]]phyir/_/yongs su spyad bya 'di dag ni/_/sus kyang kun du bzlog mi bya/_/rkang pa'i mthil gyi bsku mnye dang /_/khrus dang dril phyi sogs kyang ngo /_/gal te de la g.yan pa'i nad/_/byung na long bu man chad du/_/'drud dang skud dang rkang pa dag_/bkri ba la sogs pa yang ngo /_/
[\[46b.5\]]mchod phyir thogs pa'i se ral kha/_/rkang gdub dag kyang mi gdags te/_/rims la sogs pa zhi bya'i phyir/_/skud pa dpung pa g.yon par rung /_/de ni nad dang bral ba na/_/rtsig pa la sogs khung bur gzhug_/nges par sman pa'i man ngag gis/_/tsan+dan la sogs byug 
[\[46b.6\]]par bya/_/ma dad rigs can skye bo dang /_/ci nas dad spong mi 'gyur bar/_/tsan+dan la sogs byugs nas ni/_/dben par 'dug gis phyi rol min/_/gal te kha cig nan tan gyis/_/dris byug bsod nams 'dod pa na/_/rtsig ngos maN+Dal byas nas ni/_/de yis mthong ba gsal phyir mnam/_/nam yang dri ni rkang par 
[\[46b.7\]]min/_/gal te dad pa can dag gis/_/khas blangs rjes thogs de yi tshe/_/chu mang po yis bkru bar bya/_/me tog mdzes shing dri zhim dag_/rnyed de snom par 'dod pa na/_/mig gi ched du rnam bsams nas/_/bsnam gyis 'dod chags blos ma yin/_/gang su la yang mtshon cha dag_/
[\[47a\]]
[\[47a.1\]]ma dad sems kyis sbyin mi bya/_/sman rnams sba phyir lcags dag las/_/byas pa'i sgo lcags bcang bar bya/_/zangs lcags ra gan sa dang ni/_/shing ngam zha nye rdo las byas/_/rgya dag thub pas gang zag dang /_/tshogs la yang ni rjes su gnang /_/
[\[47a.2\]]gang zag la ni thod pa dang /_/yan lag drug gi rus pas mtshan/_/dge 'dun dag la kun rdzob pa'i/_/chos kyi 'khor los rnam par mdzes/_/gsal mthong rung ba mi bcang ste/_/lhan cig spyod pa'i tshul ngan bkag_/sbyin bdag dad pa spel ba'i phyir/_/bcos pa nyid do 
[\[47a.3\]]tshogs kyi yang /_/me long ngam ni chu sogs la/_/rang gi bzhin ni blta mi bya/_/lus kyi na tshod mtshan ma'am/_/rma dag lta phyir nyes pa med/_/rang gi lus skyes mi dgug cing /_/rdo la sogs pas mi gzhom mo/_/'dod chags me ni 'phel ba na/_/mi sdug chu 
[\[47a.4\]]yis zhi bar bya/_/glang chen rmig rjes lta bu yi/_/sa yi rang bzhin rkang khrus snod/_/'od ma ta la'i lo ma yi/_/bsil yab kyang ni rnam pa gnyis/_/lo ma yal 'dab ras ma dag_/sha na tha na bal gyi ste/_/sbrang ma la sogs zlog byed pa/_/rnam pa lnga ni rab tu gnang /_/
[\[47a.5\]]shing ngam skas la 'dzeg pa na/_/sham thabs mtha' sna bcing bar bya/_/lci ba'i khur ni dge slong gis/_/phal pa bzhin du bskur mi bya/_/so [{]mangs so[,]mang ngo [}]gas kyi yan lag mchog_/bshad par bya ba ma yin no/_/mgo skyes lag pa dag gis kyang /_/gyen du brdze bar mi bya'o/_/
[\[47a.6\]]skra la rdul gyis gos pa na/_/lag pa'am ni gos kyis kyang /_/mgo bo yongs su sprug phyi ba/_/brtul zhugs ldan la nyes pa med/_/skad gsang ha cang che chung gis/_/thub pa'i bka' ni mi brjod de/_/brjod bya dang ni mi 'gal bar/_/yul skad dus dang rjes mthun bya/_/gtsug 
[\[47a.7\]]lag khang gi steng ngos kyi/_/byang dang nub kyi phyogs cha ru/_/bshang gci bya ba'i khang pa dag_/bya ba yin te phal cher ro/_/gtsug lag khang gi nang gi ngos/_/lho dang shar gyi mtshams su ni/_/chu yi snod du nus ji bzhin/_/chu don gnyer ba rnams la sbyin/_/dge slong [(]dam [,]ngam [)]ni 
[\[47b\]]
[\[47b.1\]]khyim pa yang /_/rgan po sbrid pa byung ba la/_/tshe ring gyur cig ces brjod de/_/de dag tshe ring 'dod phyir ro/_/gsar bu la ni rgan po yis/_/a rog gya zhes brjod bya ste/_/gsar bu yis kyang rgan po la/_/lus kyis phyag ni bya ba yin/_/phyag mi bya dang mi blang dus/_/brtul ldan 
[\[47b.2\]]zas la 'khod pa dang /_/'og gi lam ni mi gtsang dang /_/gos gcig gis ni 'dug pa dang /_/ma mid na yang gang zhig gis/_/kha ni zas kyis bkang pa la/_/phyag mi bya zhing des kyang ni/_/gzhan la phyag ni mi bya'o/_/gzhan yang phyag bya'i yul min yang /_/spo mgu 
[\[47b.3\]]spyod pa la sogs yin/_/sangs rgyas dang ni sdom pas rgan/_/rtag tu dge slong phyag bya gnyis/_/bstan la dam par bya ba yi/_/dge slong phyag ni rnam pa gnyis/_/gcig ni dkyil 'khor lnga pa ste/_/gzhan gyi sgyid pa nas 'khyud yin/_/skra yi rang bzhin srad bu yis/_/lag 
[\[47b.4\]]'gro gzung bar mi bya ste/_/zhi bas rang bzhin mi 'gro na/_/de ni bskrad pa'i sbyor ba bya//_//bam po lnga pa/_/tha gu shin tu 'jam po yis/_/mgul par bcings te legs par dor/_/g.yang sa ma yin sa phyogs ni/_/'gro ba 'tshe ba med par ro/_/de bzhin 
[\[47b.5\]]gzhan la gnod med par/_/byi ba la sogs skye 'gro rnams/_/dal gyis bag yod rab bzung nas/_/'bad pas ci bder dor bar bya/_/bslab pa rjes bsrungs brtse ldan gyis/_/shig rnams dal gyis bzung nas ni/_/bsdus nas rtsig pa la sogs pa'i/_/bu gar snying rje can 
[\[47b.6\]]gyis gzhug_/rtswa sngon stug po'i sa phyogs su/_/rtsa shig dag kyang dor bar bya/_/lhag ma gzhan yang ma snad par/_/rang gi spyod yul dag tu dor/_/che ba phul chad chung ngu phyed/_/'bring po de dag bar ma ste/_/'bru mar snod du nyer bstan pa/_/rnam gsum de las gang 'dod cing /_/
[\[47b.7\]]lam 'gro chos kyi gtam nyid dam/_/mi smra ba yis 'gro bar bya/_/ngal bso'i tshe na tshigs bcad 'don/_/mtshan mo'i gnas tshe rgyud gsum pa'o/_/khru ni brgya dang lnga bcu brgyad/_/che dang chung dang bar ma ste/_/rnam gsum bas mthar shes bya zhing /_/gang la ji ltar rigs pas 
[\[48a\]]
[\[48a.1\]]kyang /_/chu rab che ba'i sa phyogs sam/_/chu yis khyab par yang ni rung /_/rjes mthun nyid kyis khyer bya'am/_/mi khyer ba ni [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]bzhin/_/gal te sdom brtson dge slong ma/_/rnams la 'dul ba ston pa na/_/yol bas bar du gcad bya ste/_/gzhan du 
[\[48a.2\]]'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/gal te rkun mas bor nas ni/_/yongs su ma bzung rnyed lta na/_/skye bos go byas khyer ba la/_/nam yang 'gal ba yod ma yin/_/kun dga' ra ba byed 'dod pas/_/gal te dge slong ljon shing btsugs/_/de yis lo lnga'i bar dag tu/_/gnas de 
[\[48a.3\]]mngon par blta bar bya/_/dge slong gis ni lhung bzed sogs/_/rtsod dang mna' ni dor mi rigs/_/khyim pa rnams ni sho be can/_/tshul ldan cho long ji ltar rung /_/gang du sdom brtson bud med dam/_/des kyang sdom ldan mthong ba yis/_/chags skyes gyur na des de yis/_/
[\[48a.4\]]spyod yul de ni myur du spang /_/dge slong ma ni nyer 'ongs na/_/bla ma lta bar 'dod gyur cing /_/dam chos mnyan par shes pa na/_/'dir legs 'dug ces brjod bya ste/_/so phag shing stan phyed tshal lam/_/gor bu dag gam srab mo yis/_/stan rung gzhan dag dge slong gi/_/mdun du 
[\[48a.5\]]dge slong ma yis min/_/dge slong rnams kyi mal stan gyi/_/lhag ma dge slong ma la gnang /_/dman pa 'am ni 'bring po ste/_/bzang po nam yang ma yin no/_/dge slong dge slong ma rnams kyang /_/phan tshun ltung ba bshags pa rnams/_/phan tshun du ni rang gi skyon/_/gang phyir 
[\[48a.6\]]sgrogs par ma nus na/_/ltung ba'i the tshom tshul 'di la/_/dge slong mas ni go bya'i phyir/_/dri'o bla ma'i 'du shes kyis/_/dge slong la ni phyogs gzhan du'o/_/gang tshe dge slong ma gang zhes/_/dge slong gnas su 'jug tshe dri/_/nad dang bcas pa ma yin na/_/shog ces des kyang 
[\[48a.7\]]brjod par bya/_/mdo sde chos mngon 'dul ba dang /_/rgyu skar dang ni nyin zhag shes/_/dgon pa'i phan yon 'dod pa rnams/_/grong nas rgyang grags gnas par bya/_/nags khrod dben par 'dug pa la/_/nges par chom rkun dga' skyed phyir/_/'bru mar la sogs nyer mkho'i snod/_/
[\[48b\]]
[\[48b.1\]]mkhas pas nye bar gzhag par bya/_/tshul ldan sngar ni bzo shes kyis/_/rang gi bzo cha spang bar bya/_/sngon ni sman pa 'breg mkhan na/_/'chang bar byed la bkag pa med/_/'phangs pa'i tshe yang dge slong gis/_/brtson la dga' ba dor mi bya/_/rab byung brtson 'grus ngo bo nyid/_/
[\[48b.2\]]de spangs 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/brtson 'grus can yang spyod bcas gang /_/yang dag rjes su bstan par bya/_/de med de la lta ba dang /_/dad dang sgom pa yod ma yin/_/man ngag dang ni bral ba'i sems/_/bsam gtan la ni sbyar mi bya/_/de sbyar 'khrul pa nyid gnas 
[\[48b.3\]]'gyur/_/bsam gtan gang yin rnyed par dka'/_/gnas khang gos dang mal stan sogs/_/dri zhim spos kyis bdug pa dang /_/me tog dri zhim bsam gtan dag_/byed na'o 'di las gzhan du min/_/lag gi bla yis shing dag gi_/mtshon gyi las la sogs pa yang /_/
[\[48b.4\]]shing drung la sogs mi mkho ba/_/de yis byin na nyes pa med/_/rab zhi kun spyod la mkhas pa/_/spyod yul gyur na 'bad pa yis/_/de ni lhan cig gnas par bzung /_/ma brtags par ni ma yin no/_/gal te chu dang me nang sogs/_/bud med gang dag 'chi 'gyur na/_/dge 
[\[48b.5\]]slong snying rje ldan pa yis/_/de 'don byed la nyes pa med/_/gzugs brnyan rnams kyi bzo dag ni/_/shes pas gang de 'dod pa na/_/sdom can gyis ni bya ba ste/_/'jig rten sems ni bsrung phyir ro/_/mkhar gyi sgo dang de bzhin du/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang ni khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]sgor/_/bud 
[\[48b.6\]]med rnams dang lhan cig tu/_/'dug dang gtam sogs mi bya'o/_/de phyir gzugs sogs rnam lnga yi/_/'dod yon rtag tu spangs nas kyang /_/rgyun du dran pa nyer gzhag gi_/rnal 'byor rnams ni yang dag bsgom/_/gang phyir lam 'di 'das pa yi/_/sangs rgyas phan bzhed kun 
[\[48b.7\]]gyi ste/_/sangs rgyas rang sangs rgyas rnams dang /_/nyan thos rnams kyi sngon 'gro yin/_/bla ma legs par rnam brtags nas/_/gnas ni cho ga bzhin du bzung /_/bla mas kyang ni yongs brtags pa'i/_/gnas pa yongs su gzung bar bya/_/phan tshun skyon dang bcas pa yis/_/
[\[49a\]]
[\[49a.1\]]nges par 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/gang gi gnyi ga dag kyang ni/_/phan tshun 'di ltar yongs su brtag_/khrims ldan nad g.yog byed pa dang /_/chos dang zang zing ser sna med/_/dus su 'doms pa'i bla ma ni/_/bla ma'i yon tan yang dag ldan/_/gnas 
[\[49a.2\]]pa'i tshul ldan shes rab can/_/dul zhing bzod dang ldan pa dang /_/bla ma la ni rtag tu gus/_/bsam gtan klog pa'i las dang ldan/_/phyag mi byed dang rang dbang can/_/mkhan po dang yang phrad pa las/_/rjes su bstan pa mi slob cing /_/de bzhin mi mthun phyogs la brten/_/
[\[49a.3\]]'di dag la ni gzhan des kyang /_/gnas kyi ltos pa med pa yin/_/'di ni ngan par shes byas te/_/slar yang gzhan ni yang dag gzung /_/gnyi gas ltos pa spangs pa la/_/gnas ni 'jig par brjod pa yin/_/gcig gis kyang ni ltos bcas na/_/slar yang gzhan ni mi 
[\[49a.4\]]gzung ngo /_/gang du dgos pas 'gro ba na/_/slar yang phyir 'ong 'dod pa yis/_/gnas de la ni go byas te/_/snga ma nyid las gzhan mi btsal/_/gnas med dag gi zhag lnga'i bar/_/tshogs kyi rnyed pa dbang ba yin/_/gang phyir gnas kyang de tsam las/_/lhag pa 
[\[49a.5\]]rung ba ma yin no/_/sdom brtson gyis ni dge slong dag_/dge 'dun gtsug lag khang dag las/_/mi dbyung chad pas gcad bya ste/_/dge slong gnas khang lnga dag yin/_/gnas ni bzlog par bya ba dang /_/klog dang gdams ngag longs spyod dang /_/yang dag smra ba dgag 
[\[49a.6\]]pa ste/_/chad pa rnam pa lnga zhes brjod/_/ci ste chad pa bzod nyid na/_/de la rjes su gnang bar 'os/_/mi 'os pa na gnyi ga la/_/de byed na ni 'gal tshabs can/_/ma dad le lo can dag dang /_/gdug sems can dang tshig ngan can/_/sdig pa'i grogs dang ma gus pa'i/_/
[\[49a.7\]]bzod pa blang bar mi bya'o/_/mthang gos dang bcas bla gos bskon/_/chu tshags snod dang yang dag bcas/_/bla mas legs par yongs brtags te/_/dge tshul yang ni bskrad par bya/_/gzhon pa la 'os yo byad drug_/dge tshul de la byin nas su/_/bla mas bskrad par 
[\[49b\]]
[\[49b.1\]]bya ba ste/_/min na bzod pa bya bar 'os/_/nges par de ni gnas med par/_/zhag gcig kyang ni 'dug mi bya/_/gzhan du lo lnga lon gyur cing /_/'dul ba'i don rig lam du'o/_/gtsug lag khang gzhan phyin na ni/_/de yi zhag lnga yis kyang ni/_/gnas kyi slob dpon 
[\[49b.2\]]btsal bya ste/_/de med par ni 'dug mi bya/_/yan lag dril dang mtshon sogs kyi/_/las ni cho ga ji bzhin du/_/bla ma rnams kyi bya ba ste/_/gnas pa rnams kyi chos nyid do/_/bla ma yon tan shes pas kyang /_/rang gi bya ba shes bya ste/_/bla ma dang ni slob mas kyang /_/rtag 
[\[49b.3\]]tu yongs su bsrung bya yin/_/klog pa yi ni slob dpon la'ang /_/mkhas pas nye gnas bya ba ste/_/kun spyod khyad par 'phags pa kun/_/gnas kyi slob dpon dag la bya/_/klog pa med par mi gnas shing /_/gnas med par yang ma yin te/_/de lta'ang gnas kyi slob dpon la/_/
[\[49b.4\]]sems ni rtag tu zhen par bya/_/sde snod gsum shes nyon mongs zad/_/lo bcu lon pa ma yin na/_/ston pa'i bka' ni mdun byas nas/_/des kyang gnas ni gzung bar bya/_/zla ba phyed dang de phyed la/_/brtson pas nyin re lan gsum du/_/ring dang nye bar gnas pa yi/_/gnas 
[\[49b.5\]]ster ba la phyag bya'o/_/gang zhig dpag tshad phyed dang gsum/_/gnas pas zla ba phyed na ste/_/de phyed de phyed gnas pa yi/_/lan gsum der gnas nyid kyis so/_/gal te dbyar ni phyi ma la/_/bla ma dus las 'das gyur na/_/so so'i rten gyis phan tshun du/_/bltas te yang ni gnas par 
[\[49b.6\]]bya/_/dbyar ni gnas par gyur pa na/_/gal te rgan po ma rnyed na/_/gnas ni gnyis pa med pa'i phyir/_/der ni gso sbyong mi bya'o/_/sdig pa'i sman gyi gso sbyong ni/_/dgag dbye byas nas yang ni bya/_/gso sbyong dag pa'i ched du ste/_/dgag dbye'i las kyang de dag nyid/_/phan tshun 
[\[49b.7\]]gdams pa'i don du ni/_/dgag dbye rjes su gnang ba yin/_/gso sbyong zla phyed re re yi/_/sdig rnams yongs su dag phyir ro/_/glo bur lhags dang gnyug mar gnas/_/mnyam mam lhag pa yin na yang /_/rjes su bzlas pa'i gso sbyong bya/_/kha ba so ga dbyar rnams kyi/_/zla ba gnyis 
[\[50a\]]
[\[50a.1\]]dang bzhi yi ni/_/mar ngo la ni bcu bzhi pa'i/_/gso sbyong sdom brtson dag gis bya/_/sa ga dang ni chu stod dang /_/de bzhin khrums smad smin drug dang /_/rgyal dang dbo zhes bya ba nyid/_/drug po'i mar ngo bcu bzhir brjod/_/de bzhin de las gzhan dag ni/_/bco brgyad 
[\[50a.2\]]bco lnga bar ni 'dod/_/glo bur lhag pa mang gyur cing /_/cig shos dag ni nyung ba na/_/glo bur lhag pa'i yon tan can/_/de dag 'dod pas gso sbyong bya/_/dge 'dun thams cad ltung bcas na/_/dge slong 'dul ba shes pa yis/_/dga' gcugs kyis legs go byas nas/_/rab tu 
[\[50a.3\]]'bad pas rim pa bzhin/_/tshangs pa mtshungs spyod mngon sum du/_/ji bzhin yang dag mthol bshags bya/_/de la bltas nas gzhan dag kyang /_/de bzhin mkhas pas byed du gzhug_/mtshams kyi mtha' ma mnan nas sam/_/la lar yang ni dkyil 'khor pa/_/de bzhin las ni 
[\[50a.4\]]thams cad la/_/mtshams ni bcad par rung ba yin/_/dge 'dun tshogs pa med gyur na/_/gang zag gcig gi mdun nyid du/_/byin gyis brlab po de yang ni/_/med na rang gi sems kyis brjod/_/de ltar byin gyis brlabs pa ni/_/brtul zhugs can gyi lhung bzed dang /_/chos gos 'gyur ro 
[\[50a.5\]]sems kyis kyang /_/de bzhin mgron du bos pa yang /_/gang gi 'khor ni 'dul ba yi/_/cho ga la ni mkhas yod na/_/de la gtad nas kyang ni gnas/_/byin gyis brlabs pa 'am ci 'dod pa/_/bslab la gus shing 'jigs bcas la/_/ltung ba brtag pa'ang de dang 'dra/_/dgag dbye de ltar 
[\[50a.6\]]shes bya ste/_/de yang lnga yi tshogs kyis so/_/mthun par bsams pa bzang po yis/_/dgag dbye bya ba kho na ste/_/dga' bas tshangs pa mtshungs spyod pa/_/gzhan la so sor gus pas so/_/nyin zhag bdun nam brgyad dang ni/_/bcu rnams kyis ni dgag dbye dag_/byed pa na ni 
[\[50a.7\]]'khon can rnams/_/rab tu 'bad pas sbyang bar bya/_/las kyi phyir ni dkyil 'khor pa/_/bya'o mtha' mtshams bkrol nas kyang /_/mtshams gcig tu ni ma 'dres pas/_/tshogs kyi las ni bya bar brjod/_/nyi shu yang na bcu'am lnga/_/dge slong bzhi zhes bya ba rnams/_/tshogs 
[\[50b\]]
[\[50b.1\]]kyi phrin las 'di lta ste/_/'di dag tshogs zhes bya bar grags/_/dge 'dun kha skong sangs rgyas kyis/_/bya ba'i las ni 'ga' yang med/_/sangs rgyas dkon mchog dge 'dun las/_/tha dad de yi las de'i min/_/nyi shu yis ni dbyung ba ste/_/bcu yi tshogs kyis bsnyen par 
[\[50b.2\]]rdzogs/_/lnga rnams kyis ni dgag dbye ste/_/bzhi po rnams kyis gso sbyong ngo /_/brjod pa la sogs cho ga bzhin/_/las ni gang du byed pa de/_/gnas zhes bya bar brjod pa ste/_/bzlog pa la ni gnas mi brtag_/chos smra ba ni ngag nyid de/_/dge slong mi smra'i yid ma bkag_/
[\[50b.3\]]'di ni mu stegs skye bo dag_/blun po sgrub pa spang phyir ro/_/mthong dang thos dang dogs pa yis/_/dngos po kun gyi bya ba ste/_/dbyar gnas pa na brtul zhugs can/_/gnas gsum gyis ni dgag dbye bya/_/bar chad ma gtogs gang du yang /_/dgag dbye ma byas mi 'gro ste/_/me 
[\[50b.4\]]sogs bar chad byung ba na/_/rgya mtsho yang ni brgal bar bya/_/glags tshol phung khrol chom rkun dang /_/rgyal po me yi bla ma rnams/_/'di dag gus pas nyan na ni/_/'dul ba mi bshad mi bya'o/_/dad pa can ni nor med dang /_/dad chung rnams la de yod pa/_/rab tu mnyan phyir 'bad pa la/_/
[\[50b.5\]]bshad par bya'o ltung ba med/_/mdo sde la sogs mkhas pa rnams/_/gzhag par bya bar rab tu grags/_/de dag las la mi bsko ste/_/'di nyid de dag nyid kyi las/_/mdo sde chos mngon 'dul ba rnams/_/'dzin dang tshogs la klog pa dang /_/nad pa tshogs kyi rgan po ste/_/drug 
[\[50b.6\]]po dag las mi byed pa'o/_/dge slong mdo sogs mkhas pa la/_/lam phyed 'ongs pa thos pa na/_/ji srid dpag tshad phyed dang gsum/_/ba dan bsgreng sogs sngon 'gro bas/_/gaN+DI brdungs te dge 'dun gyis/_/rang gi nus sbyar bsu bar bya/_/'ongs nas dga' bas rnyed pa yang /_/
[\[50b.7\]]der dbul tshogs kyis kyang ni thob/_/gnas khang 'dug stan mal stan rnams/_/de dag gus pas rtag tu dbul/_/nye gnas la yang las su bsko/_/mi dbab par ni rab tu bshad/_/bslab pa 'cha' ba bder gshegs te/_/skrod pa la ni dge 'dun dbang /_/dge 'dun gyis 
[\[51a\]]
[\[51a.1\]]ni khros pa yis/_/rab byung bslab pa mi bca'o/_/dge 'dun bsam pa tha dad pas/_/byas na yang ni ma byas yin/_/gnyis med gsungs pa'i bslab pa ni/_/sus kyang gzhan du bya mi nus/_/gro zhun zla ba'i tshes gcig nas/_/snga ma'i dbyar de gtso bo 
[\[51a.2\]]yin/_/de nyid khrums stod zla ba yi/_/tshes gcig nas ni phyi mar grags/_/zla gsum dbyar ni 'gro ba yi/_/spyod pa dge slong rnams kyis spang /_/bya rnams kyis kyang rang gi gnas/_/dbyar gyi dus su mi spong ngo /_/chu stod can gyi zla phyed nas/_/kun nas nye phyir 'bad pa 
[\[51a.3\]]yis/_/gtsug lag khang rnams sbyong ba'i las/_/byi dor la sogs bya ba yin/_/bco lnga la ni ma lus pa/_/'dus nas kyang ni gnas mal dag_/de stobs pa ni legs brtags nas/_/spro bar byas te kun nas bsko/_/'dun dang zhe sdang 'jigs rmongs pa/_/skyon rnams 
[\[51a.4\]]ring du spangs nas ni/_/gang zhig bstabs dang ma bstabs pa/_/ma lus pa ni shes pa bzung /_/de nas gtsug lag khang skyong gis/_/khrims su bya bar rab tu bsgrags/_/mkhas pa yis ni bde ba la/_/reg par gnas pa'i ched du bya/_/gang zhig spro ba gzhi med pa/_/bzhad 
[\[51a.5\]]gad rtsod pa ma yin no/_/gnas 'di dag tu brims pa yi/_/tshul shing dag ni long zhes so/_/gsol ba 'ba' zhig bya ba ste/_/de ring tshes ni bco lnga la/_/dbyar gnas pa yi ched du ni/_/tshogs kyi tshul shing blang du gsol/_/de nas rgan rims ji bzhin du/_/tshul shing brims 
[\[51a.6\]]pa'i rjes la ni/_/gnas khang la sogs ci rigs par/_/sbyin no gnas mal dag kyang ngo /_/dbyar ni nye ba'i dus na yang /_/dge slong gis ni 'bad pas brtag_/gnas dang spyod yul nyid kyang ste/_/yon tan grub par 'dod pas so/_/gnas khang na ni mkhas pa yis/_/
[\[51a.7\]]tshangs pa mtshungs par spyod pa dang /_/yon tan can dang thos pa 'dzin/_/snying rje can yid der mngon nas/_/thog mar ltung ba mi 'byin cing /_/byung ba slar yang 'chos byed pa/_/de la sogs pa'i rnam pa yis/_/gnas ni yongs su brtag par bya/_/sman dang bsod snyoms dag 
[\[51b\]]
[\[51b.1\]]kyang ni/_/gang du snying las chung dus rnyed/_/smad 'tshong tshogs kyis gang ba dag_/ma yin der ni spyod yul dag_/steng g.yogs can gnas dge slong gi_/mdun du dge slong tsog pur 'dug_/'di la dbyar gnas thob bya'i phyir/_/ji lta de ltar nges par brjod/_/sbyin bdag 
[\[51b.2\]]dang ni nye gnas grong /_/zhal ta ba dang nad g.yog smos/_/bdag ming 'di zhes bgyi ba yis/_/zla gsum dbyar gnas snga ma'am/_/phyi ma 'di la gnas bgyid de/_/ral dang grum pa bcos pa'i phyir/_/'di nyid bdag gi dbyar yin te/_/phyi rol dbyar gnas ma yin no/_/de ltar 
[\[51b.3\]]dbyar ni gnas pa dang /_/de nas phyi rol gzhan du gnas/_/nyes byas tsha sgo can gyi chu/_/ngan song thur 'bab mi brgal lo/_/dge slong gcig gi mdun song la/_/zhag bdun yang dag byin gyis brlab/_/dgos pa'i ched du mdun du 'dir/_/mi 'dug kyang ni nyes pa med/_/kun 
[\[51b.4\]]dga' ra ba la sogs bzhi'i/_/gzhi ni nye bar dgod pa dang /_/mchod rten mchod dang tshogs ston mo/_/chad pa'i las byin grogs nyid dang /_/dge 'dun lhag ma dbyung ba ste/_/dge slong dgos pa 'di dag gis/_/zhag bdun byin gyis brlabs nas ni/_/the tshom med par 'gro bar 
[\[51b.5\]]bya/_/'di dag las kyang dge slong ma'i/_/bya ba slar yang lhag ma yin/_/lci ba'i chos nyid ldan pa yis/_/de las dbyung ba mngon 'dod pas/_/dge tshul las ni lhag pa yi/_/dge slong sdom pa 'dod pa dang /_/chos dang rjes chos slob pa'i phyir/_/dge tshul ma las dge slong ma/_/
[\[51b.6\]]lhag ma'i bslab pa la slob ste/_/bsnyen par rdzogs pa thob phyir du/_/tshangs spyod nyer gnas 'dzin phyir ro/_/dge slong ma yis byed pa dang /_/yang na gsol ba la sogs las/_/gsang ste ston pa nyid dang ni/_/gang phyir mi 'dod ma yin na/_/gnyi ga'i tshogs la bsnyen 
[\[51b.7\]]rdzogs ni/_/khyim pa dag gi khyim song la/_/gal te yod na snga ma las/_/lhag par bu dang bu mo rnams/_/bag ma gtong len la sogs dang /_/pha ma dkon mchog gsum dang ni/_/slob dpon la sogs dgos sgrub la/_/zhag bdun brlabs sam lhag par rung /_/mtshan mo nyi 
[\[52a\]]
[\[52a.1\]]shu sum cu'am/_/nyin zhag bzhi bcu yang ni rung /_/tshogs la zhus nas 'gro bya ste/_/yul gyi dgos pa sgrub phyir ro/_/dgos pa khyad par 'phags pa ni/_/rnam par shes te zhus nas kyang /_/gsol ba gcig ni sngon 'gro ba'i/_/brjod pa 'di la tshogs 
[\[52a.2\]]kyis sbyin/_/bsod snyoms kyis ni phongs pa'am/_/sman yang rnyed par dka' ba dang /_/nye gnas 'ga' yang med pa na/_/dbyar ni dral yang nyes pa med/_/de bzhin mi rung sgrub pa yis/_/tshangs spyod nyams par byed pa ste/_/nye du ma yin bud med dam/_/rgyal pos mgron du gnyer na'o/_/de bzhin ma ning la sogs 
[\[52a.3\]]zhes/_/rang gi gnas su bud med 'ga'/_/chags skyes de ni bzlog dka' na/_/gnas gzhan yang dag bsten par bya/_/rgyal po rkun ma sbrul gdug dang /_/mi dang me dang me min dang /_/chu yi 'jigs pas dge slong dag_/song ba na yang nyes pa med/_/gang du sdig pa'i blo can gyis/_/
[\[52a.4\]]dge slong dge 'dun 'byed pa rtsom/_/de thos phrad pas 'jigs pa yis/_/gal te song yang nyes pa med/_/'jigs pa sna tshogs gzhan dag kyang /_/'jam pos bzlog phyir 'gro bya ste/_/ma song 'gal tshabs can du 'gyur/_/des dbyar dral yang nyes pa med/_/gtsug lag 
[\[52a.5\]]khang 'dir 'gro zhes bya/_/dbyar gyi dngos po 'di'o zhes/_/brjod de dge slong go byas nas/_/ma song na ni nyes byas yin/_/zhag 'dun yang ni lhag par ni/_/byin gyis brlab po dge slong gang /_/bar der 'ongs te song ba yang /_/byin rlabs de nyid gzhan yod min/_/sngar gyi byin 
[\[52a.6\]]rlabs de nyid ni/_/rgyun mi 'chad par gnas pa la/_/de stong rgyu yi tshogs pa ni/_/de las gzhan du mi 'thob bo/_/mtshams bcings rab tu rnam mang las/_/mdor bsdus tshul ni rnam pa bzhi/_/'di yang mdor bsdus dbang byas nas/_/phyogs tsam bstan pa brjod par bya/_/dpag 
[\[52a.7\]]tshad phyed dang gsum pa'i mtshams/_/chen po de ni phyogs bzhi ru/_/ljon shing chu bo brag ri dang /_/lam chen sogs kyis mtshon de bcad/_/dge 'dun thams cad tshogs pa na/_/dge slong gcig gis gsol ba bya/_/de bzhin brjod pa gcig las kyi/_/mtshams ni rab tu 
[\[52b\]]
[\[52b.1\]]'grub phyir yin/_/shar phyogs mtshams nas tshu rol du/_/gnas mal nyid kyi mthar thug par/_/grong dang de yi nye 'khor dag_/spangs te mtshams ni bcing bar bya/_/chos gos dag dang mi 'bral phyir/_/slar yang gsol ba sngon 'gro ba'i/_/las nyid bya'o tshul ldan dag_/
[\[52b.2\]]bde la reg par gnas phyir ro/_/chos gos gsum po med par yang /_/sdom brtson mtshams 'dir gnas rung ste/_/grong dang de yi nye 'khor dag_/ma gtogs gzhan du ci bder ro/_/sngar gyi chen po mi mkho na/_/chung ngu yang ni bya ba ste/_/gsol dang bzhi yi las nyid kyis/_/
[\[52b.3\]]chen po nye bar bkrol nas ni/_/chung ngu bcings nas snga ma bzhin/_/mtshan ma brtan po rnams kyis so/_/dkyil 'khor pa zhes grags pa der/_/tshogs kyi las ni bsgrub par bya/_/sngar bzhin chung ngu bcings nas kyang /_/de nas chen po bcing bar bya/_/gzhan yang der ni go 
[\[52b.4\]]rims 'di/_/mtshams bcing ba la gnang ba yin/_/dge 'dun thams cad shi srid dam/_/yang na mtshan ni gzhan gyur ram/_/bslab pa phul lam mtshams nyid kyi/_/phyi rol skya rengs shar ba dang /_/gsol ba sngon 'gro'i las kyis te/_/mtshams 'jig rgyu ni rnam lnga'o/_/ljon shing 
[\[52b.5\]]gcig gi phyed cha 'am/_/gnyis pa mtshams kyi phur pa ste/_/phur ba 'am ni shing gcig la/_/cha bzhir rnam par phye ba yis/_/bzhi char du yang thob pa ste/_/lnga pa nam yang ma yin no/_/'og tu yang ni dpag tshad dag_/phyed dang gsum mo steng du yang /_/ri yi steng gi shing 
[\[52b.6\]]rtse 'am/_/spyil po la sogs pa yi mthar/_/sbyin bdag rjes gzung don gyi phyir/_/ston pa nyid kyi sku gzugs la/_/sna tshogs rin chen 'od 'bar bas/_/mdzes pa'i rgyan spud rjes su gnang /_/rna rgyan dang ni lag gdub dang /_/rkang pa la sogs rgyan rnams ni/_/dgag go 
[\[52b.7\]]rmad byung 'di dag ni/_/nges par bud med rgyan yin na/_/skyes bu seng ge chen po ni/_/rtag tu rjes su 'dzin mdzad la/_/smad 'tshong gyon pa lta bu yi/_/bud med gos ni mi rigs so/_/tur+ya'i sgra yis 'dug khang du/_/ltad mo ba ni song ba'i tshe/_/sku gzugs dag la brgyan pa yi/_/mchod pa slar 
[\[53a\]]
[\[53a.1\]]yang bsdu bar bya/_/skye bo mang po 'dus pa'i tshe/_/ca co'i sgra yis g.yengs pa na/_/dus tshod brda ni sprad pa'i phyir/_/rnga 'am dung sogs sbyar byas 'dod/_/bcu 'am brgya 'am stong phrag ni/_/mdun du byas te nye bar bsgo/_/rnyed pa brtul can ci 'dod dag_/bltas 
[\[53a.2\]]te de skal rnam par brtag_/bgos nas de ni shi gyur na/_/de slar dge 'dun thams cad kyi/_/ma bgos na yang de skal ni/_/gzhan des rnam par bgo bar bya/_/skal de sbyin bdag kho na'i ste/_/gang la de phul de yi yang /_/thams cad du ni ma gnang ba/_/dge slong rnams la de mi 'os/_/
[\[53a.3\]]dge slong rnams ni mgron bos shing /_/dge slong ma yang mgron bos na/_/mchod ston tha ma der ni gang /_/rnyed pa rgan pa'i mthar bzhag na/_/gnyi gas kyang ni go ba'am/_/yang na sbyin bdag 'dod pas so/_/zang zing skal ba mnyam por ni/_/kun du skyob pa la yang yin/_/dbyig 
[\[53a.4\]]rnams rnam par 'god 'dod na/_/dge slong ma las cha gnyis te/_/'di la bsnyen par rdzogs 'dod pa'i/_/dge tshul la yang ci rigs brjod/_/de las gzhan pa'i dge tshul ma/_/rnams la yang ni sum cha 'os/_/dge slong dge slong ma rnams ni/_/skal ba mnyam par bgo 
[\[53a.5\]]bya nyid/_/gang du dge slong mang gyur cing /_/dge slong ma tshogs nyung na ni/_/de ni mnyam por mi bgo ste/_/rnyed pa re re'i grangs kyis so/_/'jig rten mgon pos 'jig rten gyi/_/yams kyi 'tshe ba zhi bya'i phyir/_/sangs rgyas sku gzugs grong sogs su/_/spyan drang bar ni 
[\[53a.6\]]gsungs pa yin/_/der ni chag chag byug pa dang /_/srang gi bzhi mdor sa gzhi la/_/sna tshogs me tog bkod pa yis/_/rab tu mdzes par bya ba yin/_/tsan+dan a gar ga pur gyi/_/bdug spos kun nas dbul bar bya/_/phyogs kyi sgo nas dri zhim dag_/dal zhing dal ba'i rlung gis 
[\[53a.7\]]bskyod/_/sgra chen pa Ta ha dag dang /_/'khar rnga ting ting shag sogs sgra/_/rgyun du 'brug sgra lta bu yi/_/sgra yi tshogs rnams rjes su bsgrag_/yang yang dal ba med par ni/_/nam mkha'i khams ni gang ba yi/_/grangs mang rol cha sna tshogs dang /_/gsal por dga' ston chen po 
[\[53b\]]
[\[53b.1\]]bya/_/rgyal mtshan rnams ni tshar du yang /_/dngar ro de yang rnam pa lnga/_/mkha' lding seng ge chu srin dang /_/khyu mchog glang po zhes bya nyid/_/rab byung chen po rnams kyis ni/_/khyim pa'i 'khor rnams la ni sbyar/_/kun mkhyen grong du gshegs pa na/_/de 'dra la sogs bsgrub par bya/_/
[\[53b.2\]]de nas sangs rgyas mdun bdar te/_/mthong bar dga' ba'i sems pa yis/_/klu dang gnod sbyin la sogs pa'i/_/'jigs pa zad par sel ba yin/_/rnyed pa kun nas thob pa dag_/tshogs kyi thad du btsong bar bya/_/dge 'dun gnas brtan blo ldan gyis/_/shes na rin thang gdab par 
[\[53b.3\]]bya/_/nges par nyo bar 'dod pa na/_/de la skyed par rung ba yin/_/rin ni thams cad ma gzhal bar/_/sdom brtson de longs spyod mi rung /_/dkon mchog gsum la su dag gis/_/gus pas chung ma sogs phul na/_/de la rin ni mi gdab ste/_/sbyin bdag 'dod pas gtang bar bya/_/
[\[53b.4\]]mi khyod glu gar rol mo rnams/_/byos shig ces ni dge slong gis/_/khyim pa rnams la bsgo mi bya/_/'di ni tshul ldan las ma yin/_/'di la tshul ni ji bzhin du/_/mchod cha ston pa mchod pa ni/_/ji ltar mdzes pa de ltar ni/_/bzhin [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]'bad pas byos shig brjod/_/mchod 
[\[53b.5\]]pa'i phyir yang mchod rten la/_/phur bu la sogs mi gzugs te/_/dang po rtsom pa'i dus nyid du/_/gzugs rten byed la nyes pa med/_/mar me 'og tu dbul bya ste/_/brtul zhugs can gyis steng du min/_/dge tshul la sogs gzhan yod bzhin/_/mchod bya'i steng du 'dzeg 
[\[53b.6\]]mi bya/_/gzhan dag med na dri yi chus/_/sdom brtson rkang pa dag bkrus nas/_/gus pas ston pa'i 'du shes ni/_/yid la byed pas rang gis 'dzeg_/dngos po gcig lhag brgya dang ni/_/gsum la sogs pa bya ba ste/_/gtsug lag khang gi glo dang 'bur/_/de yang lnga brtsegs 
[\[53b.7\]]dag tu brjod/_/kun du bdun brtsegs bya ba ste/_/de yi skar khung nang stegs can/_/dbus su ston pa'i khang chen te/_/sgo ni phyi sgo'i drang thad du'o/_/dge slong ma yi dbyar khang ni/_/sum brtsegs las ni lhag mi bya/_/gtsang khang skar khung nang stegs can/_/kun du lnga brtsegs 
[\[54a\]]
[\[54a.1\]]bya ba yin/_/sku gzugs mchod rten bder gshegs kyi/_/bka' yang mngon par bri ba ste/_/rnyings sam brlag na dge slong gis/_/slar ni gso bar 'os pa yin/_/gang du gang zhig sngar byas der/_/'di nyid de ltar gzhag bya ste/_/snga ma'i gzugs 
[\[54a.2\]]las chung ngu min/_/lhag pa nyid du bya bar brjod/_/gtsug lag khang gi 'du byed ni/_/sbyin pa po yi 'dod ji bzhin/_/chen po dang ni chung [(]du'ang [,]ngu'ang [)]rung /_/mkhas pas yo byad dang sbyar bya/_/mchod rten sku gzugs grib ma dang /_/maN+Dal yang ni 'gom mi bya/_/dgos pa'i ched du 
[\[54a.3\]]bde gshegs kyi/_/tshigs bcad sngon du byas te bgom/_/bsod snyoms dag la rgyu ba'i tshe/_/sangs rgyas la sogs brda yi tshig_/sdom can la ni su dag 'dri/_/de la gsal por go bar bya/_/sangs rgyas zhes ni brjod pa'am/_/yang na rkang gnyis mchog ces brjod/_/sdug 
[\[54a.4\]]bsngal zad ces yang na gzhan/_/bdag gam chos lam gang de gsungs/_/de bzhin dge 'dun dag la yang /_/brjod bya so so'i bdag nyid kyis/_/yon tan gang la de dag 'gyur/_/de ni de la brjod par bya/_/gcig pu mgron du bos pa la/_/dge slong ma rnams 'khod pa na/_/gang 
[\[54a.5\]]du dge slong zas kyi phyir/_/'dug pa'i stan ni dang por bzhag_/dge tshul gyis ni gral yang rung /_/de ru 'dug ste zos nas ni/_/gal te dge slong nus med na/_/dge slong ma yis yon bshad bya/_/grong rgyu ba la song nas ni/_/gar song de don zhal ta bas/_/len pa dge slong med pa na/_/
[\[54a.6\]]rnyed pa de yi skal ba bzhag_/gal te 'ongs na de yi'o/_/rnyed pa mtshams kyis bsgrub pa dang /_/khrims su byas la brten pa dang /_/ci 'dod dge slong dge 'dun rnams/_/sbyin bdag gis ni bsnyad pas so/_/mngon du ba dang dbyar dang gnas/_/so sor smos pa de bzhin te/_/brgyad 
[\[54a.7\]]po yang dag grags pa 'di/_/thob dang mi thob 'byed pas so/_/gdugs dang me tog rgyal mtshan sogs/_/bsnyen bkur dang bcas sdom brtson ro/_/dge slong rnams kyis yongs bskor nas/_/dur khrod du ni khyer bar bya/_/me chen rab tu 'bar ba yis/_/der ni yongs su brtags 
[\[54b\]]
[\[54b.1\]]te sbyang /_/gal te yod na mar dag dang /_/tsan+dan la sogs byug par bya/_/srog chags dang bcas rma can ni/_/'bad pas sa ni brkos nas su/_/sbas te de la rtswa dang ni/_/lo 'dab sogs kyis dgab par bya/_/lus can rnams la srin bu'i rigs/_/brgyad khri bzhi stong 
[\[54b.2\]]yod pa rnams/_/lhan cig skye zhing 'jig pa'i phyir/_/ming can de dag tshig par 'gyur/_/mthang gos dang bcas bla gos kyis/_/sdom brtson ro ni g.yogs te dbyung /_/lhung bzed chos gos gzhan la ni/_/btsongs te bgo bar 'os pa yin/_/de rjes mi rtag rnam pa'i tshul/_/
[\[54b.3\]]ston byed chos nyan sbyin bya ste/_/'di dag skyo ba bskyed phyir dang /_/mya ngan dag kyang bsal phyir ro/_/gang phyir 'dus byas thams cad ni/_/rkyen gyis gzhan du 'gyur ba ste/_/rtswa rtse rlung gis bskyod pa yi/_/zil ba bzhin du mi brtan no/_/gang zhig 'chi bdag khrel rgod kha/_/
[\[54b.4\]]dgang dka' der ni mi 'jug pa/_/mtho ris yang dag sa stong du/_/skyes pa de la yod dam ci/_/ji ltar sa gzhi brtan pa la/_/sreg pa'i me byung kho na bzhin/_/rang bzhin skyes cig skyes pa 'dir/_/mi rnams 'chi ba de bzhin no/_/gzugs rigs long tsho thos pa dang /_/tshul khrims 
[\[54b.5\]]la sogs rtag min te/_/rgyal po 'jig rten mgon gcig pu/_/gang gzhan 'dod chags bral ba yi/_/sdom brtson yang srid zad pa yang /_/kun du gnas pa ma mthong na/_/gang gis ji skad ji ltar bzlog_/tshe ni chu yi ri mo 'dra/_/de ltar mi rtag chos bstan nas/_/de yi 
[\[54b.6\]]dngos po'i yon yang bshad/_/rkang lag gdong pa rab bkrus nas/_/mchod rten bskor ba byas nas so/_/ji snyed 'dus byas de dag 'dir/_/mi rtag la sogs thub pa'i bka'/_/dran pa yang dag thob pa yis/_/gtsug lag khang du zhugs nas ni/_/lung bzhin skal ba mnyam 
[\[54b.7\]]por ni/_/shi ba'i yo byad sbyin bya ste/_/ro yi bsnyen bkur la sogs 'di/_/sras kyi gtso bo rnams la bcas/_/gaN+DI rgyud gsum mchod rten la/_/phyag byed tshe na gang zhig yod/_/tshul shing 'brim dang gsol ba la/_/gang yod de la 'os pa yin/_/gal te rdzas de 'god pa 
[\[55a\]]
[\[55a.1\]]na/_/brel bas der kun ma tshogs na/_/rnam par 'gyed pas cha gnyis ni/_/rgan po gsar bu dag la sbyin/_/de ltar byas pas de yi nor/_/ma lus legs par bgos par 'gyur/_/gzhan dag kha cig glo bur du/_/lhags kyang de la skal ba 
[\[55a.2\]]med/_/'chi ba po yis 'dir bzhugs dag_/'di snoms brda ni gal te sprad/_/de yis byams sems kyis bsngos na/_/de ni tshogs kyis thob pa yin/_/gal te byams sems ma yin pas/_/'tsho ba'i tshe na bsngos nas ni/_/gang zag gis ni blangs na yang /_/de la 
[\[55a.3\]]nyes pa yod ma yin/_/khyim pa rnams ni ltos byas te/_/brtul zhugs 'dzin pa de lta min/_/de phyir gang la ltos yod pa'i/_/dge slong de la de mi rung /_/gnyen la yongs su bsngos pa'i nor/_/'di ni tshe 'phos rjes la yang /_/gos dang lhung bzed la sogs pa/_/de ni 
[\[55a.4\]]tshogs kyi thad du dbul/_/rnam par spyad de dbul ba des/_/cho ga bzhin du bgo bar bya/_/rin chen la sogs yod pa na/_/der ni bgor mi rung zhes brjod/_/dge slong shi ba'i yo byad ni/_/dge slong dbang bar 'os pa nyid/_/de dag med na dge slong ma/_/de yang sring mo yin phyir 
[\[55a.5\]]dbang /_/dge slong ma ni shi ba'i nor/_/de la dge slong ma yin 'dod/_/de dag med na de la yang /_/dge slong rnams ni bdag por 'dod/_/dge slong med par dge slong dag_/khyim pa'i grong du shi ba na/_/de yi nor de der lus na/_/gang phyir gnyis kar 'dod na yang /_/cig car nyid du 
[\[55a.6\]]slong byed phyir/_/gnyi ga la yang rung ba yin/_/gang gis gang la dmigs te byin/_/de ni ci bder de yir 'gyur/_/gtsug lag khang gi mtshams dag gi_/bar du dge slong shi ba na/_/gang du mgo bo bstan pa de/_/chos gos la sogs dbang ba yin/_/bu lon blangs nas dge 
[\[55a.7\]]slong gang /_/'chi bdag khar ni zhugs pa na/_/de ni 'phags pa'i dge 'dun gyis/_/de yi nor las sbyin par bya/_/dge 'dun don du zhal ta bas/_/bu lon blangs te shi ba na/_/de kun tshogs kyis sbyin par ni/_/lung gis shin tu nges pa yin/_/gzhan du bu lon zhal ta bas/_/
[\[55b\]]
[\[55b.1\]]nges par 'di ni blang bya ste/_/rgan po rgan po go byas nas/_/dbang po dang bcas yi ger bri/_/lhan cig gnas pa rnams kyis ni/_/ba dan gos dang mal stan sogs/_/'dod pa'i 'bras bu 'dod pa yis/_/tshogs la phul na nyes pa med/_/gal te 'gyod pa'i me lce yis/_/de dag 
[\[55b.2\]]sems ni bsregs pa na/_/slar yang bzlog pa'i chu yis ni/_/ma lus zhi bar bya ba nyid/_/'di la rang bzhin gnas pa dang /_/gzhan dag gnas nas phyung ba shi/_/gcig gi yang ni yo byad rnams/_/rang bzhin gnas la rung ba yin/_/mdun du yongs su dag pa'i blos/_/rang 
[\[55b.3\]]bzhin gnas pa med gyur na/_/gnas nas phyung ba la yang ni/_/'di yis dbang thang bkag pa med/_/yi ger bri ba byas nas ni/_/gal te tshogs la phul ba na/_/shi nas de ni bgo bya ste/_/nor dang 'dra ba nyid las so/_/gtsug lag khang 'os der gyur pa/_/de ni de la mkho ba 
[\[55b.4\]]nyid/_/mtshon cha 'ching byed kun ma gtogs/_/rgyal po la 'os rgyal la byin/_/mtshon cha la ni khab la sogs/_/rjes mthun dngos po bya ba nyid/_/de bshig nas ni rtsa ba yi/_/mtshon cha gzhan du mi bya'o/_/mnyam par gzhag pas bde gshegs kyi/_/tshigs bcad 
[\[55b.5\]]gcig tsam bklags pa na/_/legs gsungs dam pa'i chos dag gi_/rnyed pa de la mi 'os min/_/thub dbang dam chos rnam pa gnyis/_/lung dang gzhan yang rtogs pa'o/_/lung de 'dzin pa'i don gyis na/_/chos kyi rnyed pa 'os pa yin/_/khrims ldan sngar ni dbyar gnas par/_/
[\[55b.6\]]khas blangs gal te shi ba'am/_/bslab pa phul lam gzhan gyur kyang /_/dbyar gyi rnyed pa 'os pa yin/_/bde gshegs sras kyi lhun po'i tshad/_/sku gzugs mchod rten bya bar 'os/_/zur bzhi logs mnyam mdzes byas nas/_/bang rim la sogs gzhan rim bzhin/_/de 
[\[55b.7\]]yang gnyis gsum bzhi dang lnga/_/'bras bu'i rim pa gdugs kyi tshad/_/mchod rten byi bo yon tan can/_/so so'i skye bo rnams la 'os/_/'di yi mthar ni ston pa yi/_/gdugs kyi tshad la nges pa med/_/brgya lhag ri bar bye ba snyed/_/mchod rten yang ni bya bar 'os/_/dman 
[\[56a\]]
[\[56a.1\]]pa bse ru'i spyod pa la/_/gdugs ni rim pa bcu gsum ste/_/de la char khab med pa dag_/bya'o zhes ni thos pa yin/_/logs dag tu ni slob ma zung /_/ston pa'i mchod rten dbus su ste/_/lhag ma rnams ni gral rim bzhin/_/de yi phyi rol 'phags min 
[\[56a.2\]]rnams/_/slob ma rnams ni phan tshun du/_/rab gus mnyan pas bsnyen bkur bya/_/dge 'dun gyis kyang cho ga bzhin/_/sman dag gis kyang nye bar bsdu/_/lhung bzed chu gtsang dri ma med/_/khrus phyir nad pa slong la sbyin/_/lhung bzed lan gsum bkrus nas kyang /_/sangs rgyas tshigs 
[\[56a.3\]]bcad bzlas nas so/_/sman dag byin len byas nas ni/_/tshul ldan nad pa la ni sbyin/_/byin len stobs pa med na ni/_/rang nyid len pa la yang rung /_/chos gos byin gyis brlabs pa dag_/nad pa la ni mi bslang ste/_/yang na de yis phul yang ni/_/dge slong rnams kyis bgo mi bya/_/
[\[56a.4\]]gang 'dir chags pas de len dang /_/sbyin bdag de ltar bskul ba dang /_/nges par 'gegs pa nyid la brtson/_/de dag gsum char skyon dang bcas/_/'phags min 'phags pa'i nad la yang /_/nam yang le lo mi bya ste/_/'khrul 'khor la sogs sbyor ba dang /_/sman pa dad pa chung bas 
[\[56a.5\]]min/_/sangs rgyas la mchod tshogs ston mo/_/nad pa gal te gso byed na/_/ngan zhing sman yang byin brlabs te/_/chos gos sogs kyis bya ba yin/_/de la nor med gyur lta na/_/gang yod gzhan las bslangs nas kyang /_/nus pa ji bzhin bsgrub bya ste/_/de yis dad pa skyed phyir ro/_/
[\[56a.6\]]tha na rdo gu me tog dang /_/phye ma dang ni bum pa'i chu/_/bsten nas kyang ni mchod pa dag_/de yis nye bar bstan bzhin bya/_/gang zhig don min nad kyis gzir/_/nor la yongs su chags pa na/_/'bad pas de ni blangs nas der/_/mchod dang brtul zhugs can ston bya/_/rang gi lhung bzed 
[\[56a.7\]]gos sogs kyang /_/de la byin nas de yi tshe/_/nad g.yog gis ni bde gshegs sogs/_/mchod pa'i las la brtson par bya/_/khyad par du yang nor bdag la/_/chags pas gang du srog mi 'chad/_/ci 'dod de yi lhung bzed sogs/_/yon tan ldan pa gzhan la dbul/_/dge 
[\[56b\]]
[\[56b.1\]]slong tshul khrims ldan yang sngon/_/lhung bzed la ni chags bzhin shi/_/rang gi lhung bzed nang du ni/_/gdug pa can gyi sbrul du skyes/_/sman dang sngags kyi sbyor ba yis/_/gzhang 'brum legs par gso bya ste/_/sman pa'i man ngag med par ni/_/sen mo sogs kyis gcad mi bya/_/
[\[56b.2\]]gang la slob ma la sogs pa'i/_/nad g.yogs med cing sman med na/_/de la sman dag sbyin pa dang /_/nad g.yog kyang ni tshogs kyis bya/_/mdze can gyis ni dge 'dun gyi/_/mal stan nyer spyod spang bya ste/_/rdzing bu ljon shing nye 'khor dang /_/spang sa 'dug sa la sogs so/_/khang 
[\[56b.3\]{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dang ni khyams kyi sgo/_/g.yos khang bshang gci'i khang par ni/_/mi 'dug dben par gnas bya ste/_/de ni tshogs kyis bskyang bar 'os/_/gtsug lag khang gi bdag por ni/_/shi 'am bcings sam ring song na/_/dka' bas kyang ni lo lnga'i bar/_/gtsug lag khang du legs gnas 
[\[56b.4\]]bya/_/lo lnga lon nas de drung [(]ge[,]gi[) ]/gtsug lag khang rten rnyed pa dag_/blangs te de ru gnas bya ste/_/bsrung phyir gso sbyong tha dad do/_/de ltar lo ni bcur gnas kyang /_/der ni 'dzin po med pa na/_/mal stan la sogs de byin nas/_/gtsug lag khang gzhan gnas par bya/_/'gro 'dod 
[\[56b.5\]]rnams kyis ci 'dod par/_/sgo bsdams rgyas btab 'gro bya ste/_/slar yang de ni 'jug pa na/_/de la 'dug gnas sbyin par bya/_/gtsug lag khang gzhan dbang ba'i dkor/_/dge slong gzhan du spyod pa bkag_/dgos pa'i phyir du de mthun pas/_/yongs su spyod la nyes pa med/_/
[\[56b.6\]]sbyin bdag rnams kyis mal stan dag_/gtsug lag khang du phul ba gang /_/mi 'dod kyang ni de lta bu/_/gzhan du dbul du gzhug mi bya/_/tshogs kyi gos ni gyon nas kyang /_/dge slong rang gi las byed bkag_/gos bkru ba dang tshon la sogs/_/de bzhin gzhan yang chen po gang /_/
[\[56b.7\]]char dang kha ba 'bab pa na/_/bla gab med par mi gzhag cing /_/bshang gci yi ni sa dag tu/_/de gyon pa ni bkag pa yin/_/gnas mal gnas skyong ma gtad par/_/mtshan mo'i dus su 'gro mi bya//_//bam po drug pa/_gtod pa na yang yul gcig la/_/go bar byas te 
[\[57a\]]
[\[57a.1\]]bzhag par 'dod/_/tshogs kyi 'dug stan la sogs kyang /_/rgan rims bzhin du sbyin bya ste/_/glo bur lhags pa'i ched du yang /_/dngos po gang yang kun tu gzhag_/gnas kyi dbye bas dgon par yang /_/rgan rims sogs la ci 'dod par/_/sa dang shing gi phug la sogs/_/
[\[57a.2\]]rim pa ji bzhin thob pa yin/_/dgon pa'i glo bur lhags pa la'ang /_/dngos po'i skal ba gzhag bya ste/_/chos gos la sogs sbas nas ni/_/gnas bya gzhan du ltung bar 'gyur/_/gang du sa gzhi khru do yi/_/tshad tsam la yang mal dag gnang /_/zhabs tshags can dang so shing sogs/_/pha gu dang ni 
[\[57a.3\]]sman gyi snod/_/kun nas 'jam por byugs te bya/_/gnas khang sogs su 'gro 'dod pas/_/sangs rgyas sras kyi 'dug gnas ni/_/gzhig nas 'gro bar mi bzhed do/_/gnas khang 'gyed pa la sogs dus/_/ma bsgo bar ni 'gro ba ni/_/slar yang rgan rims ji bzhin du/_/'dod kyang 
[\[57a.4\]]de la 'os ma yin/_/gsar bu rnams dang de mnyam par/_/gnas mal bza' ba 'bras bu dang /_/me tog la sogs sbyin pa nyid/_/bstan pa 'di la chos nyid do/_/nad tshabs che la gnas khang gzhan/_/ji ltar 'dod bzhin sbyin bya ste/_/ji srid nad sos gyur gyi bar/_/khas len 
[\[57a.5\]]mi byed na yang sbyin/_/gtsug lag khang gi phyi rol du/_/gos stan mthong na nang gzhug cing /_/rgan po na dang nyam chung gis/_/dge skos dag la go bar bya/_/dge 'dun mal stan yo byad dag_/me yis tshig gam chus khyer na/_/bdag nyid kyis ni rjes bsrung ste/_/nges par bskyang ngo 
[\[57a.6\]]grogs med na/_/dang po gang zag rnams kyi ste/_/phyi nas tshogs kyi de nas chos/_/de nas bde bar gshegs pa ste/_/'di ni dbyung ba'i rim par 'dod/_/go skabs 'byed bcug nges brtags te/_/gang phyir thabs kyis bsgrub pa nyid/_/don gnyer de yis dge slong la/_/mdo 'am 'dul 
[\[57a.7\]]nyid dris te'o/_/'dug pa'i thad du nye bas sam/_/de yis 'gro bzhin du yang rung /_/sdom brtson rang la blta ba yis/_/klog dang nye bar bstan pa sbyin/_/nye bar bstan pa 'dzin pas kyang /_/bla ma sangs rgyas dang por gus/_/rtse gcig nyal bas spangs nas ni/_/nye bar bstan pa lan gsum 
[\[57b\]]
[\[57b.1\]]bzung /_/nye bar bstan pa ston pa po/_/'di yis sgra ni mi thos pa'i/_/phyogs der de la gtad nas ni/_/lan brgya'i bar du yang ni bzla/_/'don dang rjes su 'don sogs kyi/_/dbyangs kyis thub pa'i gsung mi bsgrag_/yul skad dag dang rjes mthun pas/_/'don par byed la 'gal ba med/_/
[\[57b.2\]]sdom brtson gang zhig khang [{]bzangs kyi[,]bzang gi[}]/_/ka ba ljon shing rtsig pa la'ang /_/rab tu bteg na 'gal tshabs can/_/de yi phyir na de mi spyad/_/dpung rgyan dang ni gzhan min pa/_/so sor 'brang ma la sogs gdags/_/phan 'dod sngags ni bzla bya ste/_/gnod gyur gsang sngags ma yin 
[\[57b.3\]]no/_/lha gzhan phyag 'tshal mchod pa ni/_/gsang sngags phyir yang bkag pa yin/_/dkon mchog gsum la phyag byas nas/_/gzhan gyi gsang sngags brjod par bya/_/lhung bzed kha sbub byas pa la/_/stan dang ston mo bza' ba bkag_/dam pa'i chos ni ston pa na/_/dad ldan 
[\[57b.4\]]rnams kyis 'dus byas bsten/_/sa gzhi gos kyis g.yogs pa la/_/'dus byas mi rtag 'du shes kyis/_/dge slong rnams kyis 'gro ba gnang /_/sdom brtson chos gos gsum 'dzin la/_/khrus kyi ras bsrel rjes su gnang /_/sa bon tshos par dka' ba'i rigs/_/rlangs pas 
[\[57b.5\]]phog pa spyad du rung /_/sgregs pa byung ba dor nas ni/_/gus pas kha nang bkru bar bya/_/sngon las bzlog dka' las grub pa'i/_/med pa gnyis yod ma gtogs so/_/mngon mtshan can dag 'chang ba ni/_/dge 'dun nyid la rjes su gnang /_/bsod snyoms pa yi chos gos la/_/mdud pa bor ba la 
[\[57b.6\]]sogs min/_/thams cad kha dog gcig pa yi/_/gos rnams nye bar longs spyad min/_/me tog 'bras bu shing sogs drung /_/gci sogs kun tu spyad pa spang /_/steng du shing las bya ba ste/_/rdo yi skas ni ma yin no/_/rlung nad can gyis phyag mi bya/_/de la yang ni gzhan gyis min/_/lus 
[\[57b.7\]]gtsang byi dor byas nas ni/_/chos gos legs par bgo bar bya/_/de lus mnyed pa'i bsam pa yis/_/bsku mnye byas la nyes pa med/_/li sha'i me tog la sogs pa/_/rnyed na dben par 'dug nas ni/_/sbyin bdag rnams la phan 'dod cing /_/lta byed tshim rgyas don du bsnam/_/chos 
[\[58a\]]
[\[58a.1\]]gos 'jam phyis byas pa sngon/_/dga' bo la ni mdzes mas byin/_/skur 'debs gyur gsan thub dbang gis/_/dgag pa'i tshul 'di rab tu gsungs/_/brdungs nas 'jam por byas pa yi/_/chos gos bcang bar mi bya ste/_/de ni su zhig 'chang byed pa/_/nges par 'gal 
[\[58a.2\]]tshabs can du 'gyur/_/phag gi yan lag la sogs dang /_/bu ram shing la sogs pa dag_/chom rkun gyis ni bor ba gang /_/skye bos mthong ba blang bar bya/_/rgyab dang mgo bo rked pa yis/_/khur po khur ba'i sdom brtson gang /_/de yi 'gal tshabs sdug bsngal gyis/_/'jig rten 
[\[58a.3\]]pha rol gzir bar byed/_/srin bus la ba rin po che/_/zos pa la ni nim pa dang /_/'dab ma bdun pa la sogs pa'i/_/lo ma'i phye mas bsgo bar gsungs/_/pha ni dbul po ma yin pas/_/bu la nor skal ster ba na/_/tshul khrims ldan pas blangs nas ni/_/dkon mchog gsum gyi 
[\[58a.4\]]dgos pa bya/_/mtshams gzhan gnas pas de yi tshe/_/'dun pa phul na nyes byas te/_/'bul du bcug kyang mi 'chags pas/_/nges par nyes byas las gzhan min/_/la ba la sogs bzang po gang /_/tshogs kyi ci bder thob pa na/_/de ni btsongs te bgo bya yi/_/gshag 
[\[58a.5\]]cing gtub par mi bya'o/_/gnas mal rab tu ma brtags par/_/nyal ba la ni nyes byas 'gyur/_/rta dang khyim bya bar te ke_/glang sogs 'thab tu gzhug mi bya/_/bde gshegs kyis ni gnyis po la/_/pus 'khyud rjes su gnang ba ste/_/lus ni nad kyis nyams pa dang /_/gzhan 
[\[58a.6\]]yang rgan po de la'o/_/gnas nas 'gro bar 'dod pa yis/_/slar yang 'ong bar 'dod pa na/_/gnas mal kun nas sbyangs nas ni/_/legs par gtad de 'gro bar bya/_/gang la gtad pa de yis kyang /_/de ni 'bad pas kun tu bsrung /_/'ongs nas slar yang sbyin bya ste/_/rtags sam go 
[\[58a.7\]]bar byas te'o/_/gang sngon brang 'gro ma mthong nas/_/mnan pas dge slong bzung nas ni/_/gnyis ka yang ni shi bar gyur/_/des na 'di ni go byed yin/_/brtul zhugs can gyis zla phyed der/_/tshes brgyad dang ni bco lnga la/_/gnas mal legs par sbyang bar bya/_/ma sbyangs na ni 
[\[58b\]]
[\[58b.1\]]bslab ni 'thob/_/gnas khang bzang po der bsgrubs pa/_/tshogs la nye bar mkho ba yang /_/sngon du gang 'os thob pas sbyang /_/rgan rims ji bzhin 'dir yod min/_/bya ba yongs su rdzogs pa na/_/snod spyad dang ni yo byad gos/_/sngon du blangs nas khyer bya ste/_/phal pa'i las 
[\[58b.2\]]kyis ma yin no/_/tshogs kyi gnas mal bzang po nyid/_/gang zag dbang la de mi 'dod/_/rgyab rten bcas pa'i stan dag ni/_/gnyis ka la yang rjes su gnang /_/lo gsum khyad par zhugs pa dang /_/lhan cig stan gcig la mi 'dug_/dge slong bsnyen par ma rdzogs dang /_/lhan 
[\[58b.3\]]cig stan la rung ma yin/_/mkhan po dang ni slob ma yang /_/khyim pa'i khyim du rjes su gnang /_/stan cig la 'dug der yang ni/_/gnyis pa cung zad med na'o/_/mal stan la sogs blangs nas ni/_/'jigs bcas gzhan du 'gro ba na/_/sdom brtson gyis der longs spyad nas/_/
[\[58b.4\]]'jigs bral tshe na gtad par bya/_/'di la 'jigs dang bral nas ni/_/sngar bzhin yang dag yongs su spyad/_/rdol dang ral dang zags pa dag_/cho ga bzhin du bcos par bya/_/gang der gding ba la sogs pa/_/spyad mi bzod dag longs spyod phyir/_/de yis sa lcir legs bsres 
[\[58b.5\]]nas/_/rtsig pa'i khung bu la sogs byug_/de ltar legs par sbyar na ni/_/sbyin 'bras bde ba'i don du 'gyur/_/sbyin bdag rnams kyi bsod nams rgyun/_/mi 'chad pa yi rgyu yin no/_/gtsug lag khang gzhan rnyed pa ni/_/spyad de gzhan du gnas pa na/_/de yis gong ngam du na yang /_/thams 
[\[58b.6\]]cad rin tshad gzhal bar bya/_/dge 'dun dag gi gnas mal la/_/shes byed mtshan ma gdab par bya/_/che ge'i sbyin bya'i chos yin zhes/_/mdzes pa'i yi ge rnams kyis so/_/khri steng khri ni brtsegs pa la/_/dge slong 'dug par byed pa de/_/nyes byas dung gi sgra gcan gyis/_/
[\[58b.7\]]bdas te de ni 'og tu ltung /_/dge tshul gang zhig dad pa can/_/slob la rab gus blo ldan gyi/_/tshogs la go byas de dag gi_/gnas mal la ni rung ba nyid/_/ko lpags las gyur mal stan dag_/yul gyi dbus su bkag pa ste/_/khyim pa'i khyim du 'dug tsam rung /_/der yang nyal ba 
[\[59a\]]
[\[59a.1\]]gnang ma yin/_/gtsug lag khang gi sgo drung du/_/dom lpags nyid kyi rjes su gnang /_/ko lpags kyi ni mal stan dag_/mtha' 'khob tu ni nyes pa med/_/ngo mtshar nor bu mu tig sogs/_/rin chen mchog gis mdzes pa la/_/gser dang ba so la sogs 
[\[59a.2\]]rkang /_/mal stan chen po zhes byar brjod/_/gang gi steng du brla 'dug nas/_/rkang brkyang sa la mi reg pa/_/mal stan khyad par 'di 'dra yang /_/de ni mal stan mthon por 'dod/_/'di dag bsnyen gnas sdom par ni/_/khyim pa la yang rab bkag ste/_/de 'dir gnyis ka dge slong la/_/
[\[59a.3\]]ci phyir dgag par mi 'os min/_/khri'u la ni gcig pur 'dug_/gnyis ni shing khri brtan pa'o/_/brtan dang mi brtan shes byas nas/_/shing khri la ni gsum sogs kyang /_/gaN+DI brdungs nas zhus te 'am/_/tshul shing brim pa dag gis kyang /_/rnyed pa 'ged byed de yi tshe/_/
[\[59a.4\]]dge slong kun la go bar bya/_/shi sogs brda ni sprod byed pa'i/_/byi'o la sogs gaN+DI lnga/_/dge slong rnams kyis brdung ba ni/_/'gal med rjes su gnang ba yin/_/lan cig kun tu bteg pa yis/_/gang du rab tu brdung ba ni/_/de ni shi bar mtshon byed pa/_/byi'o nyid brjod dang po'o/_/
[\[59a.5\]]gang du lan gnyis bteg pas brdung /_/de ni gnyis pa las su shes/_/de nyid gcig gi lhag par ni/_/'du ba zhes byar mngon par brjod/_/nges med gnod byung gaN+DI ste/_/de yang 'jig rten go byed yin/_/sgra ldan khar gsil 'khrol ba ni/_/bsam gtan pa rnams go byed nyid/_/
[\[59a.6\]]gtsug lag khang du glo bur gang /_/'jug tshe phyi rol rkang gnyis bkru/_/sprug gam yang na phyi bya ste/_/chu 'am yang na lo mas so/_/nang zhugs nye bar phyin pa na/_/brtul zhugs can thad phyag bya ste/_/des kyang de la ci nus su/_/de dag la ni bya ba yin/_/dka' thub can gyis 
[\[59a.7\]]thog mar ni/_/dge 'dun dag gi nang khrims dri/_/de yis ston pa'i bka' bzhin du/_/yang dag rjes su bsrung bar bya/_/gtsug lag khang du dge slong la/_/gnod byed du ni mi 'gyur ba/_/yon tan 'phel du rung ba gang /_/de 'dra dge slong rnams kyis bca'/_/shes bya ma lus de nyid ni/_/
[\[59b\]]
[\[59b.1\]]mkhyen pa'i de bzhin gshegs pa la/_/brtul zhugs can gyis tshe ldan dang /_/rigs rus mtshan nas smos pa dang /_/yul nas brjod par mi bya'o/_/rgan po dag la gsar bu yis/_/btsun pa zhes ni brjod bya ste/_/gsar bu la ni rgan pos kyang /_/tshe ldan zhes brjod gzhan du min/_/
[\[59b.2\]]rigs pa yis ni ci nus su/_/nyal nyil nges par bsal bar bya/_/tshes brgyad dang ni bcu bzhi la/_/gaN+DI brdung nas gnas pas so/_/bshang gci mchil ma ngar snabs dang /_/khrag kyang nges par 'dor ba na/_/sgra bcas se gol gtogs bya ste/_/bslab pa zad par mi 'dod na'o/_/rtswa 
[\[59b.3\]]sngon dang ni chu dag la/_/dor bar bya ba ma yin no/_/sa gzhi gtsang ma'i phyogs dag dang /_/gang du 'gal mthong der yang min/_/nyin mtshan bder gnas dge slong gis/_/tam po la ni mi bza' ste/_/dge slong rigs pa bstan pa yi/_/nad pa la ni nyes pa med/_/dus min zas la bkag pa yis/_/
[\[59b.4\]]'bras bu gang yang mi bza' ste/_/mkhas pa rnams kyis chos 'dul las/_/gnang ba de spyad mdor bsdus yin/_/dga' bo'i mig sman thur ma sogs/_/thub pas ji skad mdor gsungs bzhin/_/de bzhin dge slong rnams kyis 'dir/_/bza' ba bag yod yongs su spyad/_/gtsug lag khang nang dge 
[\[59b.5\]]slong gis/_/skra dang kha spu breg mi bya/_/nad pas sa gzhi gtsang ma la/_/gos dag bting ste breg par bya/_/skra sogs 'breg par byed pa la/_/rgan rims ji bzhin rjes su gnang /_/nyung ngu tsam zhig bregs na yang /_/gsar bu'i 'phro ni mi gcad do/_/dang por mgo bo yang ni kha/_/de nas sna yi gtsang sbra 
[\[59b.6\]]dang /_/de 'og rkang lag sen mo ste/_/las byed rim pa 'di dag go/_/skra ni chan pas mi breg ste/_/brtul zhugs can la gnang ba ni/_/'ga' zhig gnas su rma nye na/_/chad pas gcad du rung ba yin/_/nad pa la ni go byas nas/_/tshangs spyod rnam par mkhas pa yis/_/kun khyab phyogs su dran 
[\[59b.7\]]ldan pas/_/spu dag bregs sogs rung ba yin/_/zla 'dra 'bras bu'i rnam pa'am/_/de bzhin spu gri'i so lta bur/_/gcad do rtsi yis mi byug cing /_/dag phyir yin te mdzes phyir min/_/dgon pa pa yis sor gnyis las/_/lhag par skra skye gzhag mi bya/_/grong gi mthar gnas brtul ldan 
[\[60a\]]
[\[60a.1\]]gyis/_/sor gnyis yang ni mi rung smad/_/skra rnams bregs zin rjes thogs la/_/dag pa'i don du khrus bya'o/_/sdom brtson rkyen dbang ldan gyur na/_/yan lag lnga rnams bkru bar gnang /_/dge 'dun spyi khang nyer gnas su/_/spu gri la sogs snod bcas bzhag_/skra 
[\[60a.2\]]sogs las la mkhas na ni/_/dben par byed la bkag pa med/_/so shing byas shing kha bkrus nas/_/dam pa'i chos ni bshad bya ste/_/zas dus yang ni de nyid las/_/gzhan du 'os pa ma yin no/_/de yang rnam gsum ring shos ni/_/sor ni bcu gnyis shes par bya/_/brgyad ni chung ngu de yi dbus/_/
[\[60a.3\]]bar ma zhes ni brjod pa yin/_/de dor nas kyang lce bzhar gyis/_/'bad de lce ni bzhar bar bya/_/khar ba zangs dang ra gan lcags/_/lce bzhar rnam pa bzhi yin no/_/grong gi tha mar gnas pa yis/_/de dag nyer bsgrub gzhan yang ni/_/med na legs par brud pa yi/_/shing tshal gyis kyang bzhar rung nyid/_/gal 
[\[60a.4\]]te shing tshal yang med na/_/kha dang lce yi dri ma ni/_/ji ltar sus kyang mi mthong bar/_/lci bar bcas pa'i chu yis sbyang /_/sngon byung dug can sbrul shi bas/_/bram ze'i bur skyes gyur pa zhig_/rab byung de yi lce yi dris/_/rnam sbyang nyes pa mang por gyur/_/dang por sbrang ma so shing gis/_/de yis rtsangs pa 
[\[60a.5\]]shi gyur te/_/de yis ne le de la gang /_/reg pas mtha' dag shi bar gyur/_/de yi dri dor thams cad du/_/'gro ba rnams ni rim gyis shi/_/de yi phyir na bcom ldan 'das/_/thugs rje ldan pas bkag pa yin/_/tshul ldan rnams kyi so shing ni/_/bkrus nas yongs su dor bar bya/_/chu dag med na sa 
[\[60a.6\]]la yang /_/brud de yongs su dor bar bya/_/gci ba dang ni bshang ba dang /_/so shing bca' ba'i bya ba ste/_/rnam pa gsum po dge slong gis/_/sgrib g.yogs can gyi phyogs su bya/_/dge slong dag la rim gcig pa'i/_/mchil lham bcang ba rjes su gnang /_/mchil lham rim pa mang po can/_/ma brtags 
[\[60a.7\]]par ni bcang mi bya/_/sgra can dang ni rin po che/_/bcang du rung ba ma yin no/_/tshul khrims lan pas longs spyod na/_/dge 'dun gyi la nyes pa med/_/lhags rlung can gyi sa phyogs sam/_/kha ba can du nyin mo yang /_/rkang pa'i gnas skabs rig bya ste/_/yu ba can yang rjes su gnang /_/stag dang seng ge 
[\[60b\]]
[\[60b.1\]]dred dang rta/_/glang po'i pags pas bya ba min/_/gzhan dag kyang ni de dag gi_/rgyus pa can ni rnam par spangs/_/gzhan yang sder mo can dang ni/_/mche ba can ni mi bcang ste/_/spyang khyi byi ba la sogs pa'i/_/pags pas byas pa'i mchil lham yang /_/gtsug lag khang du thams cad du/_/shing gi 
[\[60b.2\]]mchil lham mi rung ste/_/bshang dang gci ba'i khang pa dang /_/khyim pa'i grong du nyes pa med/_/'od ma mun dza la sogs pa'i/_/rang bzhin mchil lham mi bcang ste/_/rlung khrag nad kyis nyen pa yi/_/nad pa dag la bkag pa med/_/gru la sogs pa med pa na/_/glang po rta dang ma he dang /_/khyu mchog 
[\[60b.3\]]rnga mo'i mjug ma la/_/'jus te brgal ba bkag pa med/_/tshogs la 'bad pas thams cad du/_/bsrung la rag las yod ma yin/_/rgyal po'i skal ba len pa dang /_/zhing gi bya ba rjes su gnang /_/gtsug lag khang du char sogs kyi/_/'jigs pa rnams kyis g.yengs pa na/_/tshogs 
[\[60b.4\]]kyi nags kyi lo tog ni/_/'bad pas rang gis bskur bar bya/_/dge 'dun gyi ni ba lang sogs/_/rab tu 'bad pas bsrung bar bya/_/khur khol shod cig sod cig ces/_/tshig ngan brjod par mi bya'o/_/dge tshul la sogs gang rnams kyis/_/lam du lam brgyags khur ba na/_/des don bskur bya chu la 
[\[60b.5\]]yang /_/dge slong gis ni bsgral bar bya/_/dub pa na yang bskur bya zhing /_/de ni de yis byin brlabs te/_/sems kyi 'gyod pa med pas ni/_/the gu la sogs sbyor bas so/_/rkun pos skrag gyur bor ba 'am/_/brgal dka'i chu la bo ba yang /_/de ni legs blangs phyung nas kyang /_/yongs su spyod la 
[\[60b.6\]]bkag pa med/_/bza' bca' rdzas sogs kyis gang ba'i/_/shing rta bgrod dka'i lam du'am/_/kha lo ba yi stan ma gtogs/_/'bad pas dge slong rnams kyis bkri/_/'di la 'di gnyis 'os yin pas/_/gru ni 'dam sogs zin pa na/_/gru gdong rnam par spangs nas ni/_/de ni dge slong rnams 
[\[60b.7\]]kyis bkri/_/rlung sogs med na tshon gyi las/_/bya'o nyi ma ngan par min/_/'di la gang du skyon 'byung ba'i/_/sa yi cha dang nyin gung min/_/mkhas pas phal cher rdul dang char/_/rlung dang nyi dus ma yin te/_/'di ni khang [{]bzangs so[,]bzang ngo [}]gas don du/_/sa gzhi gtsang mar sbyangs nas so/_/dge 
[\[61a\]]
[\[61a.1\]]slong rnams ni rtsod pa dag_/byed na chos ldan lhan cig pas/_/ma gus byed pa de dag ni/_/yal bar mi dor dgag par bya/_/khrims 'chal gyur la khrims ldan gyis/_/tshul mnyam rnams la mang thos kyis/_/thos pa mnyam la spobs pa can/_/spobs pa mnyam 
[\[61a.2\]]la'ang phul byung gis/_/gang zhig dang por rtsod pa la/_/de yi tshig gis 'dul gyur ba/_/spobs can nyid la'ang gang lhag pa/_/de dag tshig gis 'dul ba yin/_/rab tu nges dang ldan pa yis/_/rtsod pa yongs su rtog pa pos/_/phan tshun nang khon bstan pa la/_/yang na btang snyoms gzhag par 
[\[61a.3\]]bya/_/mi mthun pas kyang lam dag tu/_/phrad na rgan la phyag bya ste/_/des kyang nad med gyur cig brjod/_/gzhan du gnyis ka ltung bar 'gyur/_/bsro khang khrus kyi gnas su ni/_/sgo drung srung ma gzhag bya ste/_/dge slong rnams ni khrus srod dag_/byed pa ma dad bzlog phyir ro/_/sdom brtson sna tshogs 
[\[61a.4\]]tshon dag gis/_/ri me'i cho ga de dag gnang /_/gang dag la ni rung ba'i las/_/sems can rnam pa ma gtogs so/_/gnas khang dag tu rus pa yi/_/tshogs bcas dur khrod chen po bri/_/gcer bu so gtsigs 'jigs pa yi/_/thod pa'i phreng ba rgyan du bya/_/rtsa ba'i sgor ni dbang phyug che/_/kun 
[\[61a.5\]]dga' bzhin gyi dkyil 'khor can/_/lag na be con thogs pa yi/_/gnod sbyin rnams kyi bdag po bri/_/cho 'phrul pad ma rgyas pa la/_/bder gshegs gzugs mang bzhugs pa dang /_/srid pa'i 'khor lo'i bar dag tu/_/sgo khang logs ni gnyis su'o/_/gtsang khang sgor ni gnod sbyin 
[\[61a.6\]]rnams/_/me tog phreng thogs bri bya ste/_/gus pas bza' ba mang thogs ni/_/khyams kyi bkod pa'i sa dag tu'o/_/mdzod sgor gnod sbyin chen po ni/_/lag na rin chen myu gu'i ming /_/kha nas rin chen mang skyug pa/_/gser gyi bum pa bzang thogs pa'o/_/kun nas 'du ba'i khang par ni/_/brtul 
[\[61a.7\]]ldan rgan zhing rgan pa rnams/_/chos dang mngon brjod 'chad pa ni/_/byed pa mngon par gnas pa bri/_/bsro khang me yi gnas su ni/_/mdo sde lha yi pho nya bri/_/nad pa'i sngas su sku gzugs ni/_/nad pa'i dbugs 'byin bla mar mdzad/_/sna tshogs rgyan gyis phun tshogs pa'i/_/klu mo chu yi 
[\[61b\]]
[\[61b.1\]]gnas su ste/_/dur khrod 'jigs su rung ba ni/_/bshang dang gci ba'i khang par ro/_/khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]dag gi ngos dag tu/_/thub pa'i sngon gyi skyes rabs bri/_/dper na thams cad sgrol dang ni/_/zla 'od bzod par smra la sogs/_/gtsug lag khang sogs 'dul ba la/_/'jig rten mngon 
[\[61b.2\]]par dad 'phel phyir/_/ri mo'i cho ga brjod pa 'di/_/thugs rje can gyis legs par gnang /_/khyams dang khang [{]bzangs [,]bzang [}]rnams su ni/_/ched du me sbar mi bya ste/_/dgos pas de dang mthun par ni/_/mdag ma yongs su spyad par bzhed/_/sa gzhi yongs su sbyangs pa la/_/me ni btang bar 
[\[61b.3\]]mi bya ste/_/de phyir de ni sbar ba'i phyir/_/me ling rjes su gnang ba yin/_/sangs rgyas tshogs kyi rgan po dang /_/rgyal po dang ni slob dpon dang /_/de bzhin mkhan po nyid kyi bka'/_/gang zhig rigs na 'da' mi bya/_/dang po 'dir ni gang cung zad/_/gnang med bkag pa med pa ni/_/
[\[61b.4\]]gang zhig rung dang mthun pa na/_/de ni the tshom med par spyad/_/mi rung ba dang nye lta na/_/sdom brtson rnams kyis de mi spyad/_/'di ni chos 'dul mdor bsdus pa/_/bstan bcos sgron ma lta bur gzhag_/'dul ba 'am ni mdo sde yi/_/gleng gzhi gsal por ma dran na/_/bzhugs pa'i 
[\[61b.5\]]grong khyer rnams kyi ming /_/gang 'dod don ni smos par bya/_/mnyan yod gnas bcas tsam pa ka_/bA rA Na sI yangs pa can/_/rgyal po'i khab ces grags pa ste/_/'di dag grong khyer chen por shes/_/khyim bdag mgon med zas sbyin te/_/sa bdag mi dbang gsal rgyal brjod/_/bud 
[\[61b.6\]]med rnams la sa ga ste/_/brjed ngas dag ni smos par bya/_/grong khyer bA rA Na sI ste/_/sa bdag yang ni tshangs sbyin no/_/skyes pa'am de bzhin bud med la/_/bsnyen gnas ster na bsgrub bya ba/_/dang po'i sbyor ba gang yin la/_/klog pa dran pa mi gsal na/_/bslab pa'i gzhi ni mthar thug pa/_/
[\[61b.7\]]'di ni thub pas gsungs pa yin/_/thog mar bA rA Na sIr ni/_/chos kyi 'khor lo bskor ba na/_/lnga sde bzang la phan bzhed pas/_/mdo sde bcom ldan 'das kyis gsungs/_/gnyis pa'ang gang gi 'khor kun ni/_/gzugs la sogs pa phung por bcas/_/bdag 'dzin zhags pa gcig 
[\[62a\]]
[\[62a.1\](]bu [,]pu [)]yis/_/'ching ba mtha' dag rnam grol mdzad/_/lnga sde bzang rnams nyid kyi phyir/_/gos dag legs bgos 'tsham dang mdzes/_/zlum po la sogs tha snyad kyis/_/bslab gzhi yang dag gsungs pa yin/_/ji ltar gnas ni gtsang ma'i lha/_/bag yod gos 
[\[62a.2\]]yongs gyon byed pa/_/de bzhin thub pas dge slong la/_/zlum por rab tu gsungs pa yin/_/thub pas rim pa 'di yis ni/_/bzang sbyin la sogs mtho ris dang /_/thar pa 'dod pa de dag la/_/bslab pa'i gzhi yang bca' bar mdzad/_/so ga'i dus na mtha' med pa'i/_/rgya mtshor 'jug 
[\[62a.3\]]pa ji bzhin du/_/gang gis dang por 'dul ba la/_/tshul khrims dri ma sbyong mdzad pa/_/drug sde bdag nyid chen po rnams/_/ngo mtshar bcas pa'i spobs pa can/_/mtshon pa med kyang grub pa ste/_/bzang po de dag drang srong che/_/dga' bo nye dga' 'gro mgyogs dang /_/'char ka 
[\[62a.4\]]'dun pa nam so ste/_/mkhas pa dpa' po drug sder grags/_/sangs rgyas bstan pa bsrung bar mdzad/_/slar yang de dag shin tu bzang /_/rab bsngags mun pa 'joms byed pa/_/mkhas pa rnams kyis gang ltar yang /_/'dul ba las ni 'da' mi byed/_/sangs rgyas sras su gang 
[\[62a.5\]]gyur pa/_/de dag yon tan bsam mi khyab/_/gang 'dir nye bar bstan pa ni/_/rtogs par byed pa'i bshes gnyen mchog_/yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]bsags sdig mi shes pas/_/'gro ba gnyid kyis log pa 'di/_/gang gis dam chos bstan pa yi/_/lag pas bskul te sad mdzad pa'o/_/yun 
[\[62a.6\]{]rings [,]ring [}]goms pas bskyed pa yi/_/thugs rjes thugs ni brlan pa ste/_/bdag nyid chen po'i mdzad pa ni/_/rnam bcu gdon mi za ba yin/_/gzhan dag byang chub lung bstan dang /_/byang chub sems bskyed bstan pa dang /_/sku tshe lnga cha 'dor ba dang /_/mchog gnyis rab tu byung ba dang /_/
[\[62a.7\]]sangs rgyas gdul bya 'dul ba dang /_/mtshams gcad pa ni bsgrub pa dang /_/cho 'phrul rab tu bstan pa dang /_/lha yi gnas su gshegs pa dang /_/las kyi dbye ba bshad pa dang /_/yab yum bden pa mthong ba la/_/ji srid ma bkod de srid par/_/mya ngan 'da' bar mi mdzad do/_/gang gis thub pa'i 
[\[62b\]]
[\[62b.1\]]bstan pa 'di/_/'jig rten snang mdzad rin po che/_/rgya mtsho sgrol ba'i gru lta bu/_/sdud po rnams la phyag 'tshal lo/_/gang zhig mang du thos pa yis/_/skye ba'i lcags sgrog rnam grol ba/_/thos 'dzin mdo sde'i sde snod kyi/_/sdud po 'phags pa kun dga' la'ang /_/gang gis ngan song sbrul gdug ni/_/
[\[62b.2\]]'dul ba'i legs bshad dri med pa'i/_/sngags kyis gso bar mdzad pa dang /_/'phags pa nye ba 'khor la yang /_/gang gis kun nas ma mo yi/_/sde snod rab tu bstan pa yis/_/sgron ma bzhin du gsal mdzad pa/_/blo ldan 'phags pa 'od srungs la'ang /_/rgyal po'i khab tu sde snod gsum/_/
[\[62b.3\]]'dis legs thams cad bsdus pa yin/_/lhar bcas mi yi 'jig rten du/_/snang ba cher mdzad 'di yin no/_/gang du thos ldan 'di dag dang /_/dgra bcom sdig pa ring spangs pa/_/lnga brgya po ni dam pa'i chos/_/yang dag bsdus pa'i dbang por grags/_/brgya byin thog mar byas pa yi/_/
[\[62b.4\]]lha rnams dam chos gus pa yis/_/gang du 'dus pa chen pos ni/_/nam mkha'i ngos ni rgyan du byas/_/rgyal po'i khab kyi mthar yang ni/_/sa gzhi lha rnams me tog ni/_/lag nas kun tu 'thor ba yis/_/gang du de ni yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]mdzes/_/gang tshe dam chos rin chen mchog_/nub nyams 
[\[62b.5\]]'dam du bying ba bzhin/_/lha dang mi rnams dogs 'jigs pa/_/bsdus pas slar yang yid sos mdzad/_/gang phyir thub pas dam pa'i chos 'di ni/_/skye dgu gso byed skyob pa'i dpung bral ba/_/sdug bsngal ma lus nyer zhi'i don bsgrub phyir/_/brtson pas 'khor bar yun [{]rings [,]ring [}]spyod 
[\[62b.6\]]pa na/_/'od 'bar dbu ni mgul gos bcas pa dang /_/rang gi sha dang rus dum khrag rgyun btang /_/sras kyang zla tshes cha 'dra yid 'ong ba'i/_/btsun mo mchog sbyin dpag med gang don phyir/_/bder gshegs dpal brnyes zhi lam chos bstan 'di/_/ji srid ma nub bar du rnam kun nas/_/snyom 
[\[62b.7\]]las don med ltung ba rnam spangs te/_/zhi ba'i don du yang dag brtson par bya/_/bder [(]bshegs [,]gshegs [)]bka' ni mi rnams tshig las khyad 'phags phyir/_/chos gang cung zad spros kyang snyan dngags mchog gyur te/_/bsam pa'i thabs kyis bsgrub 'bras kha na ma tho med/_/'di de las gzhan be sha kas kyang 
[\[63a\]]
[\[63a.1\]]byas pa la/_/gang zhig ma tshang lhag gam yang na gzhan du byas/_/sngar bshad rnams las [{]go rims [,]go rim [}]'chol bar bshad kyang rung /_/de kun rang bzhin zhi ba'i bsam pas bzod par mdzod/_/nyam nga'i lam du long ba rdeg 'chos min nam ci/_/dge slong chos 'dul thig 
[\[63a.2\]]ler byas mdzes me tog phreng rgyud legs brgyus las/_/gang thob bsod nams dpal des 'khor ba'i lus can rnams dang lhan cig bdag_/'dod pa'i 'dam las rnam par brgal nas dad sogs yon tan kun gyis brgyan/_/mya ngan 'das par tshe rabs gzhan du'ang bsnyen par 
[\[63a.3\]]rdzogs pa byed par shog_/gang dag tshul khrims dri med gnas 'dod cing /_/mya ngan 'das pa'i grong khyer 'jug 'dod pa/_/rtag tu tshig ler byas pa'i don 'di nyid/_/klog dang 'don dang sems pa nyid du shog_/ji ltar 'jig rten rga nad 'chi ba'i gdung bas gdungs/_/ji srid dus 
[\[63a.4\]]na 'dod chags me yis dga' mgur bsreg_/de ltar de srid sems rgyun dri ma ngan 'khrud pa'i/_/bder gshegs bka' ni gang gA'i chu bzhin gnas gyur cig_/'phags pa gzhi thams cad yod par smra ba'i 'dul ba tshig le'ur byas pa/_me tog gi phreng rgyud ces bya ba/_/'dul ba 'dzin pa chen po 
[\[63a.5\]]'phags pa dge 'dun 'bangs kyi slob ma/_dpal 'phags pa sa ga'i lhas mdzad pa rdzogs so//_//bal po'i paN+Di ta dza yA ka ra dang /_bod kyi lo tsA ba dge slong pradz+nyA kI rtis bsgyur cing zhus te gtan la phab pa'o//_//rgya gar shar phyogs sa dan ga ra'i paN+Di ta ba na rad+na ma hA s+thi bI ra la 
[\[63a.6\]]gtugs te/_sgra'i don la mkhas pa rong ston shes bya kun rig gis/_'gyur cung zad bcos te gtan la phab pa'o//[
